Modul: Birinchi renderingiz (Your first render)

Module description
Materials, a light and a camera.
Materiallar, yorugʻlik manbai va kamera.

Obyektlarga material berish — Giving objects a material · article · 419 words

Summary
Base colour, roughness and metallic: describe what a surface is made of.
Asosiy rang, gʻadir-budurlik va metallik: sirt nimadan yasalganini tavsiflang.
Lesson text

What is a material?

A material describes what a surface is made of — painted wood, shiny metal, rough clay — by telling the render engine how light bounces off it. In this lesson you will create materials with Blender's main shader and preview them in the viewport.

Seeing materials in the viewport

The viewport normally shows plain grey Solid shading. To see materials, switch to Material Preview: press Z in the viewport and choose it from the pie menu, or click the third of the round shading buttons at the top right of the viewport.

Creating a material

  1. Select an object.
  2. In the Properties Editor, open the Material tab (the red sphere icon).
  3. Click New. Blender creates a material using the Principled BSDF shader.

Rename the material straight away (for example Clay_Red) so you can find it later.

The three settings that matter most

Setting What it controls Try
Base Color The colour of the surface Any colour you like
Metallic Whether the surface behaves like metal 0 for plastic, wood or skin; 1 for metal
Roughness How blurred reflections are 0.1 for polished, 0.8 for matte

Real materials are usually either fully metallic or not metallic at all, so keep Metallic at 0 or 1 most of the time and use Roughness to vary the look.

[!TIP] Make small test materials before your main scene: a red plastic ball (Metallic 0, Roughness 0.4), a gold coin (Metallic 1, Roughness 0.2) and a clay pot (Metallic 0, Roughness 0.9). Seeing them side by side teaches the settings faster than reading.

Glowing surfaces

Emission makes a surface give off light, like a screen or a lamp. In the Principled BSDF, set an Emission Color and raise the Emission Strength above 0.

Several materials on one object

One object can have several materials, such as a mug with a white outside and a dark inside:

  1. Add a second material slot with the + button in the Material tab and give it a new material.
  2. In Edit Mode, select the faces that should use it.
  3. Click Assign.

Try it

Give the snowman from the earlier lesson materials: white matte snow, a shiny black material for the eyes and an orange material for the nose.

Common mistakes

  • Looking for materials in Solid shading, where they are not shown.
  • Setting Metallic to 0.5 for everyday objects, which looks unrealistic.
  • Leaving materials with default names such as Material.003.

Key terms

  • Material — the description of a surface used by the render engine.
  • Principled BSDF — Blender's main all-purpose shader.
  • Roughness — how sharp or blurred reflections are.
  • Material Preview — a viewport shading mode that shows materials.

Material nima?

Material sirt nimadan yasalganini — boʻyalgan yogʻoch, yaltiroq metall, gʻadir-budur loy — render dvigateliga yorugʻlik undan qanday qaytishini aytish orqali tavsiflaydi. Bu darsda Blenderning asosiy sheyderi bilan materiallar yaratasiz va ularni koʻrinish maydonida koʻrib chiqasiz.

Materiallarni koʻrinish maydonida koʻrish

Koʻrinish maydoni odatda oddiy kulrang Solid (bir rangli) rejimda boʻladi. Materiallarni koʻrish uchun Material Preview rejimiga oʻting: koʻrinish maydonida Z ni bosib, uni doiraviy menyudan tanlang yoki koʻrinish maydonining oʻng yuqori qismidagi dumaloq rejim tugmalarining uchinchisini bosing.

Material yaratish

  1. Obyektni tanlang.
  2. Properties Editor da Material yorligʻini (qizil shar belgisi) oching.
  3. New ni bosing. Blender Principled BSDF sheyderidan foydalanadigan material yaratadi.

Keyinroq topish oson boʻlishi uchun materialni darhol qayta nomlang (masalan, Clay_Red).

Eng muhim uchta sozlama

Sozlama Nimani boshqaradi Sinab koʻring
Base Color (asosiy rang) Sirtning rangi Oʻzingiz yoqtirgan istalgan rang
Metallic (metallik) Sirt metalldek oʻzini tutishi Plastmassa, yogʻoch yoki teri uchun 0; metall uchun 1
Roughness (gʻadir-budurlik) Aksning qanchalik xira ekani Sayqallangan uchun 0.1, xira uchun 0.8

Haqiqiy materiallar odatda yo butunlay metall, yo umuman metall emas, shuning uchun koʻp hollarda Metallic ni 0 yoki 1 da saqlang, koʻrinishni esa Roughness bilan oʻzgartiring.

[!TIP] Asosiy sahnadan oldin kichik sinov materiallarini yarating: qizil plastmassa shar (Metallic 0, Roughness 0.4), oltin tanga (Metallic 1, Roughness 0.2) va loy koʻza (Metallic 0, Roughness 0.9). Ularni yonma-yon koʻrish sozlamalarni oʻqishdan koʻra tezroq oʻrgatadi.

Yorugʻlik taratuvchi sirtlar

Emission (nurlanish) sirtni ekran yoki chiroq kabi yorugʻlik taratadigan qiladi. Principled BSDF da Emission Color ni belgilang va Emission Strength ni 0 dan yuqori qiling.

Bitta obyektda bir nechta material

Bitta obyektda bir nechta material boʻlishi mumkin, masalan, tashqarisi oq, ichi toʻq rangli krujka:

  1. Material yorligʻidagi + tugmasi bilan ikkinchi material slotini qoʻshing va unga yangi material bering.
  2. Edit Mode’da shu materialdan foydalanishi kerak boʻlgan yoqlarni tanlang.
  3. Assign ni bosing.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Oldingi darsdagi qorboboga materiallar bering: oq xira qor, koʻzlar uchun yaltiroq qora material va burun uchun toʻq sariq material.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Materiallar koʻrsatilmaydigan Solid rejimida ularni qidirish.
  • Kundalik buyumlar uchun Metallic ni 0.5 qilib qoʻyish — natija gʻayritabiiy koʻrinadi.
  • Materiallarni Material.003 kabi standart nomlar bilan qoldirish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Material — render dvigateli foydalanadigan sirt tavsifi.
  • Principled BSDF — Blenderning asosiy universal sheyderi.
  • Roughness (gʻadir-budurlik) — akslar qanchalik aniq yoki xira ekani.
  • Material Preview — materiallarni koʻrsatadigan koʻrinish rejimi.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Yoritish va birinchi tasviringizni render qilish — Lighting and rendering your first image · article · 459 words

Summary
Lights, the camera, EEVEE versus Cycles and saving a PNG image.
Yorugʻlik manbalari, kamera, EEVEE va Cycles farqi hamda PNG tasvirni saqlash.
Lesson text

From scene to picture

Rendering turns your scene into a finished image as seen through the camera. In this lesson you will light a scene, frame it with the camera, choose a render engine and save the image.

Adding lights

Press Shift + A → Light and choose a type:

Light Behaves like Good for
Point A bare bulb shining in all directions Small lamps, candles
Sun Distant sunlight with parallel rays Outdoor scenes
Spot A cone of light Stage lights, torches
Area A glowing panel Soft studio light

Select a light and adjust its Power (in watts) and colour in the Properties Editor's light tab. A classic setup is three-point lighting: a strong key light at one side, a weaker fill light on the other side and a rim light behind the subject to outline it.

Framing with the camera

  • Press Numpad 0 (or use the camera icon in the navigation gizmo) to look through the camera.
  • To move the camera to your current view, press Ctrl + Alt + Numpad 0.
  • Fine-tune with G and R while looking through the camera, or adjust its position in the sidebar (N).

Choosing a render engine

Open the Render Properties tab (the back of a camera icon):

  • EEVEE — real-time and fast. Great for previews, stylised images and animation on modest computers.
  • Cycles — traces light physically. Realistic shadows and reflections, but slower. If you have a supported GPU, choose it under Preferences → System.

In Cycles, the Samples value controls quality: more samples mean less grain but longer renders. Keep Denoise on to remove most of the remaining grain.

Rendering and saving

  1. Press F12 to render. A new window shows the image as it is calculated.
  2. In that window, choose Image → Save As (Shift + Alt + S).
  3. Choose PNG as the format and save.

[!WARNING] A render is not saved automatically. Closing the render window without saving loses the image (but not your scene).

Saving your scene

Save the .blend file with Ctrl + S, and save often. If Blender closes unexpectedly, File → Recover → Auto Save can bring back a recent automatic copy.

Try it

Light your snowman with three-point lighting, frame it with the camera, render it once in EEVEE and once in Cycles, and save both images as PNG files. Compare the shadows and render times.

Common mistakes

  • Rendering without a camera in the scene, or with the camera pointing away from the subject.
  • Lights so weak that the render looks black, or so strong that everything is white.
  • Closing the render window without saving the image.

Key terms

  • Rendering — calculating the final image from the scene.
  • Key, fill and rim light — the three lights of a classic studio setup.
  • EEVEE / Cycles — Blender's real-time and physically accurate render engines.
  • Samples — the number of light calculations per pixel in Cycles.

Sahnadan tasvirga

Rendering sahnangizni kamera orqali koʻrinadigan tayyor tasvirga aylantiradi. Bu darsda sahnani yoritasiz, uni kamera bilan kadrga olasiz, render dvigatelini tanlaysiz va tasvirni saqlaysiz.

Yorugʻlik manbalarini qoʻshish

Shift + A → Light ni bosing va turini tanlang:

Yorugʻlik Nimaga oʻxshaydi Nima uchun qulay
Point (nuqtali) Har tomonga nur sochadigan ochiq lampochka Kichik chiroqlar, shamlar
Sun (quyosh) Nurlari parallel boʻlgan uzoqdagi quyosh Ochiq havodagi sahnalar
Spot (projektor) Konussimon yorugʻlik Sahna chiroqlari, fonarlar
Area (maydonli) Nur taratuvchi panel Yumshoq studiya yorugʻligi

Yorugʻlik manbaini tanlab, Properties Editor’ning yorugʻlik yorligʻida uning Power (quvvat, vattlarda) va rangini sozlang. Klassik usul — uch nuqtali yoritish: bir tomonda kuchli asosiy yorugʻlik (key light), boshqa tomonda kuchsizroq toʻldiruvchi yorugʻlik (fill light) va obyektni chiziq bilan ajratib koʻrsatish uchun uning ortida kontur yorugʻligi (rim light).

Kamera bilan kadrga olish

  • Kamera orqali qarash uchun Numpad 0 ni bosing (yoki navigatsiya gizmosidagi kamera belgisidan foydalaning).
  • Kamerani joriy koʻrinishingizga koʻchirish uchun Ctrl + Alt + Numpad 0 ni bosing.
  • Kamera orqali qarab turib G va R bilan aniq sozlang yoki uning joylashuvini yon panelda (N) oʻzgartiring.

Render dvigatelini tanlash

Render Properties yorligʻini (kamera orqa tomoni belgisi) oching:

  • EEVEE — real vaqtda ishlaydigan va tez. Oldindan koʻrish, stilizatsiya qilingan tasvirlar va oddiy kompyuterlarda animatsiya uchun juda yaxshi.
  • Cycles — yorugʻlikni fizik qonunlarga koʻra hisoblaydi. Soyalar va akslar realistik, lekin sekinroq. Qoʻllab-quvvatlanadigan videokartangiz boʻlsa, uni Preferences → System boʻlimida tanlang.

Cycles’da Samples (namunalar) qiymati sifatni boshqaradi: namunalar qancha koʻp boʻlsa, donadorlik shuncha kam, render esa shuncha uzoq boʻladi. Qolgan donadorlikning katta qismini yoʻqotish uchun Denoise ni yoqilgan holda qoldiring.

Render qilish va saqlash

  1. Render qilish uchun F12 ni bosing. Yangi oynada tasvir hisoblanayotgani koʻrinadi.
  2. Oʻsha oynada Image → Save As (Shift + Alt + S) ni tanlang.
  3. Format sifatida PNG ni tanlab, saqlang.

[!WARNING] Render avtomatik saqlanmaydi. Render oynasini saqlamasdan yopsangiz, tasvir yoʻqoladi (sahnangiz esa saqlanib qoladi).

Sahnangizni saqlash

.blend faylini Ctrl + S bilan saqlang va tez-tez saqlab turing. Agar Blender kutilmaganda yopilsa, File → Recover → Auto Save yaqinda avtomatik saqlangan nusxani qaytarishi mumkin.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Qorbobongizni uch nuqtali yoritish bilan yoriting, kamera bilan kadrga oling, bir marta EEVEE’da va bir marta Cycles’da render qiling hamda ikkala tasvirni PNG fayl sifatida saqlang. Soyalar va render vaqtini solishtiring.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Sahnada kamera boʻlmasa yoki kamera obyektga qaramasa ham render qilish.
  • Yorugʻlik juda kuchsiz boʻlib, render qop-qora chiqishi yoki juda kuchli boʻlib, hamma narsa oppoq chiqishi.
  • Tasvirni saqlamasdan render oynasini yopish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Rendering — sahnadan yakuniy tasvirni hisoblash.
  • Asosiy, toʻldiruvchi va kontur yorugʻligi — klassik studiya yoritishining uchta manbai.
  • EEVEE / Cycles — Blenderning real vaqtdagi va fizik jihatdan aniq render dvigatellari.
  • Samples (namunalar) — Cycles’da har bir piksel uchun yorugʻlik hisob-kitoblari soni.

Birinchi render: bilimni tekshirish — First render: check · quiz

Summary
Five questions on materials, lights and rendering.
Materiallar, yorugʻlik va render boʻyicha beshta savol.
Test: “Birinchi renderingiz” moduli boʻyicha test
Q1

Your material changes do not show in the viewport. What is the most likely reason?

Material oʻzgarishlaringiz koʻrinish maydonida koʻrinmayapti. Eng ehtimoliy sababi nima?

Options
  • ✓ The viewport is in Solid shading, not Material Preview
  • The material is too dark
  • The camera is off
  • ✓ Koʻrinish maydoni Material Preview emas, Solid rejimida
  • Material juda toʻq
  • Kamera oʻchirilgan
Explanation

Solid shading does not show materials; switch to Material Preview.

Solid rejimi materiallarni koʻrsatmaydi; Material Preview ga oʻting.

Q2

Which setting makes a surface look polished instead of matte?

Qaysi sozlama sirtni xira emas, sayqallangan koʻrsatadi?

Options
  • ✓ A low Roughness value
  • A high Roughness value
  • A Metallic value of 0.5
  • ✓ Past Roughness qiymati
  • Yuqori Roughness qiymati
  • Metallic qiymati 0.5
Explanation

Low Roughness gives sharp reflections; high Roughness looks matte.

Past Roughness aniq akslar beradi; yuqori Roughness xira koʻrinadi.

Q3

Which shortcut renders the image?

Qaysi tezkor tugma tasvirni render qiladi?

Options
  • ✓ F12
  • Ctrl + S — Ctrl + S saves the .blend file.
  • Tab — Tab switches between Object and Edit Mode.
  • ✓ F12
  • Ctrl + S — Ctrl + S .blend faylini saqlaydi.
  • Tab — Tab Object va Edit Mode orasida almashtiradi.
Explanation

F12 renders the current frame through the active camera.

F12 joriy kadrni faol kamera orqali render qiladi.

Q4

How do you move the camera to match your current view?

Kamerani joriy koʻrinishingizga qanday moslashtirasiz?

Options
  • ✓ Ctrl + Alt + Numpad 0
  • Numpad 0 — Numpad 0 looks through the camera without moving it.
  • Shift + A — Shift + A opens the Add menu.
  • ✓ Ctrl + Alt + Numpad 0
  • Numpad 0 — Numpad 0 kamerani siljitmasdan u orqali qaraydi.
  • Shift + A — Shift + A Add menyusini ochadi.
Explanation

Ctrl + Alt + Numpad 0 aligns the active camera to the view.

Ctrl + Alt + Numpad 0 faol kamerani koʻrinishga moslaydi.

Q5

In three-point lighting, which light outlines the subject from behind?

Uch nuqtali yoritishda qaysi yorugʻlik obyektni orqadan chiziq bilan ajratib koʻrsatadi?

Options
  • ✓ Rim light
  • Key light
  • Fill light
  • ✓ Kontur yorugʻligi
  • Asosiy yorugʻlik
  • Toʻldiruvchi yorugʻlik
Explanation

The rim light separates the subject from the background.

Kontur yorugʻligi obyektni fondan ajratadi.

Modul: Yakuniy loyiha va imtihon (Final project and assessment)

Module description
Build, light and render a scene of your own.
Oʻz sahnangizni yarating, yoriting va render qiling.

Loyiha: natyurmort sahnasi — Project: a still-life scene · project · 166 words

Summary
Model, texture, light and render a still-life scene from simple shapes.
Oddiy shakllardan natyurmort sahnasini modellashtiring, material bering, yoriting va render qiling.
Lesson text

Your project

Create a still-life scene — a table with a few everyday objects, such as a cup, a bowl of fruit and a book — using only the tools from this course. Light it, frame it and render a finished image.

Requirements

  1. At least five objects built from primitives and edited in Edit Mode (extrude, loop cut, scale).
  2. Tidy objects: sensible origins, applied scale and meaningful names in the Outliner.
  3. At least four materials with different Base Color, Metallic and Roughness values, including one metal and one matte material.
  4. Three-point lighting (or a clearly described alternative) and a camera that frames the scene well.
  5. A final render in Cycles or EEVEE, saved as PNG at 1920 × 1080.
  6. A short note (three or four sentences) on your lighting and material choices.

How to submit

Upload the PNG render and, if you like, the .blend file. Paste your note in the text box.

[!NOTE] A reviewer checks your render against the rubric and leaves feedback. You can improve the scene and resubmit.

Loyihangiz

Faqat ushbu kursdagi vositalardan foydalanib, natyurmort sahnasini — ustida bir nechta kundalik buyum, masalan, piyola, bir idish meva va kitob turgan stolni yarating. Uni yoriting, kadrga oling va tayyor tasvirni render qiling.

Talablar

  1. Primitivlardan qurilgan va Edit Mode’da tahrirlangan (extrude, loop cut, masshtab) kamida beshta obyekt.
  2. Tartibli obyektlar: oqilona origin nuqtalari, qoʻllangan masshtab va Outliner’da mazmunli nomlar.
  3. Base Color, Metallic va Roughness qiymatlari har xil boʻlgan, jumladan, bitta metall va bitta xira material bilan kamida toʻrtta material.
  4. Uch nuqtali yoritish (yoki aniq tasvirlangan muqobil usul) va sahnani yaxshi kadrga oladigan kamera.
  5. Cycles yoki EEVEE da yakuniy render, 1920 × 1080 oʻlchamdagi PNG sifatida saqlangan.
  6. Yoritish va material tanlovlaringiz haqida qisqa izoh (uch-toʻrt gap).

Qanday topshiriladi

PNG renderni va, xohlasangiz, .blend faylini yuklang. Izohingizni matn maydoniga joylang.

[!NOTE] Tekshiruvchi renderingizni baholash mezonlari asosida koʻrib chiqadi va fikr-mulohaza qoldiradi. Sahnani yaxshilab, qayta topshirishingiz mumkin.

Assignment brief

Model, texture, light and render a still-life scene of five or more objects, with a short note on your choices.

Besh va undan ortiq obyektdan iborat natyurmort sahnasini modellashtiring, material bering, yoriting va render qiling hamda tanlovlaringiz haqida qisqa izoh yozing.

Rubric
Criterion Points
Five or more objects shaped in Edit Mode 20
Tidy scene: origins, applied scale, clear names 15
Four or more convincing materials (including metal and matte) 20
Lighting and camera framing 25
Final PNG render at 1920 × 1080 with a note on choices 20
Mezon Ball
Edit Mode’da shakl berilgan besh va undan ortiq obyekt 20
Tartibli sahna: origin nuqtalari, qoʻllangan masshtab, aniq nomlar 15
Toʻrt va undan ortiq ishonarli material (metall va xira material bilan) 20
Yoritish va kamera bilan kadrga olish 25
1920 × 1080 oʻlchamdagi yakuniy PNG render va tanlovlar haqida izoh 20

Which axis points up in Blender?

  • X

How many edges does a cube have?

A cube has 8 vertices, 12 edges and 6 faces.

Which key switches between Object Mode and Edit Mode?

  • ✓ Tab

  • N

What should you do before beveling an object you scaled in Object Mode?

Options
  • ✓ Apply the scale (Ctrl + A → Scale)
  • Delete the object and start again
  • Switch to Cycles
  • ✓ Masshtabni qoʻllash (Ctrl + A → Scale)
  • Obyektni oʻchirib, qaytadan boshlash

You type R X 90. What happens?

Siz R X 90 deb yozasiz. Nima sodir boʻladi?

  • ✓ The object rotates 90° around the X axis
  • The object moves 90 metres along X
  • The object scales to 90%

Explanation

R rotates, X limits it to the X axis, 90 is the angle in degrees.

Which material settings suit a gold coin?

  • Metallic 0, yuqori Roughness
  • Metallic 0.5, Roughness 0.5
Explanation

Metal: Metallic 1, with a low Roughness for a polished look.

Metall: Metallic 1, sayqallangan koʻrinish uchun past Roughness.

Q7

Which render engine is real-time and fast?

  • ✓ EEVEE
  • Cycles
  • ✓ EEVEE
  • Cycles
Explanation

EEVEE renders in real time; Cycles is physically accurate but slower.

EEVEE real vaqtda render qiladi; Cycles fizik jihatdan aniq, ammo sekinroq.

Q8

True or false: a render is saved automatically when it finishes.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: render tugagach, u avtomatik saqlanadi.

Options
  • True
  • ✓ False
  • Toʻgʻri
  • ✓ Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

False — save it with Image → Save As before closing the render window.

Notoʻgʻri — render oynasini yopishdan oldin uni Image → Save As bilan saqlang.

Kompyuter asoslari: noldan boshlab

Computer Fundamentals from Zero

Subtitle
Understand the machine in front of you — no prior knowledge needed
Oldingizdagi mashinani tushunib oling — oldindan bilim shart emas
Description

This course assumes nothing. If you have ever wondered what actually happens inside a computer, why files have strange endings like .pdf, or what people mean by “hardware” and “software”, you are in the right place.

You will build a clear mental model of how a computer takes in information, works on it, stores it and shows you the result. Every term is explained the first time it appears.

Bu kurs sizdan hech narsa bilishni talab qilmaydi. Agar kompyuter ichida aslida nima sodir boʻlishi, fayllar nega .pdf kabi gʻalati qoʻshimchalar bilan tugashi yoki odamlar “apparat taʼminoti” va “dasturiy taʼminot” deganda nimani nazarda tutishi haqida oʻylab koʻrgan boʻlsangiz, toʻgʻri joydasiz.

Kompyuter maʼlumotni qanday qabul qilishi, uni qayta ishlashi, saqlashi va natijani sizga qanday koʻrsatishi haqida aniq tasavvur hosil qilasiz. Har bir atama birinchi marta uchraganda tushuntiriladi.

Outcomes
  • Explain what a computer is using the input → processing → storage → output model
  • Tell hardware and software apart and name the main parts of a computer
  • Understand bits and bytes and read storage sizes like GB and TB
  • Navigate files, folders and paths with confidence
  • Kompyuter nima ekanini kiritish → qayta ishlash → saqlash → chiqarish modeli orqali tushuntira olasiz
  • Apparat va dasturiy taʼminotni farqlaysiz hamda kompyuterning asosiy qismlarini ayta olasiz
  • Bit va baytlarni tushunasiz, GB va TB kabi xotira hajmlarini oʻqiy olasiz
  • Fayllar, papkalar va fayl yoʻllarida bemalol harakatlanasiz
Prerequisites
  • No prior knowledge — just curiosity and access to any computer
  • Oldindan bilim shart emas — qiziqish va istalgan kompyuterdan foydalanish imkoniyati kifoya

Modul: Kompyuter aslida nima (What a computer really is)

Module description
The core idea behind every computer, from phones to servers.
Telefondan tortib serverlargacha — har bir kompyuter ortidagi asosiy gʻoya.

Kompyuter nima? — What is a computer? · article · 300 words

Summary
A computer is a machine that follows instructions to turn input into useful output.
Kompyuter — kiritilgan maʼlumotni koʻrsatmalarga amal qilib foydali natijaga aylantiradigan mashina.
Lesson text

A machine that follows instructions

A computer is a machine that takes in information, follows a list of instructions to work with that information, and gives you a result. That’s it. Your laptop, your phone, a smartwatch, a cash register and the servers that run websites are all computers.

What makes computers special is not that they are clever — they are not. They are extremely fast and perfectly obedient. A modern computer can follow billions of simple instructions every second, exactly as written.

[!NOTE] The list of instructions a computer follows is called a program. Writing programs is called programming.

The four jobs of every computer

Every computer, no matter its size, does the same four jobs:

  1. Input — receiving information. Typing on a keyboard, tapping a screen, speaking into a microphone or taking a photo are all input.
  2. Processing — working on that information by following instructions. Adding numbers, applying a photo filter or checking a password are processing.
  3. Storage — keeping information so it can be used later. Your photos, documents and installed apps are stored.
  4. Output — showing or sending the result. Text on a screen, sound from a speaker, a printed page or a message sent to a friend are output.

An everyday example

When you take a photo with your phone:

  • the camera captures light — input
  • the phone improves colours and sharpness — processing
  • the photo is saved to the gallery — storage
  • the photo appears on your screen — output

[!TIP] Whenever a new technology feels confusing, ask: what is the input, what processing happens, what is stored, and what is the output? This one question explains a surprising amount of computing.

Key terms

  • Computer — a machine that follows instructions to process information.
  • Program — a list of instructions for a computer.
  • Input / Output — information going into / coming out of a computer.

Koʻrsatmalarga amal qiladigan mashina

Kompyuter — maʼlumotni qabul qiladigan, shu maʼlumot bilan ishlash uchun koʻrsatmalar roʻyxatiga amal qiladigan va sizga natija beradigan mashina. Bor-yoʻgʻi shu. Noutbukingiz, telefoningiz, aqlli soat, kassa apparati va veb-saytlarni ishlatadigan serverlar — bularning barchasi kompyuter.

Kompyuterlarni maxsus qiladigan narsa ularning aqlli ekani emas — ular aqlli emas. Ular nihoyatda tez va soʻzsiz itoatkor. Zamonaviy kompyuter har soniyada milliardlab oddiy koʻrsatmalarni aynan yozilganidek bajara oladi.

[!NOTE] Kompyuter amal qiladigan koʻrsatmalar roʻyxati dastur deb ataladi. Dastur yozish esa dasturlash deyiladi.

Har bir kompyuterning toʻrt vazifasi

Hajmidan qatʼi nazar, har qanday kompyuter bir xil toʻrt vazifani bajaradi:

  1. Kiritish — maʼlumotni qabul qilish. Klaviaturada yozish, ekranga teginish, mikrofonga gapirish yoki suratga olish — bularning hammasi kiritish.
  2. Qayta ishlash — koʻrsatmalarga amal qilib, shu maʼlumot ustida ishlash. Sonlarni qoʻshish, fotosuratga filtr qoʻllash yoki parolni tekshirish — bular qayta ishlash.
  3. Saqlash — maʼlumotni keyinroq foydalanish uchun saqlab qoʻyish. Fotosuratlaringiz, hujjatlaringiz va oʻrnatilgan ilovalaringiz saqlanadi.
  4. Chiqarish — natijani koʻrsatish yoki yuborish. Ekrandagi matn, karnaydan chiqqan ovoz, chop etilgan sahifa yoki doʻstingizga yuborilgan xabar — bular chiqarish.

Kundalik misol

Telefoningizda suratga olganingizda:

  • kamera yorugʻlikni qayd etadi — kiritish
  • telefon ranglar va tiniqlikni yaxshilaydi — qayta ishlash
  • surat galereyaga saqlanadi — saqlash
  • surat ekraningizda paydo boʻladi — chiqarish

[!TIP] Biror yangi texnologiya chalkash tuyulsa, oʻzingizdan soʻrang: nima kiritiladi, qanday qayta ishlanadi, nima saqlanadi va natijada nima chiqariladi? Shu birgina savol hisoblash texnikasidagi juda koʻp narsani tushuntirib beradi.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Kompyuter — koʻrsatmalarga amal qilib maʼlumotni qayta ishlaydigan mashina.
  • Dastur — kompyuter uchun koʻrsatmalar roʻyxati.
  • Kiritish / Chiqarish — kompyuterga kirayotgan / undan chiqayotgan maʼlumot.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Apparat va dasturiy taʼminot — Hardware and software · article · 334 words

Summary
The physical parts you can touch, and the instructions that bring them to life.
Qoʻl bilan ushlash mumkin boʻlgan jismoniy qismlar va ularga jon kirituvchi koʻrsatmalar.
Lesson text

Two halves of every computer

Every computer is made of two kinds of things:

  • Hardware — the physical parts you could touch: the screen, keyboard, chips and cables.
  • Software — the instructions (programs) that tell the hardware what to do: the operating system, a web browser, a game.

Hardware without software is a box that does nothing. Software without hardware has nowhere to run. You need both.

The main hardware parts

Part What it does A simple comparison
CPU (processor) Follows instructions — the “thinking” part A chef following a recipe
RAM (memory) Holds what you are working on right now The chef’s worktable
Storage (SSD or hard drive) Keeps files and apps even when power is off The pantry
GPU (graphics processor) Draws images, video and 3D quickly A specialist pastry chef
Motherboard Connects every part so they can communicate The kitchen itself
Power supply / battery Provides electricity The gas line

[!NOTE] RAM is temporary. When the power goes off, whatever is only in RAM disappears. That is why you save your work — saving copies it from RAM to storage.

Kinds of software

  • Operating system (OS) — the main program that manages the hardware and runs everything else. Examples: Windows, macOS, Linux, Android, iOS.
  • Applications (apps) — programs that help you do a task: a browser, a photo editor, a messenger.
  • Drivers — small programs that let the operating system talk to a specific piece of hardware, such as a printer.

Bits and bytes

Inside the computer, all information — text, photos, music — is stored as numbers made of only two digits, 0 and 1. One such digit is a bit.

  • 8 bits = 1 byte (enough to store one simple letter like “A”)
  • about 1,000 bytes = 1 kilobyte (KB)
  • about 1,000 KB = 1 megabyte (MB) — a typical photo is a few MB
  • about 1,000 MB = 1 gigabyte (GB) — a film is a few GB
  • about 1,000 GB = 1 terabyte (TB)

[!TIP] When buying a computer, storage (e.g. 512 GB SSD) tells you how much you can keep, while RAM (e.g. 16 GB) tells you how much you can work on at the same time.

Har bir kompyuterning ikki yarmi

Har qanday kompyuter ikki xil narsadan iborat:

  • Apparat taʼminoti (inglizcha hardware) — qoʻl bilan ushlash mumkin boʻlgan jismoniy qismlar: ekran, klaviatura, mikrosxemalar va kabellar.
  • Dasturiy taʼminot (inglizcha software) — apparatga nima qilishni aytadigan koʻrsatmalar, yaʼni dasturlar: operatsion tizim, veb-brauzer, oʻyin.

Dasturiy taʼminotsiz apparat hech narsa qilmaydigan qutidan boshqa narsa emas. Apparatsiz esa dasturiy taʼminot ishlaydigan joy yoʻq. Ikkalasi ham kerak.

Asosiy apparat qismlari

Qism Vazifasi Oddiy taqqoslash
CPU (markaziy protsessor) Koʻrsatmalarni bajaradi — “fikrlovchi” qism Retsept boʻyicha ovqat tayyorlayotgan oshpaz
RAM (operativ xotira) Ayni hozir ishlayotgan narsangizni ushlab turadi Oshpazning ish stoli
Doimiy xotira (SSD yoki qattiq disk) Elektr oʻchganda ham fayllar va ilovalarni saqlaydi Oziq-ovqat omborxonasi
GPU (grafik protsessor) Tasvir, video va 3D grafikani tez chizadi Shirinliklarga ixtisoslashgan oshpaz
Ona plata (tizim platasi) Barcha qismlarni bir-biri bilan bogʻlaydi Oshxonaning oʻzi
Quvvat bloki / batareya Elektr energiyasi bilan taʼminlaydi Gaz quvuri

[!NOTE] RAM vaqtinchalik xotira. Elektr oʻchganda faqat RAMda turgan maʼlumot yoʻqoladi. Ishingizni aynan shuning uchun saqlaysiz — saqlash uni RAMdan doimiy xotiraga koʻchiradi.

Dasturiy taʼminot turlari

  • Operatsion tizim (OT) — apparatni boshqaradigan va qolgan barcha dasturlarni ishga tushiradigan asosiy dastur. Misollar: Windows, macOS, Linux, Android, iOS.
  • Ilovalar — biror vazifani bajarishga yordam beradigan dasturlar: brauzer, fotomuharrir, messenjer.
  • Drayverlar — operatsion tizimga muayyan qurilma, masalan, printer bilan “gaplashish” imkonini beradigan kichik dasturlar.

Bit va baytlar

Kompyuter ichida barcha maʼlumot — matn, fotosurat, musiqa — faqat ikki raqam, 0 va 1 dan tashkil topgan sonlar koʻrinishida saqlanadi. Bunday bitta raqam bit deyiladi.

  • 8 bit = 1 bayt (“A” kabi bitta oddiy harfni saqlashga yetadi)
  • taxminan 1 000 bayt = 1 kilobayt (KB)
  • taxminan 1 000 KB = 1 megabayt (MB) — oddiy fotosurat bir necha MB boʻladi
  • taxminan 1 000 MB = 1 gigabayt (GB) — film bir necha GB boʻladi
  • taxminan 1 000 GB = 1 terabayt (TB)

[!TIP] Kompyuter sotib olayotganda doimiy xotira (masalan, 512 GB SSD) qancha narsa saqlay olishingizni, RAM (masalan, 16 GB) esa bir vaqtning oʻzida qancha narsa bilan ishlay olishingizni bildiradi.

Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Bilimingizni tekshiring — Check your understanding · quiz

Summary
Five quick questions on computers, hardware and software.
Kompyuterlar, apparat va dasturiy taʼminot boʻyicha beshta qisqa savol.
Test: 1-modul boʻyicha test
Q1

You type a message and press Send. Which of the four jobs is typing the message?

Siz xabar yozib, Yuborish tugmasini bosasiz. Xabarni yozish toʻrt vazifaning qaysi biri?

Options
  • ✓ Input — Correct — you are giving information to the computer.
  • Processing — Processing is the computer working on information, not you entering it.
  • Storage — Storage is keeping information for later.
  • Output — Output is information coming *out* of the computer.
  • ✓ Kiritish — Toʻgʻri — siz kompyuterga maʼlumot beryapsiz.
  • Qayta ishlash — Qayta ishlash — bu siz maʼlumot kiritishingiz emas, kompyuterning maʼlumot ustida ishlashi.
  • Saqlash — Saqlash — maʼlumotni keyinroq foydalanish uchun saqlab qoʻyish.
  • Chiqarish — Chiqarish — kompyuterdan *chiqayotgan* maʼlumot.
Explanation

Typing gives information to the computer, so it is input.

Yozish orqali siz kompyuterga maʼlumot berasiz, shuning uchun bu kiritish.

Q2

Which part temporarily holds what you are working on right now, and is cleared when the power goes off?

Qaysi qism ayni hozir ishlayotgan narsangizni vaqtincha ushlab turadi va elektr oʻchganda tozalanadi?

Options
  • Storage (SSD) — An SSD keeps data even without power.
  • ✓ RAM — Yes — that is why unsaved work can be lost.
  • GPU — The GPU draws graphics; it is not general working memory.
  • Power supply — The power supply provides electricity, not memory.
  • Doimiy xotira (SSD) — SSD elektr boʻlmaganda ham maʼlumotni saqlaydi.
  • ✓ RAM — Ha — saqlanmagan ish shuning uchun yoʻqolishi mumkin.
  • GPU — GPU grafikani chizadi; u umumiy ishchi xotira emas.
  • Quvvat bloki — Quvvat bloki xotira emas, elektr energiyasini beradi.
Explanation

RAM is fast, temporary working memory. Storage keeps files permanently.

RAM — tezkor, vaqtinchalik ishchi xotira. Doimiy xotira esa fayllarni uzoq muddat saqlaydi.

Q3

Select all the items that are software.

Dasturiy taʼminotga tegishli barcha variantlarni belgilang.

Options
  • ✓ Windows
  • ✓ A web browser
  • A keyboard — A keyboard is a physical input device — hardware.
  • A monitor — A monitor is a physical output device — hardware.
  • ✓ Windows
  • ✓ Veb-brauzer
  • Klaviatura — Klaviatura — jismoniy kiritish qurilmasi, yaʼni apparat.
  • Monitor — Monitor — jismoniy chiqarish qurilmasi, yaʼni apparat.
Explanation

Software is instructions. Windows and a web browser are programs; a keyboard and a monitor are physical hardware.

Dasturiy taʼminot — bu koʻrsatmalar. Windows va veb-brauzer dasturlardir; klaviatura va monitor esa jismoniy apparat qismlari.

Q4

True or false: 1 byte is made of 8 bits.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: 1 bayt 8 bitdan iborat.

Options
  • ✓ True
  • False — It is true — 8 bits make one byte.
  • ✓ Toʻgʻri
  • Notoʻgʻri — Bu toʻgʻri — 8 bit bitta baytni tashkil qiladi.
Explanation

A byte is a group of 8 bits.

Bayt — 8 bitdan iborat guruh.

Q5

Which is the largest amount?

Qaysi biri eng katta hajm?

Options
  • 500 MB
  • 2 GB
  • ✓ 1 TB — Right — a terabyte is about 1,000 gigabytes.
  • 900 KB
  • 500 MB
  • 2 GB
  • ✓ 1 TB — Toʻgʻri — bir terabayt taxminan 1 000 gigabaytga teng.
  • 900 KB
Explanation

KB < MB < GB < TB. Each step is roughly 1,000 times bigger.

KB < MB < GB < TB. Har bir keyingi birlik taxminan 1 000 marta katta.

Modul: Kompyuter ichida (Inside the machine)

Module description
The parts that do the work: processor, memory, storage and ports.
Ishni bajaradigan qismlar: protsessor, operativ xotira, doimiy xotira va portlar.

Protsessor va xotira: kompyuter qanday “fikrlaydi” — Processor and memory: how a computer thinks · article · 603 words

Summary
What the processor, RAM and storage really do — and why “more” is not always “faster”.
Protsessor, operativ xotira va doimiy xotira aslida nima qiladi — va nega “koʻproq” har doim ham “tezroq” degani emas.
Lesson text

Three parts that work together

In this lesson you will learn what the processor, memory and storage actually do, so you can understand any computer's specifications. Every time you open an app, these three parts work as a team:

  1. The app's files are read from storage.
  2. They are loaded into memory (RAM), where they can be reached quickly.
  3. The processor (CPU) follows the app's instructions, step by step.

The processor (CPU)

The CPU repeats one simple cycle billions of times per second: fetch an instruction, work out what it means, carry it out.

  • Clock speed, measured in gigahertz (GHz), is how many cycles happen each second. 3 GHz means about three billion cycles per second.
  • Cores are independent workers inside one processor. A 6-core CPU can work on six streams of instructions at the same time.

More cores help when a job can be split into parts — exporting video, rendering 3D, compiling code or running many apps at once. Many everyday tasks run mainly on one core, so a CPU with fewer but faster cores can feel just as quick for web browsing and documents.

[!NOTE] The GPU (graphics processor) has hundreds or thousands of small cores designed for doing the same calculation on many pixels at once. That is why it is so good at graphics, video and AI work.

Memory (RAM)

RAM is the computer's workspace. Everything you have open right now — browser tabs, documents, the operating system itself — lives in RAM. It is very fast, but it is cleared when the power goes off.

When RAM is full, the operating system moves some data to storage to make room (a page file or swap). Storage is much slower than RAM, so the computer suddenly feels sluggish.

Kind of use Workable RAM Comfortable RAM
Web, documents, studying 8 GB 16 GB
Programming, design 16 GB 32 GB
Video editing, 3D, games 16 GB 32 GB or more

Storage

Storage keeps your files and apps when the computer is off. There are three common kinds:

  • HDD (hard disk drive) — spinning magnetic disks. Cheap for large capacity, but slow and sensitive to knocks.
  • SATA SSD — no moving parts and several times faster than an HDD.
  • NVMe SSD — connects directly to the motherboard (usually in an M.2 slot) and is the fastest common option.

An SSD makes the whole computer feel faster: it starts up in seconds and apps open quickly. For most people, an SSD matters more than a slightly faster processor.

A worked example

You open a 50 MB photo in an editor. The photo is read from the SSD, loaded into RAM, the CPU runs the editor's instructions, the GPU helps apply a filter, and the result appears on the screen. If RAM is almost full, the computer has to keep swapping data to storage, and every click feels slow.

Try it

Find the processor, RAM and storage of the computer you are using:

  • Windows: Settings → System → About, and Task Manager → Performance (press Ctrl + Shift + Esc).
  • macOS: Apple menu → About This Mac.

Write down the number of cores, the amount of RAM and the type and size of storage.

Common mistakes

  • Judging a processor by GHz alone. Cores and the processor's generation matter as much.
  • Buying a lot of storage but too little RAM, so the computer slows down whenever several apps are open.
  • Mixing up RAM and storage. When a phone is advertised with “128 GB”, that is storage, not RAM.

Key terms

  • CPU / core — the processor and one of its independent workers.
  • Clock speed (GHz) — cycles per second, in billions.
  • RAM — fast, temporary working memory.
  • SSD / HDD / NVMe — kinds of permanent storage, from fastest (NVMe) to slowest (HDD).

Birgalikda ishlaydigan uchta qism

Bu darsda protsessor, operativ xotira va doimiy xotira aslida nima qilishini bilib olasiz — shunda istalgan kompyuterning texnik tavsifini tushuna olasiz. Har safar ilovani ochganingizda bu uch qism jamoa boʻlib ishlaydi:

  1. Ilova fayllari doimiy xotiradan oʻqiladi.
  2. Ular tez murojaat qilish mumkin boʻlgan operativ xotira (RAM) ga yuklanadi.
  3. Protsessor (CPU) ilovaning koʻrsatmalarini qadamma-qadam bajaradi.

Protsessor (CPU)

Protsessor har soniyada milliardlab marta bitta oddiy siklni takrorlaydi: koʻrsatmani oladi, uning maʼnosini aniqlaydi va uni bajaradi.

  • Takt chastotasi gigagerts (GHz) bilan oʻlchanadi va har soniyada nechta sikl bajarilishini bildiradi. 3 GHz — soniyasiga taxminan uch milliard sikl degani.
  • Yadrolar — bitta protsessor ichidagi mustaqil “ishchilar”. 6 yadroli protsessor bir vaqtning oʻzida oltita koʻrsatmalar oqimi ustida ishlay oladi.

Koʻp yadro ishni qismlarga boʻlish mumkin boʻlganda yordam beradi: videoni eksport qilish, 3D render qilish, kodni kompilyatsiya qilish yoki bir vaqtda koʻp ilova bilan ishlash. Koʻplab kundalik vazifalar asosan bitta yadroda bajariladi, shuning uchun yadrolari kamroq, lekin tezroq protsessor internetda ishlash va hujjatlar uchun xuddi shunday tez tuyulishi mumkin.

[!NOTE] GPU (grafik protsessor) bir xil hisob-kitobni koʻplab piksellar uchun bir vaqtda bajarishga moʻljallangan yuzlab yoki minglab kichik yadrolarga ega. Shu sababli u grafika, video va sunʼiy intellekt bilan ishlashda juda kuchli.

Operativ xotira (RAM)

RAM — kompyuterning ish stoli. Hozir ochiq turgan hamma narsa — brauzer yorliqlari, hujjatlar, hatto operatsion tizimning oʻzi — RAMda joylashadi. U juda tez, ammo elektr oʻchganda tozalanadi.

RAM toʻlib qolganda operatsion tizim joy boʻshatish uchun maʼlumotlarning bir qismini doimiy xotiraga koʻchiradi (page file yoki swap). Doimiy xotira RAMdan ancha sekin, shuning uchun kompyuter birdan sekinlashib qoladi.

Foydalanish turi Yetarli RAM Qulay RAM
Internet, hujjatlar, oʻqish 8 GB 16 GB
Dasturlash, dizayn 16 GB 32 GB
Video montaj, 3D, oʻyinlar 16 GB 32 GB va undan koʻp

Doimiy xotira

Doimiy xotira kompyuter oʻchiq boʻlganda ham fayllaringiz va ilovalaringizni saqlaydi. Uning uchta keng tarqalgan turi bor:

  • HDD (qattiq disk) — aylanuvchi magnit disklar. Katta hajm uchun arzon, lekin sekin va zarbalarga sezgir.
  • SATA SSD — harakatlanuvchi qismlari yoʻq va HDD dan bir necha barobar tez.
  • NVMe SSD — bevosita ona plataga ulanadi (odatda M.2 uyasiga) va eng tez keng tarqalgan variant hisoblanadi.

SSD butun kompyuterni tezroq his qildiradi: u bir necha soniyada ishga tushadi, ilovalar tez ochiladi. Koʻpchilik uchun SSD biroz tezroq protsessordan muhimroq.

Ishlangan misol

Siz 50 MB hajmli fotosuratni muharrirda ochasiz. Surat SSD dan oʻqiladi, RAM ga yuklanadi, CPU muharrirning koʻrsatmalarini bajaradi, GPU filtr qoʻllashda yordam beradi va natija ekranda paydo boʻladi. Agar RAM deyarli toʻla boʻlsa, kompyuter maʼlumotlarni doimiy xotiraga qayta-qayta koʻchirishga majbur boʻladi va har bir bosish sekin bajariladi.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Foydalanayotgan kompyuteringizning protsessori, RAMi va doimiy xotirasini toping:

  • Windows: Settings → System → About hamda Task Manager → Performance (Ctrl + Shift + Esc ni bosing).
  • macOS: Apple menyusi → About This Mac.

Yadrolar sonini, RAM hajmini hamda doimiy xotiraning turi va hajmini yozib oling.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Protsessorni faqat GHz boʻyicha baholash. Yadrolar soni va protsessor avlodi ham shunchalik muhim.
  • Koʻp doimiy xotira, lekin juda kam RAM sotib olish — natijada bir nechta ilova ochilganda kompyuter sekinlashadi.
  • RAM va doimiy xotirani chalkashtirish. Telefon “128 GB” deb reklama qilinganda, bu RAM emas, doimiy xotira.

Asosiy atamalar

  • CPU / yadro — protsessor va uning mustaqil “ishchilaridan” biri.
  • Takt chastotasi (GHz) — soniyadagi sikllar soni, milliardlarda.
  • RAM — tezkor, vaqtinchalik ishchi xotira.
  • SSD / HDD / NVMe — doimiy xotira turlari, eng tezidan (NVMe) eng sekiniga (HDD) qadar.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Portlar, tashqi qurilmalar va texnik tavsifni oʻqish — Ports, peripherals and reading a spec sheet · article · 493 words

Summary
Connect devices with the right ports, and read a computer specification line by line.
Qurilmalarni toʻgʻri portlarga ulang va kompyuter texnik tavsifini qatorma-qator oʻqing.
Lesson text

Peripherals: everything you plug in

A peripheral is a device connected to a computer from outside: a keyboard, mouse, monitor, printer, headphones or an external drive. By the end of this lesson you will recognise the common ports and be able to read a computer's specification sheet with confidence.

Common ports

Port What it is used for
USB-A The classic rectangular USB: mice, keyboards, flash drives
USB-C Small reversible connector for data, charging and often video
HDMI Video and sound to a monitor, TV or projector
Ethernet (RJ-45) Wired network connection — more stable than Wi-Fi
3.5 mm audio Wired headphones and microphones
SD card slot Memory cards from cameras and drones

[!WARNING] Every USB-C port has the same shape, but not the same abilities. One port may charge and show video, another may only transfer data slowly. Check the specification or the small symbols next to the port (for example, the lightning bolt for Thunderbolt) before you buy cables and adapters.

Wireless connections

  • Wi-Fi connects to the internet without cables. Newer standards (Wi-Fi 6, 6E and 7) are faster and cope better with many devices in one place.
  • Bluetooth connects nearby accessories such as wireless headphones, keyboards and mice.

Reading a specification sheet

Here is a typical laptop specification, explained line by line:

Specification What it tells you
8-core processor, up to 4.5 GHz Good for multitasking and heavier work; “up to” is the short burst speed
16 GB RAM Comfortable for studying, office work and programming
512 GB NVMe SSD Fast storage; about 476 GB will be shown as usable
14-inch, 1920×1200 display Screen size measured diagonally, and the number of pixels
Integrated graphics Fine for everyday work; limited for 3D and games
2× USB-C, 1× USB-A, HDMI Plenty of connections without adapters
Wi-Fi 6, Bluetooth 5.3 Modern wireless standards
Battery up to 10 hours Measured in light use; expect less in real life

Why does 512 GB show as about 476 GB? Manufacturers count 1 GB as 1 000 000 000 bytes, while operating systems often count in units of 1 024. The operating system and recovery files also take some space.

Matching a computer to a need

Person Focus on
Student writing and browsing SSD, 8–16 GB RAM, a light laptop with good battery life
Video editor Strong multi-core CPU, 32 GB RAM, large NVMe SSD, a dedicated GPU
Office worker Reliable SSD, 16 GB RAM, good keyboard, enough ports for a monitor

Try it

Open the specification pages of two laptops sold in your area. For a student on a budget, decide which one you would choose, and write two sentences explaining why using the lines of the specification.

Common mistakes

  • Trusting “up to” numbers for battery life and speed as everyday values.
  • Assuming every USB-C cable or port supports charging and video.
  • Choosing a computer for its large storage while it has too little RAM.

Key terms

  • Peripheral — a device connected to the computer from outside.
  • Port — a socket for connecting a cable.
  • USB-C — a reversible connector whose abilities vary by device.
  • Specification (spec sheet) — the list of a device's parts and features.

Tashqi qurilmalar: ulanadigan hamma narsa

Tashqi qurilma (peripheral) — kompyuterga tashqaridan ulanadigan qurilma: klaviatura, sichqoncha, monitor, printer, quloqchin yoki tashqi disk. Dars oxirida keng tarqalgan portlarni taniysiz va kompyuterning texnik tavsifini ishonch bilan oʻqiy olasiz.

Keng tarqalgan portlar

Port Nima uchun ishlatiladi
USB-A Klassik toʻrtburchak USB: sichqoncha, klaviatura, fleshka
USB-C Maʼlumot, quvvatlash va koʻpincha video uchun kichik, ikki tomonlama ulagich
HDMI Monitor, televizor yoki proyektorga tasvir va ovoz uzatish
Ethernet (RJ-45) Simli tarmoq ulanishi — Wi-Fi ga qaraganda barqarorroq
3.5 mm audio Simli quloqchin va mikrofonlar
SD karta uyasi Kamera va dronlardagi xotira kartalari

[!WARNING] Barcha USB-C portlar shakli bir xil, ammo imkoniyatlari har xil. Bir port quvvatlay oladi va tasvir chiqaradi, boshqasi esa faqat sekin maʼlumot uzatishi mumkin. Kabel va adapterlar sotib olishdan oldin texnik tavsifni yoki port yonidagi kichik belgilarni (masalan, Thunderbolt uchun chaqmoq belgisini) tekshiring.

Simsiz ulanishlar

  • Wi-Fi internetga simsiz ulanadi. Yangi standartlar (Wi-Fi 6, 6E va 7) tezroq va bir joydagi koʻplab qurilmalar bilan yaxshiroq ishlaydi.
  • Bluetooth simsiz quloqchin, klaviatura va sichqoncha kabi yaqin atrofdagi qurilmalarni ulaydi.

Texnik tavsifni oʻqish

Quyida odatiy noutbuk texnik tavsifi qatorma-qator tushuntirilgan:

Tavsif Nimani bildiradi
8 yadroli protsessor, 4.5 GHz gacha Bir vaqtda koʻp ish va ogʻirroq vazifalar uchun yaxshi; “gacha” — qisqa muddatli eng yuqori tezlik
16 GB RAM Oʻqish, ofis ishlari va dasturlash uchun qulay
512 GB NVMe SSD Tez doimiy xotira; foydalanish uchun taxminan 476 GB koʻrsatiladi
14 dyuymli, 1920×1200 ekran Diagonal boʻyicha oʻlchangan ekran hajmi va piksellar soni
Integratsiyalashgan grafika Kundalik ishlar uchun yetarli; 3D va oʻyinlar uchun cheklangan
2× USB-C, 1× USB-A, HDMI Adaptersiz yetarlicha ulanish imkoniyati
Wi-Fi 6, Bluetooth 5.3 Zamonaviy simsiz standartlar
Batareya 10 soatgacha Yengil foydalanishda oʻlchangan; amalda kamroq boʻladi

Nega 512 GB taxminan 476 GB boʻlib koʻrinadi? Ishlab chiqaruvchilar 1 GB ni 1 000 000 000 bayt deb hisoblaydi, operatsion tizimlar esa koʻpincha 1 024 lik birliklarda hisoblaydi. Operatsion tizim va tiklash fayllari ham biroz joy egallaydi.

Kompyuterni ehtiyojga moslash

Kim uchun Nimaga eʼtibor berish kerak
Matn yozadigan va internetdan foydalanadigan talaba SSD, 8–16 GB RAM, batareyasi yaxshi yengil noutbuk
Video montajchi Kuchli koʻp yadroli protsessor, 32 GB RAM, katta NVMe SSD, alohida videokarta
Ofis xodimi Ishonchli SSD, 16 GB RAM, qulay klaviatura, monitor uchun yetarli portlar

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Hududingizda sotiladigan ikkita noutbukning texnik tavsif sahifalarini oching. Byudjeti cheklangan talaba uchun qaysi birini tanlashingizni hal qiling va texnik tavsif qatorlariga tayanib, sababini ikki gap bilan yozing.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Batareya va tezlik boʻyicha “gacha” raqamlarini kundalik qiymat deb ishonish.
  • Har qanday USB-C kabel yoki port quvvatlash va tasvir uzatishni qoʻllab-quvvatlaydi deb oʻylash.
  • RAMi juda kam boʻlsa-da, doimiy xotirasi katta boʻlgani uchun kompyuterni tanlash.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Tashqi qurilma — kompyuterga tashqaridan ulanadigan qurilma.
  • Port — kabel ulash uchun uya.
  • USB-C — imkoniyatlari qurilmaga qarab farq qiladigan ikki tomonlama ulagich.
  • Texnik tavsif (specification) — qurilmaning qismlari va imkoniyatlari roʻyxati.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Kompyuter ichida: bilimni tekshirish — Inside the machine: check · quiz

Summary
Five questions on processors, memory, storage and ports.
Protsessor, xotira, doimiy xotira va portlar boʻyicha beshta savol.
Test: “Kompyuter ichida” moduli boʻyicha test
Q1

What does more RAM mainly help with?

Koʻproq RAM asosan nimaga yordam beradi?

Options
  • ✓ Working on more things at once without slowing down
  • Storing more files permanently — Permanent storage is the job of the SSD or HDD.
  • A faster internet connection — Internet speed depends on your connection, not RAM.
  • ✓ Bir vaqtda koʻp ish bilan sekinlashmasdan ishlashga
  • Fayllarni uzoq muddat koʻproq saqlashga — Uzoq muddat saqlash — SSD yoki HDD vazifasi.
  • Internetning tezroq ishlashiga — Internet tezligi RAMga emas, ulanishingizga bogʻliq.
Explanation

RAM is working space: more of it lets you keep more apps and files open without the computer slowing down.

RAM — ishchi maydon: u koʻproq boʻlsa, kompyuter sekinlashmasdan koʻproq ilova va fayllarni ochiq tutish mumkin.

Q2

Which type of storage is usually the fastest?

Odatda doimiy xotiraning qaysi turi eng tez?

Options
  • HDD — HDDs spin magnetic disks and are the slowest.
  • SATA SSD — Faster than an HDD, but slower than NVMe.
  • ✓ NVMe SSD
  • HDD — HDD magnit disklarni aylantiradi va eng sekini hisoblanadi.
  • SATA SSD — HDD dan tez, lekin NVMe dan sekin.
  • ✓ NVMe SSD
Explanation

NVMe SSDs connect directly to the motherboard and are the fastest common storage.

NVMe SSD bevosita ona plataga ulanadi va eng tez keng tarqalgan doimiy xotira hisoblanadi.

Q3

True or false: a processor with more cores is always faster for every task.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: yadrolari koʻproq protsessor har qanday vazifada har doim tezroq ishlaydi.

Options
  • True — Many tasks cannot use extra cores.
  • ✓ False
  • Toʻgʻri — Koʻplab vazifalar qoʻshimcha yadrolardan foydalana olmaydi.
  • ✓ Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

False. More cores help when work can be split up; many everyday tasks depend mostly on one fast core.

Notoʻgʻri. Koʻp yadro ishni boʻlish mumkin boʻlganda yordam beradi; koʻplab kundalik vazifalar asosan bitta tez yadroga bogʻliq.

Q4

A new laptop is advertised with 512 GB of storage, but the system shows about 476 GB. Why?

Yangi noutbuk 512 GB doimiy xotira bilan reklama qilingan, ammo tizim taxminan 476 GB koʻrsatadi. Nega?

Options
  • ✓ Different ways of counting gigabytes, plus space used by the system
  • The laptop is faulty — This difference is normal on every computer.
  • A virus is hiding files — The difference is expected and has nothing to do with viruses.
  • ✓ Gigabaytlar har xil usulda hisoblanadi, ustiga tizim ham joy egallaydi
  • Noutbuk nosoz — Bu farq har qanday kompyuterda odatiy hol.
  • Virus fayllarni yashirmoqda — Bu kutilgan farq, uning viruslarga aloqasi yoʻq.
Explanation

Manufacturers count in thousands, operating systems often in units of 1 024, and the system itself uses some space.

Ishlab chiqaruvchilar mingliklarda, operatsion tizimlar esa koʻpincha 1 024 lik birliklarda hisoblaydi, tizimning oʻzi ham biroz joy egallaydi.

Q5

Depending on the device, what can a USB-C port carry? Select all that apply.

Qurilmaga qarab USB-C porti nimalarni uzata oladi? Mos keladigan barcha variantlarni belgilang.

Options
  • ✓ Data
  • ✓ Charging power
  • ✓ Video to a monitor
  • A Wi-Fi signal — Wi-Fi is wireless; it does not travel through a USB-C port.
  • ✓ Maʼlumot
  • ✓ Quvvatlash toki
  • ✓ Monitorga tasvir
  • Wi-Fi signali — Wi-Fi simsiz ishlaydi; u USB-C porti orqali uzatilmaydi.
Explanation

USB-C can carry data, power and video — but each device decides which of these its ports support.

USB-C maʼlumot, quvvat va tasvir uzata oladi — ammo uning portlari qaysilarini qoʻllab-quvvatlashini har bir qurilmaning oʻzi belgilaydi.

Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Modul: Fayllar bilan ishlash (Working with files)

Module description
How information is organised so you can always find it again.
Maʼlumotni har doim qayta topa olishingiz uchun u qanday tartibga solinadi.

Fayllar, papkalar va yoʻllar — Files, folders and paths · article · 238 words

Summary
Name things well, organise them in folders, and read a path like a street address.
Fayllarni toʻgʻri nomlang, papkalarga joylang va fayl yoʻlini koʻcha manzili kabi oʻqing.
Lesson text

What is a file?

A file is a single piece of stored information with a name: a document, a photo, a song, a program. Files live on storage (your SSD or hard drive, a USB stick, or the cloud).

Most file names end with a dot and a few letters, for example report.pdf or holiday.jpg. That ending is the file extension. It tells the computer what kind of file it is and which app should open it.

Extension Kind of file
.pdf Document that looks the same everywhere
.docx Word document
.jpg, .png Image
.mp4 Video
.mp3 Audio
.zip Several files compressed into one

[!WARNING] Renaming photo.jpg to photo.mp4 does not turn a photo into a video. It only confuses the computer about which app to use.

Folders

A folder (also called a directory) is a container for files and other folders. Folders inside folders create a tree:

Documents
├── School
│   ├── math-homework.pdf
│   └── essay.docx
└── Work
    └── invoice-march.pdf

Paths: a file’s address

A path is the full address of a file — every folder you pass through to reach it.

  • On Windows: C:\Users\Aziza\Documents\School\essay.docx
  • On macOS and Linux: /Users/aziza/Documents/School/essay.docx

Read it left to right like an address: drive or root → user → folder → sub-folder → file. Windows uses a backslash \ between folders; macOS and Linux use a forward slash /.

[!TIP] Good habits that professionals use:

  • Use clear names: invoice-2026-03.pdf beats scan1 final FINAL.pdf.
  • Put dates as YYYY-MM-DD so files sort in order.
  • Avoid spaces and special symbols in names for files you will use in code or on the web.
  • Keep a backup of important files in a second place.

Fayl nima?

Fayl — nomga ega boʻlgan, saqlangan bitta maʼlumot birligi: hujjat, fotosurat, qoʻshiq yoki dastur. Fayllar doimiy xotirada (SSD yoki qattiq diskda, fleshkada yoki bulutda) joylashadi.

Koʻpchilik fayl nomlari nuqta va bir nechta harf bilan tugaydi, masalan, hisobot.pdf yoki dam-olish.jpg. Ana shu oxirgi qism fayl kengaytmasi deyiladi. U kompyuterga faylning turini va uni qaysi ilova ochishi kerakligini bildiradi.

Kengaytma Fayl turi
.pdf Har qanday qurilmada bir xil koʻrinadigan hujjat
.docx Word hujjati
.jpg, .png Tasvir
.mp4 Video
.mp3 Audio
.zip Bitta faylga siqilgan bir nechta fayl

[!WARNING] rasm.jpg faylini rasm.mp4 deb qayta nomlash fotosuratni videoga aylantirmaydi. Bu faqat kompyuterni qaysi ilovadan foydalanish kerakligi borasida chalgʻitadi.

Papkalar

Papka (yoki katalog, direktoriya) — fayllar va boshqa papkalar uchun idish. Papka ichidagi papkalar daraxtsimon tuzilmani hosil qiladi:

Documents
├── Maktab
│   ├── matematika-uy-vazifasi.pdf
│   └── insho.docx
└── Ish
    └── hisob-faktura-mart.pdf

Yoʻl: faylning manzili

Yoʻl (inglizcha path) — faylning toʻliq manzili, yaʼni unga yetib borish uchun oʻtadigan barcha papkalar.

  • Windowsda: C:\Users\Aziza\Documents\Maktab\insho.docx
  • macOS va Linuxda: /Users/aziza/Documents/Maktab/insho.docx

Uni manzil kabi chapdan oʻngga oʻqing: disk yoki ildiz → foydalanuvchi → papka → ichki papka → fayl. Windows papkalar orasida teskari qiya chiziq \, macOS va Linux esa toʻgʻri qiya chiziq / ishlatadi. Users va Documents kabi tizim papkalari koʻpincha inglizcha nom bilan qoladi.

[!TIP] Mutaxassislar amal qiladigan foydali odatlar:

  • Aniq nom bering: hisob-faktura-2026-03.pdf nomi skan1 yakuniy YAKUNIY.pdf dan ancha yaxshi.
  • Fayllar tartib bilan saralanishi uchun sanani YYYY-MM-DD (yil-oy-kun) koʻrinishida yozing.
  • Kodda yoki internetda ishlatiladigan fayllar nomida boʻsh joy va maxsus belgilardan qoching.
  • Muhim fayllarning zaxira nusxasini boshqa joyda ham saqlang.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:

Dasturlarni xavfsiz oʻrnatish va oʻchirish — Installing and removing software safely · article · 448 words

Summary
Where to download apps, how installers work and how to uninstall cleanly.
Ilovalarni qayerdan yuklab olish, oʻrnatuvchilar qanday ishlashi va dasturni toʻliq oʻchirish.
Lesson text

Where software should come from

In this lesson you will learn how to install and remove programs without picking up malware or unwanted extras. Safe sources, in order of preference:

  1. The official app store of your system — Microsoft Store on Windows, the App Store on macOS. Apps there are checked before they are listed.
  2. The developer's official website — for example, videolan.org for VLC or python.org for Python. Type the address yourself or check it carefully.
  3. A package manager — a tool that installs software from trusted lists. You will meet these in later courses.

[!WARNING] “Cracked” or pirated software is one of the most common ways malware spreads, and using it is illegal. If a program costs money, look for an official free version, an education discount or a free alternative.

Red flags on download pages

  • Several large “Download” buttons — usually advertisements leading elsewhere.
  • A file that does not match what you expect (for example, a 1 MB file for a 300 MB program).
  • A site that is not the developer's own domain.

Installing on Windows

Windows installers are usually .exe or .msi files. When you run one:

  • A User Account Control prompt asks whether the app may make changes. Allow it only for software you trust.
  • Read every screen. Installers sometimes offer extra software such as browser toolbars — untick these offers.
  • Choose the default install folder unless you have a reason to change it.

Installing on macOS

Mac apps usually come as .dmg files. Open the file and drag the app into the Applications folder. If macOS warns that the developer cannot be verified, stop and check where the file came from instead of bypassing the warning.

Keeping software updated

Updates fix security problems as well as adding features. Turn on automatic updates where available, and install updates for your browser and operating system promptly.

Removing software

  • Windows: Settings → Apps → Installed apps, choose the app, then Uninstall.
  • macOS: drag the app from Applications to the Trash. Some apps include their own uninstaller — use it if there is one.

Uninstalling programs you no longer use frees space and removes software that would otherwise need updates.

Try it

Install a free program from its official website — for example, the VLC media player from videolan.org. Open it once, then uninstall it using the steps above.

Common mistakes

  • Clicking the first search result, which is often an advertisement.
  • Clicking Next on every installer screen without reading it.
  • Leaving old, unused programs installed and out of date.

Key terms

  • Installer — a file that copies a program onto your computer and sets it up.
  • Malware — software designed to harm you or your computer.
  • Uninstall — to remove a program properly.
  • Update — a new version that fixes problems or adds features.

Dasturlar qayerdan olinishi kerak

Bu darsda dasturlarni zararli yoki keraksiz qoʻshimchalarsiz oʻrnatish va oʻchirishni oʻrganasiz. Xavfsiz manbalar, afzallik tartibida:

  1. Tizimingizning rasmiy ilovalar doʻkoni — Windowsda Microsoft Store, macOSda App Store. U yerdagi ilovalar roʻyxatga kiritilishidan oldin tekshiriladi.
  2. Ishlab chiqaruvchining rasmiy sayti — masalan, VLC uchun videolan.org yoki Python uchun python.org. Manzilni oʻzingiz yozing yoki diqqat bilan tekshiring.
  3. Paketlar menejeri — dasturlarni ishonchli roʻyxatlardan oʻrnatadigan vosita. Ular bilan keyingi kurslarda tanishasiz.

[!WARNING] “Buzilgan” (cracked) yoki qaroqchilik yoʻli bilan tarqatilgan dasturlar zararli dasturlar tarqalishining eng keng tarqalgan yoʻllaridan biri, ulardan foydalanish esa qonunga xilof. Agar dastur pullik boʻlsa, uning rasmiy bepul versiyasini, taʼlim uchun chegirmani yoki bepul muqobilini qidiring.

Yuklab olish sahifalaridagi xavf belgilari

  • Bir nechta katta “Download” tugmasi — odatda boshqa joyga olib boradigan reklamalar.
  • Kutilganga mos kelmaydigan fayl (masalan, 300 MB lik dastur oʻrniga 1 MB lik fayl).
  • Ishlab chiqaruvchining oʻz domeni boʻlmagan sayt.

Windowsda oʻrnatish

Windows oʻrnatuvchilari odatda .exe yoki .msi fayllari boʻladi. Ulardan birini ishga tushirganingizda:

  • User Account Control (foydalanuvchi hisoblarini boshqarish) oynasi ilovaga oʻzgartirishlar kiritishga ruxsat berish-bermaslikni soʻraydi. Faqat ishonchli dasturlarga ruxsat bering.
  • Har bir oynani oʻqing. Oʻrnatuvchilar baʼzan brauzer panellari kabi qoʻshimcha dasturlarni taklif qiladi — bu takliflardan belgini olib tashlang.
  • Biror sababingiz boʻlmasa, standart oʻrnatish papkasini tanlang.

macOSda oʻrnatish

Mac ilovalari odatda .dmg fayllari koʻrinishida keladi. Faylni oching va ilovani Applications papkasiga torting. Agar macOS ishlab chiquvchini tasdiqlab boʻlmasligi haqida ogohlantirsa, ogohlantirishni chetlab oʻtish oʻrniga toʻxtang va fayl qayerdan kelganini tekshiring.

Dasturlarni yangilab turish

Yangilanishlar yangi imkoniyatlar qoʻshish bilan birga xavfsizlik muammolarini ham tuzatadi. Imkon boʻlgan joyda avtomatik yangilanishni yoqing, brauzer va operatsion tizim yangilanishlarini kechiktirmasdan oʻrnating.

Dasturlarni oʻchirish

  • Windows: Settings → Apps → Installed apps boʻlimida ilovani tanlang, soʻngra Uninstall ni bosing.
  • macOS: ilovani Applications papkasidan Trash (savat) ga torting. Baʼzi ilovalarning oʻz oʻchirish dasturi boʻladi — agar bor boʻlsa, undan foydalaning.

Foydalanmaydigan dasturlarni oʻchirish joy boʻshatadi va aks holda yangilab turish kerak boʻladigan dasturlardan xalos qiladi.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Bepul dasturni uning rasmiy saytidan oʻrnating — masalan, VLC media pleyerini videolan.org saytidan. Uni bir marta oching, soʻngra yuqoridagi qadamlar bilan oʻchiring.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Koʻpincha reklama boʻlib chiqadigan birinchi qidiruv natijasini bosish.
  • Oʻrnatuvchining har bir oynasida oʻqimasdan Next ni bosish.
  • Eski, foydalanilmaydigan dasturlarni yangilamasdan oʻrnatilgan holda qoldirish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Oʻrnatuvchi (installer) — dasturni kompyuteringizga koʻchiradigan va sozlaydigan fayl.
  • Zararli dastur (malware) — sizga yoki kompyuteringizga zarar yetkazish uchun yaratilgan dastur.
  • Oʻchirish (uninstall) — dasturni toʻgʻri usulda olib tashlash.
  • Yangilanish (update) — muammolarni tuzatadigan yoki imkoniyat qoʻshadigan yangi versiya.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Zaxira nusxalar va bulut — Backups and the cloud · article · 408 words

Summary
The 3-2-1 backup rule, how cloud storage works and why sync is not the same as backup.
3-2-1 zaxiralash qoidasi, bulutli xotira qanday ishlashi va nega sinxronlash zaxira nusxa bilan bir xil emasligi.
Lesson text

Why backups matter

Sooner or later, every device fails, gets lost or is stolen — and people delete the wrong file by accident. In this lesson you will learn how to keep copies so that losing a device never means losing your work.

Common causes of data loss:

  • A storage drive fails, often without warning.
  • A laptop or phone is lost or stolen.
  • A file is deleted or overwritten by mistake.
  • Ransomware — malware that locks your files and demands payment.

The 3-2-1 rule

Professionals follow a simple rule:

  • 3 copies of important data (the original and two backups),
  • on 2 different kinds of storage (for example, your laptop and an external drive),
  • with 1 copy kept off-site (in the cloud or at another location).

If the house floods, the off-site copy survives. If the cloud account is locked, the external drive still has your files.

Cloud storage

Services such as Google Drive, iCloud and OneDrive keep your files on their servers and make them available on all your devices. Most offer some free space.

[!WARNING] Sync is not a backup. A synced folder copies every change everywhere — including deletions. If you delete a file on your laptop, it disappears from the cloud too. Use the service's version history or a separate backup to protect against this.

Built-in backup tools

  • Windows: Settings → Accounts → Windows Backup, and File History for keeping older versions of files on an external drive.
  • macOS: Time Machine backs up the whole Mac to an external drive automatically.

Test your backups

A backup you have never restored is only a hope. Every few months, restore one file from your backup to check that it works.

A worked example

Aziza is a student with essays, photos and project files:

Copy Where Kind of storage
Original Laptop SSD Internal drive
Backup 1 External drive, Time Machine weekly External drive
Backup 2 Cloud folder with version history Off-site

Try it

Make a table like Aziza's for your own important files. Then switch on the built-in backup tool on your computer, or choose a cloud service.

Common mistakes

  • Treating a synced folder as a backup.
  • Keeping the backup drive plugged in all the time, where ransomware can reach it too.
  • Never testing whether files can actually be restored.

Key terms

  • Backup — a separate copy kept so you can restore lost data.
  • 3-2-1 rule — three copies, two kinds of storage, one off-site.
  • Sync — keeping the same files identical on several devices.
  • Ransomware — malware that locks files and demands payment.

Zaxira nusxalar nega muhim

Ertami-kechmi har qanday qurilma buziladi, yoʻqoladi yoki oʻgʻirlanadi — odamlar esa notoʻgʻri faylni bilmasdan oʻchirib yuboradi. Bu darsda qurilmani yoʻqotish hech qachon ishingizni yoʻqotish degani boʻlmasligi uchun nusxalarni qanday saqlashni oʻrganasiz.

Maʼlumot yoʻqolishining keng tarqalgan sabablari:

  • Doimiy xotira qurilmasi koʻpincha ogohlantirishsiz ishdan chiqadi.
  • Noutbuk yoki telefon yoʻqoladi yoki oʻgʻirlanadi.
  • Fayl xato tufayli oʻchirib yuboriladi yoki ustidan yoziladi.
  • Ransomware (toʻlov talab qiluvchi dastur) — fayllaringizni qulflab, pul talab qiladigan zararli dastur.

3-2-1 qoidasi

Mutaxassislar oddiy qoidaga amal qiladi:

  • muhim maʼlumotlarning 3 nusxasi (asl nusxa va ikkita zaxira),
  • 2 xil turdagi xotirada (masalan, noutbukingiz va tashqi disk),
  • 1 nusxa boshqa joyda (bulutda yoki boshqa manzilda) saqlanadi.

Agar uyni suv bossa, boshqa joydagi nusxa saqlanib qoladi. Agar bulut hisobi bloklansa, fayllaringiz tashqi diskda baribir boʻladi.

Bulutli xotira

Google Drive, iCloud va OneDrive kabi xizmatlar fayllaringizni oʻz serverlarida saqlaydi va ularni barcha qurilmalaringizda ochish imkonini beradi. Aksariyati maʼlum hajmdagi joyni bepul beradi.

[!WARNING] Sinxronlash — zaxira nusxa emas. Sinxronlangan papka har bir oʻzgarishni hamma joyga koʻchiradi — oʻchirishlarni ham. Noutbukingizda faylni oʻchirsangiz, u bulutdan ham yoʻqoladi. Bunga qarshi xizmatning versiyalar tarixidan yoki alohida zaxira nusxadan foydalaning.

Oʻrnatilgan zaxiralash vositalari

  • Windows: Settings → Accounts → Windows Backup hamda fayllarning eski versiyalarini tashqi diskda saqlash uchun File History.
  • macOS: Time Machine butun Mac kompyuterni tashqi diskka avtomatik zaxiralaydi.

Zaxira nusxalaringizni tekshiring

Hech qachon tiklab koʻrilmagan zaxira nusxa — shunchaki umid. Har bir necha oyda zaxira nusxadan bitta faylni tiklab, uning ishlashini tekshiring.

Ishlangan misol

Aziza — insholar, fotosuratlar va loyiha fayllari bor talaba:

Nusxa Qayerda Xotira turi
Asl nusxa Noutbuk SSD si Ichki disk
1-zaxira Tashqi disk, haftada bir marta Time Machine Tashqi disk
2-zaxira Versiyalar tarixi bor bulut papkasi Boshqa joyda

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Muhim fayllaringiz uchun Azizanikiga oʻxshash jadval tuzing. Keyin kompyuteringizdagi oʻrnatilgan zaxiralash vositasini yoqing yoki bulut xizmatini tanlang.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Sinxronlangan papkani zaxira nusxa deb hisoblash.
  • Zaxira diskni doim ulangan holda qoldirish — u yerga ransomware ham yetib boradi.
  • Fayllarni haqiqatan ham tiklash mumkinligini hech qachon tekshirmaslik.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Zaxira nusxa (backup) — yoʻqolgan maʼlumotni tiklash uchun alohida saqlanadigan nusxa.
  • 3-2-1 qoidasi — uchta nusxa, ikki xil xotira, bittasi boshqa joyda.
  • Sinxronlash (sync) — bir nechta qurilmadagi fayllarni bir xil holatda saqlash.
  • Ransomware — fayllarni qulflab, pul talab qiladigan zararli dastur.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Fayllar bilan ishlash: bilimni tekshirish — Working with files: check · quiz

Summary
Five questions on files, safe installs and backups.
Fayllar, xavfsiz oʻrnatish va zaxira nusxalar boʻyicha beshta savol.
Test: “Fayllar bilan ishlash” moduli boʻyicha test
Q1

What does the 3-2-1 backup rule say?

3-2-1 zaxiralash qoidasi nimani bildiradi?

Options
  • ✓ 3 copies, 2 kinds of storage, 1 copy off-site
  • 3 backups a day, 2 passwords, 1 cloud account
  • 3 folders, 2 drives, 1 computer
  • ✓ 3 ta nusxa, 2 xil xotira, 1 nusxa boshqa joyda
  • Kuniga 3 ta zaxira, 2 ta parol, 1 ta bulut hisobi
  • 3 ta papka, 2 ta disk, 1 ta kompyuter
Explanation

Three copies, on two different kinds of storage, with one copy off-site.

Uchta nusxa, ikki xil turdagi xotirada, bittasi boshqa joyda.

Q2

True or false: a synced cloud folder is a complete backup on its own.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: sinxronlangan bulut papkasining oʻzi toʻliq zaxira nusxa hisoblanadi.

Options
  • True — If you delete a file, sync deletes it everywhere.
  • ✓ False
  • Toʻgʻri — Faylni oʻchirsangiz, sinxronlash uni hamma joydan oʻchiradi.
  • ✓ Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

False. Sync copies deletions too; you need version history or a separate backup.

Notoʻgʻri. Sinxronlash oʻchirishlarni ham koʻchiradi; sizga versiyalar tarixi yoki alohida zaxira nusxa kerak.

Q3

Where is the safest place to download a program?

Dasturni yuklab olish uchun eng xavfsiz joy qayer?

Options
  • ✓ The official app store or the developer's website
  • The first advert in the search results — Adverts often lead to look-alike sites.
  • A site offering a “cracked” version — Cracked software is illegal and a common source of malware.
  • ✓ Rasmiy ilovalar doʻkoni yoki ishlab chiqaruvchining sayti
  • Qidiruv natijalaridagi birinchi reklama — Reklamalar koʻpincha oʻxshatib yasalgan saytlarga olib boradi.
  • “Buzilgan” versiyani taklif qiladigan sayt — Buzilgan dasturlar qonunga xilof va zararli dasturlarning keng tarqalgan manbai.
Explanation

The official app store or the developer's own website.

Rasmiy ilovalar doʻkoni yoki ishlab chiqaruvchining oʻz sayti.

Q4

An installer offers to add a “free browser toolbar”. What should you do?

Oʻrnatuvchi “bepul brauzer paneli”ni qoʻshishni taklif qilmoqda. Nima qilish kerak?

Options
  • ✓ Untick the offer and continue
  • Accept it — it is free — Bundled extras are often unwanted or harmful.
  • Cancel and never install anything again — You can install safely by reading each screen.
  • ✓ Taklifdan belgini olib tashlab, davom etish
  • Qabul qilish — axir u bepul — Qoʻshib berilgan qoʻshimchalar koʻpincha keraksiz yoki zararli boʻladi.
  • Bekor qilib, boshqa hech narsa oʻrnatmaslik — Har bir oynani oʻqib, xavfsiz oʻrnatishingiz mumkin.
Explanation

Untick the extra offer; install only what you came for.

Qoʻshimcha taklifdan belgini olib tashlang; faqat kerakli dasturni oʻrnating.

Q5

Why should you not keep a backup drive plugged in all the time?

Nega zaxira diskni doim ulangan holda qoldirmaslik kerak?

Options
  • ✓ Ransomware could lock the backup too
  • It makes the computer slower — A connected drive does not slow the computer down.
  • The drive will run out of power — External drives do not have their own batteries to drain.
  • ✓ Ransomware zaxira nusxani ham qulflashi mumkin
  • U kompyuterni sekinlashtiradi — Ulangan disk kompyuterni sekinlashtirmaydi.
  • Diskning quvvati tugab qoladi — Tashqi disklarning quvvati tugaydigan oʻz batareyasi yoʻq.
Explanation

Ransomware can lock files on every connected drive, including the backup.

Ransomware ulangan barcha disklardagi, jumladan, zaxira diskdagi fayllarni ham qulflashi mumkin.
