Izohlar:

Modul: Xavfsizlik va nazorat (Staying safe and in control)

Module description
How the operating system protects you, and how to protect your accounts.
Operatsion tizim sizni qanday himoya qiladi va hisoblaringizni qanday himoya qilish kerak.

Operatsion tizim nima qiladi — What an operating system does · article · 437 words

Summary
Users, permissions, updates and the tools that show what your computer is doing.
Foydalanuvchilar, ruxsatlar, yangilanishlar va kompyuter nima qilayotganini koʻrsatadigan vositalar.
Lesson text

The manager of the computer

You already know that the operating system (OS) is the main program that runs everything else. In this lesson you will see what it manages for you — and which of its settings keep you safe.

The OS has five main jobs:

  1. Hardware — it talks to the processor, memory, storage and devices through drivers.
  2. Programs — it starts apps, shares processor time between them and stops them.
  3. Files — it organises storage into files and folders.
  4. Users and security — it keeps each person's files separate and decides who may do what.
  5. Interface — it gives you the desktop, windows, menus and touch gestures.

Common operating systems are Windows, macOS and Linux on computers, and Android and iOS on phones.

Users and accounts

Each person who uses a computer should have their own user account, with their own files and settings.

  • An administrator account can install software and change system settings.
  • A standard account can use apps but needs an administrator's approval for system changes.

[!TIP] Using a standard account for everyday work limits the damage if you open a harmful file by mistake: malware cannot change the system without asking.

Permissions

Apps must ask before using sensitive things such as the camera, microphone, location, contacts or your files. Grant only what an app genuinely needs. A flashlight app has no reason to read your contacts.

  • Windows: Settings → Privacy & security.
  • macOS: System Settings → Privacy & Security.
  • Phones: Settings → Apps (Android) or Settings → Privacy & Security (iOS).

Updates

OS updates fix security holes that criminals actively use. Install them promptly, and restart when asked — many updates only finish after a restart.

Seeing what is running

When an app freezes, the OS can show you what is happening:

  • Windows: Task Manager (Ctrl + Shift + Esc) lists running apps and how much CPU and memory each uses. Select a frozen app and choose End task.
  • macOS: Activity Monitor (in Applications → Utilities) does the same; Force Quit stops a frozen app (Option + Command + Esc).

Try it

  1. Find which version of your operating system you are running.
  2. Open the privacy settings and check which apps may use your camera and microphone.
  3. Open Task Manager or Activity Monitor and find the app using the most memory.

Common mistakes

  • Postponing updates for weeks.
  • Using an administrator account for everything.
  • Allowing every permission an app asks for without thinking.

Key terms

  • Operating system (OS) — the program that manages the hardware and runs all other programs.
  • Administrator / standard account — an account that can or cannot change the system.
  • Permission — an app's right to use something sensitive, such as the camera.
  • Task Manager / Activity Monitor — tools that show running programs.

Kompyuterning boshqaruvchisi

Siz operatsion tizim (OT) qolgan barcha dasturlarni ishga tushiradigan asosiy dastur ekanini allaqachon bilasiz. Bu darsda u siz uchun nimalarni boshqarishini — va uning qaysi sozlamalari sizni xavfsiz saqlashini koʻrasiz.

Operatsion tizimning beshta asosiy vazifasi bor:

  1. Apparat — u drayverlar orqali protsessor, xotira, doimiy xotira va qurilmalar bilan aloqa qiladi.
  2. Dasturlar — ilovalarni ishga tushiradi, protsessor vaqtini ular oʻrtasida taqsimlaydi va ularni toʻxtatadi.
  3. Fayllar — doimiy xotirani fayl va papkalarga ajratib tartibga soladi.
  4. Foydalanuvchilar va xavfsizlik — har bir odamning fayllarini alohida saqlaydi va kim nima qila olishini hal qiladi.
  5. Interfeys — sizga ish stoli, oynalar, menyular va sensorli imo-ishoralarni taqdim etadi.

Keng tarqalgan operatsion tizimlar: kompyuterlarda Windows, macOS va Linux, telefonlarda Android va iOS.

Foydalanuvchilar va hisoblar

Kompyuterdan foydalanadigan har bir kishining oʻz fayllari va sozlamalari bilan alohida foydalanuvchi hisobi boʻlishi kerak.

  • Administrator hisobi dasturlarni oʻrnatishi va tizim sozlamalarini oʻzgartirishi mumkin.
  • Standart hisob ilovalardan foydalana oladi, ammo tizimdagi oʻzgarishlar uchun administrator ruxsati kerak.

[!TIP] Kundalik ish uchun standart hisobdan foydalanish zararli faylni bilmasdan ochib qoʻysangiz, zararni cheklaydi: zararli dastur soʻramasdan tizimni oʻzgartira olmaydi.

Ruxsatlar

Ilovalar kamera, mikrofon, joylashuv, kontaktlar yoki fayllaringiz kabi maxfiy narsalardan foydalanishdan oldin ruxsat soʻrashi shart. Faqat ilovaga haqiqatan kerak boʻlgan ruxsatni bering. Fonar ilovasining kontaktlaringizni oʻqishiga hech qanday sabab yoʻq.

  • Windows: Settings → Privacy & security.
  • macOS: System Settings → Privacy & Security.
  • Telefonlar: Settings → Apps (Android) yoki Settings → Privacy & Security (iOS).

Yangilanishlar

Operatsion tizim yangilanishlari jinoyatchilar faol foydalanadigan xavfsizlik teshiklarini yopadi. Ularni kechiktirmasdan oʻrnating va soʻralganda qayta ishga tushiring — koʻplab yangilanishlar faqat qayta ishga tushirilgandan keyin yakunlanadi.

Nima ishlayotganini koʻrish

Ilova qotib qolganda operatsion tizim nima boʻlayotganini koʻrsata oladi:

  • Windows: Task Manager (Ctrl + Shift + Esc) ishlab turgan ilovalarni va ularning har biri qancha protsessor va xotira ishlatayotganini koʻrsatadi. Qotib qolgan ilovani tanlab, End task ni bosing.
  • macOS: Activity Monitor (Applications → Utilities ichida) xuddi shu ishni qiladi; Force Quit qotib qolgan ilovani toʻxtatadi (Option + Command + Esc).

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

  1. Operatsion tizimingizning qaysi versiyasi oʻrnatilganini toping.
  2. Maxfiylik sozlamalarini ochib, qaysi ilovalar kamera va mikrofondan foydalana olishini tekshiring.
  3. Task Manager yoki Activity Monitor ni ochib, eng koʻp xotira ishlatayotgan ilovani toping.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Yangilanishlarni haftalab kechiktirish.
  • Hamma ish uchun administrator hisobidan foydalanish.
  • Ilova soʻragan har bir ruxsatni oʻylamasdan berish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Operatsion tizim (OT) — apparatni boshqaradigan va qolgan barcha dasturlarni ishga tushiradigan dastur.
  • Administrator / standart hisob — tizimni oʻzgartira oladigan yoki oʻzgartira olmaydigan hisob.
  • Ruxsat — ilovaning kamera kabi maxfiy narsadan foydalanish huquqi.
  • Task Manager / Activity Monitor — ishlab turgan dasturlarni koʻrsatadigan vositalar.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Parollar, ikki bosqichli autentifikatsiya va fishing — Passwords, two-factor authentication and phishing · article · 499 words

Summary
Strong passwords, a second factor for every important account, and how to spot a scam.
Kuchli parollar, har bir muhim hisob uchun ikkinchi bosqich va firibgarlikni qanday aniqlash.
Lesson text

Your accounts are your keys

Email, messengers, banking and study accounts hold a large part of your life. In this lesson you will learn three habits that stop most account thefts: strong unique passwords, two-factor authentication and recognising phishing.

Strong passwords

  • Length beats complexity. A long passphrase of four or more random words, such as river-lamp-orange-cloud, is far harder to guess than P@ss1.
  • One password per account. When one website is hacked, criminals try the same email and password everywhere else.
  • Use a password manager. It creates and remembers strong, unique passwords, so you only need to remember one main password. Well-known managers are built into browsers and phones, and standalone apps exist too.

[!WARNING] Never use personal information — your name, birthday or phone number — in a password. It is the first thing attackers try.

Two-factor authentication (2FA)

2FA means logging in needs two different proofs: something you know (your password) and something you have (your phone or a security key).

  • An authenticator app shows a new six-digit code every 30 seconds — the recommended option.
  • An SMS code is weaker, because phone numbers can be hijacked, but it is still much better than no second factor.
  • Passkeys let you sign in with your fingerprint or face instead of a password, and cannot be phished.

Turn on 2FA first for your email account — it can reset all your other passwords.

Phishing

Phishing is a message that pretends to be from someone you trust — a bank, a delivery company, a messenger's support team — to trick you into giving away a password or code.

Warning signs:

  • Urgency or threats: “Your card will be blocked in one hour.”
  • A link that does not match: the text says your bank, but the address is something like bank-secure-login.example.
  • A request for a one-time code. No real bank or messenger will ever ask you to tell them a code you received by SMS.

[!TIP] If a message worries you, do not use its links or phone numbers. Open the official app or call the number printed on your bank card.

If you clicked a phishing link

  1. Change the password of that account immediately (and anywhere you reused it).
  2. Turn on 2FA if it was off.
  3. Check the account's list of active sessions or devices and sign out ones you do not recognise.
  4. If money or a bank card is involved, call your bank straight away.

Try it

Turn on two-factor authentication for your main email account using an authenticator app. Then look at a recent message from a company and check the real address behind its links.

Common mistakes

  • Reusing one password for several sites.
  • Telling a one-time code to someone on the phone who “works at the bank”.
  • Trusting a message because it shows a familiar logo.

Key terms

  • Passphrase — a long password made of several words.
  • Password manager — an app that creates and stores unique passwords.
  • Two-factor authentication (2FA) — logging in with two different proofs of identity.
  • Phishing — a fake message designed to steal passwords, codes or money.

Hisoblaringiz — sizning kalitlaringiz

Elektron pochta, messenjerlar, bank va oʻqish hisoblari hayotingizning katta qismini saqlaydi. Bu darsda hisoblar oʻgʻirlanishining aksariyatini toʻxtatadigan uchta odatni oʻrganasiz: kuchli va takrorlanmas parollar, ikki bosqichli autentifikatsiya hamda fishingni tanib olish.

Kuchli parollar

  • Uzunlik murakkablikdan muhimroq. daryo-chiroq-apelsin-bulut kabi toʻrt va undan ortiq tasodifiy soʻzdan iborat uzun parol iborasini topish P@ss1 dan ancha qiyin.
  • Har bir hisob uchun alohida parol. Bitta sayt buzilganda jinoyatchilar oʻsha email va parolni boshqa barcha joylarda sinab koʻradi.
  • Parollar menejeridan foydalaning. U kuchli va takrorlanmas parollarni yaratadi va eslab qoladi, shuning uchun siz faqat bitta asosiy parolni eslab qolishingiz kifoya. Mashhur menejerlar brauzer va telefonlarga oʻrnatilgan, alohida ilovalar ham bor.

[!WARNING] Parolda hech qachon shaxsiy maʼlumotlardan — ismingiz, tugʻilgan kuningiz yoki telefon raqamingizdan foydalanmang. Hujumchilar birinchi navbatda aynan shularni sinab koʻradi.

Ikki bosqichli autentifikatsiya (2FA)

2FA — tizimga kirish uchun ikki xil dalil kerak degani: siz biladigan narsa (parolingiz) va sizda bor narsa (telefoningiz yoki xavfsizlik kaliti).

  • Autentifikator ilovasi har 30 soniyada yangi olti xonali kod koʻrsatadi — tavsiya etiladigan usul.
  • SMS kod kuchsizroq, chunki telefon raqamini egallab olish mumkin, ammo u baribir ikkinchi bosqichning umuman yoʻqligidan ancha yaxshi.
  • Passkey (kirish kaliti) parol oʻrniga barmoq izi yoki yuz bilan kirish imkonini beradi va uni fishing orqali oʻgʻirlab boʻlmaydi.

2FA ni birinchi navbatda elektron pochtangiz uchun yoqing — u orqali boshqa barcha parollarni tiklash mumkin.

Fishing

Fishing — parol yoki kodni berib qoʻyishingiz uchun siz ishonadigan kimsa — bank, yetkazib berish xizmati yoki messenjerning qoʻllab-quvvatlash xizmati nomidan yuborilgan soxta xabar.

Ogohlantiruvchi belgilar:

  • Shoshiltirish yoki tahdid: “Kartangiz bir soatdan keyin bloklanadi.”
  • Mos kelmaydigan havola: matnda bankingiz nomi yozilgan, lekin manzil bank-secure-login.example ga oʻxshash.
  • Bir martalik kodni soʻrash. Hech bir haqiqiy bank yoki messenjer sizdan SMS orqali kelgan kodni aytishingizni soʻramaydi.

[!TIP] Biror xabar sizni xavotirga solsa, undagi havola yoki telefon raqamlaridan foydalanmang. Rasmiy ilovani oching yoki bank kartangizda yozilgan raqamga qoʻngʻiroq qiling.

Agar fishing havolasini bosib qoʻygan boʻlsangiz

  1. Oʻsha hisob parolini (va u takrorlangan barcha joylarda) darhol oʻzgartiring.
  2. Agar 2FA oʻchiq boʻlsa, uni yoqing.
  3. Hisobdagi faol seanslar yoki qurilmalar roʻyxatini tekshiring va tanimaganlaringizdan chiqib keting.
  4. Agar pul yoki bank kartasi bilan bogʻliq boʻlsa, darhol bankingizga qoʻngʻiroq qiling.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Asosiy elektron pochta hisobingiz uchun autentifikator ilovasi orqali ikki bosqichli autentifikatsiyani yoqing. Soʻngra biror kompaniyadan kelgan soʻnggi xabarni ochib, undagi havolalarning haqiqiy manzilini tekshiring.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Bitta parolni bir nechta saytda ishlatish.
  • “Bankda ishlayman” degan kishiga telefonda bir martalik kodni aytib berish.
  • Xabarda tanish logotip borligi uchun unga ishonish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Parol iborasi (passphrase) — bir nechta soʻzdan iborat uzun parol.
  • Parollar menejeri — takrorlanmas parollarni yaratadigan va saqlaydigan ilova.
  • Ikki bosqichli autentifikatsiya (2FA) — shaxsni ikki xil dalil bilan tasdiqlab kirish.
  • Fishing — parol, kod yoki pulni oʻgʻirlash uchun yuborilgan soxta xabar.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Xavfsizlik: bilimni tekshirish — Staying safe: check · quiz

Summary
Five questions on accounts, permissions, passwords and phishing.
Hisoblar, ruxsatlar, parollar va fishing boʻyicha beshta savol.
Test: “Xavfsizlik va nazorat” moduli boʻyicha test
Q1

Which password is the strongest?

Qaysi parol eng kuchli?

Options
  • ✓ `river-lamp-orange-cloud`
  • `P@ss1` — Short passwords are guessed quickly, even with symbols.
  • `Aziza2008` — Names and years are the first things attackers try.
  • ✓ `daryo-chiroq-apelsin-bulut`
  • `P@ss1` — Qisqa parollar belgilar boʻlsa ham tez topiladi.
  • `Aziza2008` — Ism va yillar hujumchilar birinchi sinab koʻradigan narsa.
Explanation

Length matters most: a passphrase of several random words beats a short complex password.

Eng muhimi — uzunlik: bir nechta tasodifiy soʻzdan iborat parol iborasi qisqa, murakkab paroldan kuchliroq.

Q2

True or false: it is safe to read out an SMS code to a caller who says they work at your bank and knows your name.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: bankda ishlashini aytgan va ismingizni biladigan qoʻngʻiroq qiluvchiga SMS kodni aytish xavfsiz.

Options
  • True — Knowing your name proves nothing; this is a classic scam.
  • ✓ False
  • Toʻgʻri — Ismingizni bilishi hech narsani isbotlamaydi; bu klassik firibgarlik.
  • ✓ Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

False. Banks never ask for one-time codes. Hang up and call the number on your card.

Notoʻgʻri. Banklar hech qachon bir martalik kodlarni soʻramaydi. Goʻshakni qoʻying va kartangizdagi raqamga qoʻngʻiroq qiling.

Q3

Why use a standard (non-administrator) account for everyday work?

Nega kundalik ish uchun standart (administrator boʻlmagan) hisobdan foydalanish kerak?

Options
  • ✓ It limits the damage from malware and mistakes
  • It makes the internet faster
  • It saves battery
  • ✓ U zararli dasturlar va xatolardan keladigan zararni cheklaydi
  • U internetni tezlashtiradi
  • U batareyani tejaydi
Explanation

Malware and mistakes cannot change the system without an administrator's approval.

Zararli dasturlar va xatolar administrator ruxsatisiz tizimni oʻzgartira olmaydi.

Q4

A flashlight app asks for access to your contacts. What should you do?

Fonar ilovasi kontaktlaringizdan foydalanishga ruxsat soʻramoqda. Nima qilasiz?

Options
  • ✓ Deny it
  • Allow it — apps need everything — Apps should get only the permissions they need.
  • Allow it once to see what happens — Once allowed, the app can copy your contacts.
  • ✓ Rad etaman
  • Ruxsat beraman — ilovalarga hammasi kerak — Ilovalar faqat kerakli ruxsatlarni olishi kerak.
  • Nima boʻlishini koʻrish uchun bir marta ruxsat beraman — Ruxsat berilgach, ilova kontaktlaringizdan nusxa olishi mumkin.
Explanation

Grant only what an app needs; a flashlight does not need your contacts.

Ilovaga faqat kerakli ruxsatni bering; fonarga kontaktlaringiz kerak emas.

Q5

Which are warning signs of phishing? Select all that apply.

Quyidagilardan qaysilari fishingning ogohlantiruvchi belgilari? Mos keladigan barcha variantlarni belgilang.

Options
  • ✓ An urgent threat, such as “your card will be blocked”
  • ✓ A link whose address does not match the company
  • ✓ A request for a one-time code
  • A message you expected, from a known contact, with ordinary content — Expected, ordinary messages are not a warning sign in themselves.
  • ✓ “Kartangiz bloklanadi” kabi shoshilinch tahdid
  • ✓ Manzili kompaniyaga mos kelmaydigan havola
  • ✓ Bir martalik kodni soʻrash
  • Tanish kontaktdan kutilgan, oddiy mazmundagi xabar — Kutilgan, oddiy xabarlarning oʻzi ogohlantiruvchi belgi emas.
Explanation

Urgency, mismatched links and requests for codes are classic signs.

Shoshiltirish, mos kelmaydigan havolalar va kod soʻrash — klassik belgilar.

Module description
Show what you have learned to earn your certificate.
Sertifikat olish uchun oʻrganganlaringizni koʻrsating.

Loyiha: kompyuterni sozlash va himoyalash — Project: set up and secure a computer · project · 148 words

Summary
Recommend, set up and protect a computer for a real person, and explain your choices.
Real inson uchun kompyuter tanlang, sozlang va himoyalang hamda tanlovlaringizni tushuntiring.
Lesson text

Your project

A relative or friend needs a computer for study or work. Prepare a short plan, as an IT professional would, that covers choosing, setting up and protecting it.

What to deliver

  1. Who it is for — the person, what they will use the computer for, and the budget.
  2. Specification — processor, RAM, storage and ports, with one sentence explaining each choice.
  3. Setup plan — user accounts (administrator and standard), updates, and the software to install from official sources.
  4. Backup plan — which files matter and how the 3-2-1 rule is met.
  5. Security plan — password manager, two-factor authentication for key accounts, and three phishing warning signs you will teach them.

How to submit

Write your plan in the text box (about one page), upload it as a document, or share a link to it.

[!NOTE] A reviewer checks your plan against the rubric and leaves feedback. You can improve it and resubmit.

Loyihangiz

Qarindoshingiz yoki doʻstingizga oʻqish yoki ish uchun kompyuter kerak. IT mutaxassisi kabi, kompyuterni tanlash, sozlash va himoyalashni qamrab oladigan qisqa reja tayyorlang.

Nimani topshirish kerak

  1. Kim uchun — inson, u kompyuterdan nima uchun foydalanishi va byudjet.
  2. Texnik tavsif — protsessor, RAM, doimiy xotira va portlar; har bir tanlov bir gap bilan izohlangan.
  3. Sozlash rejasi — foydalanuvchi hisoblari (administrator va standart), yangilanishlar va rasmiy manbalardan oʻrnatiladigan dasturlar.
  4. Zaxiralash rejasi — qaysi fayllar muhim va 3-2-1 qoidasiga qanday amal qilinadi.
  5. Xavfsizlik rejasi — parollar menejeri, asosiy hisoblar uchun ikki bosqichli autentifikatsiya va unga oʻrgatadigan fishingning uchta belgisi.

Qanday topshiriladi

Rejangizni matn maydoniga yozing (taxminan bir sahifa), hujjat sifatida yuklang yoki unga havola ulashing.

[!NOTE] Tekshiruvchi rejangizni baholash mezonlari asosida koʻrib chiqadi va fikr-mulohaza qoldiradi. Uni yaxshilab, qayta topshirishingiz mumkin.

Assignment brief

Write a one-page plan to choose, set up, back up and secure a computer for a real person.

Real inson uchun kompyuterni tanlash, sozlash, zaxiralash va himoyalash boʻyicha bir sahifalik reja yozing.

Rubric
Criterion Points
Specification fits the person and budget, with a reason for each part 25
Setup plan: accounts, updates and software from official sources 20
Backup plan that meets the 3-2-1 rule 25
Security plan: passwords, 2FA and phishing awareness 20
Clear, well-organised writing 10
Mezon Ball
Texnik tavsif insonga va byudjetga mos, har bir qism izohlangan 25
Sozlash rejasi: hisoblar, yangilanishlar va rasmiy manbalardan dasturlar 20
3-2-1 qoidasiga mos zaxiralash rejasi 25
Xavfsizlik rejasi: parollar, 2FA va fishingdan xabardorlik 20
Aniq va tartibli yozilgan matn 10

Yakuniy imtihon — Final assessment · quiz

Summary
A short exam covering the whole course. Pass mark: 70%.
Butun kursni qamrab oluvchi qisqa imtihon. Oʻtish balli: 70%.
Test: Kompyuter asoslari — yakuniy imtihon
Q1

Which piece of software manages the hardware and runs all other programs?

Qaysi dasturiy taʼminot apparatni boshqaradi va boshqa barcha dasturlarni ishga tushiradi?

Options
  • A web browser — A browser is an application that runs *on* the operating system.
  • ✓ The operating system
  • A driver — A driver helps the OS talk to one device; it does not run everything.
  • Veb-brauzer — Brauzer — operatsion tizim *ustida* ishlaydigan ilova.
  • ✓ Operatsion tizim
  • Drayver — Drayver operatsion tizimga bitta qurilma bilan aloqa qilishga yordam beradi; u hamma narsani ishga tushirmaydi.
Explanation

The operating system (Windows, macOS, Linux, Android, iOS) sits between hardware and apps.

Operatsion tizim (Windows, macOS, Linux, Android, iOS) apparat va ilovalar oʻrtasida turadi.

Q2

What is the extension of the file summer-trip.mp4? (type just the letters, without the dot)

yozgi-sayohat.mp4 faylining kengaytmasi qanday? (nuqtasiz, faqat harflarni yozing)

Options
  • ✓ mp4
  • ✓ .mp4
  • ✓ mp4
  • ✓ .mp4
Explanation

The extension is the part after the last dot: mp4, which marks a video file.

Kengaytma — oxirgi nuqtadan keyingi qism: mp4. U video faylni bildiradi.

Q3

In the path /Users/aziza/Documents/School/essay.docx, which folder directly contains the file?

/Users/aziza/Documents/Maktab/insho.docx yoʻlida faylni bevosita qaysi papka oʻz ichiga oladi?

Options
  • Users
  • Documents — Documents contains the School folder, which contains the file.
  • ✓ School
  • Users
  • Documents — Documents ichida Maktab papkasi bor, fayl esa Maktab papkasida joylashgan.
  • ✓ Maktab
Explanation

Read the path left to right — the last folder before the file name contains it.

Yoʻlni chapdan oʻngga oʻqing — fayl nomidan oldingi oxirgi papka uni oʻz ichiga oladi.

Q4

Which of these are examples of output?

Quyidagilardan qaysilari chiqarishga misol boʻladi?

Options
  • ✓ Music playing from speakers
  • ✓ A page coming out of a printer
  • Speaking into a microphone — That is input.
  • Scanning a document — A scanner sends information into the computer — input.
  • ✓ Karnaydan yangrayotgan musiqa
  • ✓ Printerdan chiqayotgan sahifa
  • Mikrofonga gapirish — Bu kiritish.
  • Hujjatni skanerlash — Skaner maʼlumotni kompyuterga yuboradi — bu kiritish.
Explanation

Output is information coming out of the computer — through the screen, speakers or a printer.

Chiqarish — kompyuterdan ekran, karnay yoki printer orqali chiqayotgan maʼlumot.

Q5

What is RAM used for?

RAM nima uchun ishlatiladi?

Options
  • ✓ Temporary working space for open apps and files
  • Keeping files when the computer is off — That is storage (SSD or HDD).
  • Connecting to Wi-Fi — Wi-Fi is handled by the network adapter.
  • ✓ Ochiq ilova va fayllar uchun vaqtinchalik ishchi maydon
  • Kompyuter oʻchiq boʻlganda fayllarni saqlash — Bu doimiy xotira (SSD yoki HDD) vazifasi.
  • Wi-Fi ga ulanish — Wi-Fi bilan tarmoq adapteri ishlaydi.
Explanation

RAM is fast, temporary working memory for whatever is open right now.

RAM — ayni paytda ochiq turgan narsalar uchun tezkor, vaqtinchalik ishchi xotira.

Q6

Which is the safest habit when installing software?

Dastur oʻrnatishda qaysi odat eng xavfsiz?

Options
  • ✓ Download from official sources and read each installer screen
  • Use the first download button you see
  • Install cracked versions of paid apps
  • ✓ Rasmiy manbalardan yuklab olish va oʻrnatuvchining har bir oynasini oʻqish
  • Koʻringan birinchi yuklab olish tugmasini bosish
  • Pullik ilovalarning buzilgan versiyalarini oʻrnatish
Explanation

Use official stores or the developer's website and read each installer screen.

Rasmiy doʻkonlar yoki ishlab chiqaruvchi saytidan foydalaning va oʻrnatuvchining har bir oynasini oʻqing.

Q7

True or false: following the 3-2-1 rule, one copy of your data should be kept off-site.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: 3-2-1 qoidasiga koʻra maʼlumotlaringizning bitta nusxasi boshqa joyda saqlanishi kerak.

Options
  • ✓ True
  • False
  • ✓ Toʻgʻri
  • Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

True — an off-site copy (for example, in the cloud) survives fire, flood or theft at home.

Toʻgʻri — boshqa joydagi nusxa (masalan, bulutda) uydagi yongʻin, suv toshqini yoki oʻgʻirlikdan omon qoladi.

Q8

What is two-factor authentication?

Ikki bosqichli autentifikatsiya nima?

Options
  • ✓ Signing in with a password plus a second proof, such as a phone code
  • Using two different passwords
  • Logging in on two devices
  • ✓ Parol va telefon kodi kabi ikkinchi dalil bilan tizimga kirish
  • Ikki xil paroldan foydalanish
  • Ikki qurilmada tizimga kirish
Explanation

Signing in with two different proofs, such as a password and a code from your phone.

Parol va telefoningizdagi kod kabi ikki xil dalil bilan tizimga kirish.

Q9

You get a message: “Your card is blocked — send us the SMS code to unlock it.” What do you do?

Sizga xabar keldi: “Kartangiz bloklandi — ochish uchun bizga SMS kodni yuboring.” Nima qilasiz?

Options
  • ✓ Ignore the message and contact the bank through official channels
  • Send the code quickly before the card is blocked — Sharing the code gives the scammer access.
  • Reply asking whether it is real — Replying confirms your number is active; use official channels instead.
  • ✓ Xabarga eʼtibor bermay, bank bilan rasmiy kanallar orqali bogʻlanaman
  • Karta bloklanmasidan oldin kodni tezda yuboraman — Kodni berish firibgarga hisobingizga kirish imkonini beradi.
  • Bu haqiqiymi, deb javob yozaman — Javob yozish raqamingiz faol ekanini tasdiqlaydi; buning oʻrniga rasmiy kanallardan foydalaning.
Explanation

Never share one-time codes. Contact the bank through its official app or the number on your card.

Bir martalik kodlarni hech qachon bermang. Bank bilan uning rasmiy ilovasi yoki kartangizdagi raqam orqali bogʻlaning.

Q10

Which of these are jobs of an operating system? Select all that apply.

Quyidagilardan qaysilari operatsion tizimning vazifasi? Mos keladigan barcha variantlarni belgilang.

Options
  • ✓ Managing hardware
  • ✓ Running and stopping programs
  • ✓ Controlling user accounts and permissions
  • Designing websites — Websites are designed by people using applications.
  • ✓ Apparatni boshqarish
  • ✓ Dasturlarni ishga tushirish va toʻxtatish
  • ✓ Foydalanuvchi hisoblari va ruxsatlarni nazorat qilish
  • Veb-saytlarni loyihalash — Veb-saytlarni odamlar ilovalar yordamida loyihalaydi.
Explanation

The OS manages hardware, runs programs, organises files and controls users and permissions.

Operatsion tizim apparatni boshqaradi, dasturlarni ishga tushiradi, fayllarni tartibga soladi hamda foydalanuvchilar va ruxsatlarni nazorat qiladi.

Dizayn tamoyillari: noldan boshlab

Design Principles from Zero

Subtitle
Why some designs feel effortless — and how to make yours feel that way
Nega baʼzi dizaynlar yengil va tabiiy tuyuladi — va oʻzingiznikini qanday shunday qilish mumkin
Description

Good design is not decoration; it is communication. This course teaches the principles behind clear, beautiful work — hierarchy, contrast, alignment, proximity, repetition and white space — so you can judge and improve any poster, slide, website or app.

Yaxshi dizayn — bezak emas, balki muloqot. Bu kurs aniq va chiroyli ishlar ortidagi tamoyillarni — ierarxiya, kontrast, tekislash, yaqinlik, takrorlash va boʻsh joyni oʻrgatadi. Shunda istalgan afisha, slayd, veb-sayt yoki ilovani baholay va yaxshilay olasiz.

Outcomes
  • Create a clear visual hierarchy
  • Use contrast, alignment and proximity deliberately
  • Critique a layout using precise vocabulary
  • Aniq vizual ierarxiya yarata olasiz
  • Kontrast, tekislash va yaqinlikdan ongli ravishda foydalanasiz
  • Maketni aniq atamalar yordamida tanqidiy tahlil qila olasiz
Prerequisites
  • None
  • Talab qilinmaydi

Modul: Dizaynerdek koʻrish (Seeing like a designer)

Module description
The principles behind every strong layout.
Har bir kuchli maket ortidagi tamoyillar.

Vizual ierarxiya — Visual hierarchy · article · 155 words

Summary
Guiding the eye from the most important element to the least.
Nigohni eng muhim elementdan eng kam muhimigacha yoʻnaltirish.
Lesson text

Order of importance

Visual hierarchy is the arrangement of elements so that people notice them in order of importance. In a well-designed poster you see the title first, then the key detail (date, price), and only then the small print — without trying.

Tools that create hierarchy

  • Size — bigger elements are seen first.
  • Weight — bold text stands out from regular text.
  • Colour and contrast — a bright accent on a calm background draws attention.
  • Position — people in left-to-right languages tend to start at the top left.
  • Space — an element surrounded by empty space feels important.

[!TIP] The squint test: squint at your design until it blurs. Whatever still stands out is what people will notice first. If that is not the most important thing, adjust the hierarchy.

One focal point

If everything is bold, nothing is. Choose one primary element per screen or page, support it with one or two secondary elements, and keep everything else quiet.

Muhimlik tartibi

Vizual ierarxiya — elementlarni odamlar ularni muhimlik tartibida payqaydigan qilib joylashtirish. Yaxshi ishlangan afishada avval sarlavhani, keyin asosiy tafsilotni (sana, narx) va faqat shundan soʻng mayda yozuvni koʻrasiz — hech qanday harakat qilmasdan.

Ierarxiya yaratuvchi vositalar

  • Oʻlcham — kattaroq elementlar birinchi koʻrinadi.
  • Qalinlik — qalin matn oddiy matn fonida ajralib turadi.
  • Rang va kontrast — vazmin fondagi yorqin urgʻu rang diqqatni tortadi.
  • Joylashuv — chapdan oʻngga yoziladigan tillarda (masalan, oʻzbek tilida) odamlar odatda yuqori chap burchakdan boshlaydi.
  • Boʻsh joy — atrofi boʻsh joy bilan oʻralgan element muhim tuyuladi.

[!TIP] Koʻzni qisib koʻrish sinovi: dizayningizga u xiralashguncha koʻzingizni qisib qarang. Shunda ham ajralib turgan narsani odamlar birinchi payqaydi. Agar bu eng muhim element boʻlmasa, ierarxiyani oʻzgartiring.

Bitta asosiy nuqta

Hamma narsa qalin boʻlsa, hech narsa ajralib turmaydi. Har bir ekran yoki sahifa uchun bitta asosiy elementni tanlang, uni bir-ikkita ikkinchi darajali element bilan qoʻllab-quvvatlang, qolgan hamma narsani esa vazmin qoldiring.

Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Kontrast, tekislash va yaqinlik — Contrast, alignment and proximity · article · 167 words

Summary
Three principles that instantly make layouts look intentional.
Maketlarni bir zumda puxta oʻylangan koʻrinishga keltiradigan uchta tamoyil.
Lesson text

Contrast

Contrast is difference: large vs small, dark vs light, bold vs thin. Contrast creates interest and tells people which things are different. Make differences obvious — two font sizes that are almost the same look like a mistake.

[!WARNING] Text must contrast strongly with its background to be readable. Accessibility guidelines (WCAG) recommend a contrast ratio of at least 4.5 : 1 for normal body text.

Alignment

Alignment means placing elements along shared invisible lines. Aligned elements look connected and organised; randomly placed elements look careless. Pick one alignment (for example, everything left-aligned to the same edge) and stick to it.

Proximity

Proximity means related items belong close together, and unrelated items are separated by space. A caption sits right under its photo; a new section starts after a larger gap.

Repetition and white space

  • Repetition — reuse the same colours, fonts and spacing so the design feels like one system.
  • White space — empty space is not wasted space. It gives content room to breathe and makes everything easier to read.

Kontrast

Kontrast — bu farq: katta va kichik, toʻq va och, qalin va ingichka. Kontrast qiziqish uygʻotadi va qaysi narsalar bir-biridan farq qilishini koʻrsatadi. Farqlarni yaqqol qiling — deyarli bir xil boʻlgan ikki shrift oʻlchami xatodek koʻrinadi.

[!WARNING] Matn oʻqilishi uchun u fon bilan kuchli kontrast hosil qilishi kerak. Foydalanish qulayligi boʻyicha tavsiyalar (WCAG) oddiy asosiy matn uchun kamida 4.5 : 1 kontrast nisbatini tavsiya etadi.

Tekislash

Tekislash (alignment) — elementlarni umumiy koʻrinmas chiziqlar boʻylab joylashtirish. Tekislangan elementlar bir-biriga bogʻliq va tartibli koʻrinadi; tartibsiz joylashganlari esa eʼtiborsizlikdek taassurot qoldiradi. Bitta tekislash usulini tanlang (masalan, hamma narsani bir xil chap chetga tekislang) va unga amal qiling.

Yaqinlik

Yaqinlik (proximity) — bir-biriga aloqador elementlar yonma-yon, aloqasizlari esa boʻsh joy bilan ajratilgan boʻlishi kerak. Rasm izohi rasmning ostida turadi; yangi boʻlim esa kattaroq boʻsh joydan keyin boshlanadi.

Takrorlash va boʻsh joy

  • Takrorlash — dizayn yagona tizimdek tuyulishi uchun bir xil ranglar, shriftlar va oraliqlardan qayta foydalaning.
  • Boʻsh joy — boʻsh joy behuda sarflangan joy emas. U kontentga “nafas olish” imkonini beradi va hamma narsani oʻqishni osonlashtiradi.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Bilimingizni tekshiring — Check your understanding · quiz

Summary
Design vocabulary in practice.
Dizayn atamalari amalda.
Test: Dizayn tamoyillari testi
Q1

A caption is placed far from its photo and close to an unrelated paragraph. Which principle is broken?

Rasm izohi rasmdan uzoqda va unga aloqasi yoʻq xatboshiga yaqin joylashtirilgan. Qaysi tamoyil buzilgan?

Options
  • ✓ Proximity
  • Repetition
  • Contrast
  • ✓ Yaqinlik
  • Takrorlash
  • Kontrast
Explanation

Proximity: related items should be grouped closely.

Yaqinlik: bir-biriga aloqador elementlar yaqin guruhlanishi kerak.

Q2

What does the squint test reveal?

Koʻzni qisib koʻrish sinovi nimani aniqlaydi?

Options
  • Spelling mistakes
  • ✓ Which elements dominate the hierarchy
  • Whether the colours are printable
  • Imlo xatolarini
  • ✓ Ierarxiyada qaysi elementlar ustunlik qilishini
  • Ranglarni chop etish mumkinmi-yoʻqligini
Explanation

Blurring removes detail so you see only the strongest elements — your real hierarchy.

Xiralashtirish mayda tafsilotlarni yoʻqotadi va faqat eng kuchli elementlar — haqiqiy ierarxiyangiz qoladi.

Q3

True or false: white space is wasted space and should be filled.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: boʻsh joy — behuda sarflangan joy, uni toʻldirish kerak.

Options
  • True — Empty space helps people focus.
  • ✓ False
  • Toʻgʻri — Boʻsh joy odamlarga diqqatni jamlashga yordam beradi.
  • ✓ Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

False — white space improves readability and focus.

Notoʻgʻri — boʻsh joy oʻqishni osonlashtiradi va diqqatni jamlaydi.

Modul: Rang va shrift (Colour and type)

Module description
Choose colours and typefaces that communicate clearly.
Fikrni aniq yetkazadigan ranglar va shriftlarni tanlang.

Rang asoslari — Colour basics · article · 432 words

Summary
What colour does, how to describe it and how to build a simple, readable palette.
Rang nima qiladi, uni qanday tavsiflash va oddiy, oʻqilishi oson palitrani qanday tuzish.
Lesson text

What colour does

Colour is often the first thing people notice. In this lesson you will learn to choose colours on purpose, so they guide attention instead of competing for it. In design, colour does four jobs:

  1. Attention — a bright accent pulls the eye.
  2. Mood — warm colours feel energetic, cool colours feel calm.
  3. Meaning — red often signals errors or urgency, green success.
  4. Grouping — things in the same colour look related.

Describing colour

Designers describe a colour with three properties:

  • Hue — the colour family: red, orange, blue.
  • Saturation — how intense it is, from grey to vivid.
  • Lightness — how light or dark it is.

On screens, colours are written as codes such as HEX (#1E3A8A) or RGB. Printers use CMYK inks instead, so a bright screen colour may look duller on paper.

Building a simple palette

A reliable starting point is the 60-30-10 rule:

Share Role Example
60% Dominant neutral background Off-white
30% Secondary colour for large areas Deep blue
10% Accent for the most important elements Warm orange

Other simple schemes are monochromatic (shades of one hue) and complementary (two hues opposite each other on the colour wheel, such as blue and orange).

Colour and readable text

Text must contrast strongly with its background. The WCAG accessibility guidelines ask for a contrast ratio of at least 4.5 : 1 for normal text and 3 : 1 for large text. Free online contrast checkers calculate the ratio from two colour codes.

[!WARNING] Never rely on colour alone to carry meaning. About 1 in 12 men and 1 in 200 women have some form of colour blindness. Add a label, an icon or a pattern: “Error: password too short” in red with a warning icon, not just a red border.

Colour and culture

Colour meanings change between cultures and situations. White is the colour of weddings in many places but of mourning in others; red can mean danger or celebration. Think about who will see your design.

Try it

Take a photo of a place you love — for example, the blue tiles of Samarkand. Pick five colours from it: one dominant, one secondary, one accent and two neutrals for text and background. Check that your text colour has at least 4.5 : 1 contrast with your background.

Common mistakes

  • Using many bright colours at once, so nothing stands out.
  • Light grey text on white, which looks elegant but is hard to read.
  • Showing status with colour alone.

Key terms

  • Hue, saturation, lightness — the colour family, its intensity and its brightness.
  • HEX / RGB / CMYK — colour codes for screens and inks for print.
  • Palette — the set of colours used in a design.
  • Contrast ratio — how different two colours are in lightness, used to judge readability.

Rang nima qiladi

Koʻpincha odamlar birinchi boʻlib rangni payqaydi. Bu darsda ranglarni ongli tanlashni oʻrganasiz — shunda ular diqqat uchun bir-biri bilan bellashmay, uni yoʻnaltiradi. Dizaynda rang toʻrtta vazifani bajaradi:

  1. Diqqat — yorqin urgʻu rang nigohni tortadi.
  2. Kayfiyat — iliq ranglar gʻayratli, sovuq ranglar xotirjam tuyuladi.
  3. Maʼno — qizil koʻpincha xato yoki shoshilinchlikni, yashil esa muvaffaqiyatni bildiradi.
  4. Guruhlash — bir xil rangdagi narsalar bir-biriga bogʻliqdek koʻrinadi.

Rangni tavsiflash

Dizaynerlar rangni uchta xususiyat bilan tavsiflaydi:

  • Tus (hue) — rang oilasi: qizil, toʻq sariq, koʻk.
  • Toʻyinganlik (saturation) — rangning kulrangdan yorqingacha boʻlgan jadalligi.
  • Yorqinlik (lightness) — rangning qanchalik och yoki toʻq ekani.

Ekranlarda ranglar HEX (#1E3A8A) yoki RGB kabi kodlar bilan yoziladi. Printerlar esa CMYK boʻyoqlaridan foydalanadi, shuning uchun ekrandagi yorqin rang qogʻozda xiraroq koʻrinishi mumkin.

Oddiy palitra tuzish

Ishonchli boshlangʻich nuqta — 60-30-10 qoidasi:

Ulush Vazifasi Misol
60% Asosiy neytral fon Oqish rang
30% Katta maydonlar uchun ikkinchi rang Toʻq koʻk
10% Eng muhim elementlar uchun urgʻu rang Iliq toʻq sariq

Boshqa oddiy sxemalar — monoxrom (bitta tusning turli soyalari) va komplementar (rang doirasida bir-biriga qarama-qarshi joylashgan ikki tus, masalan, koʻk va toʻq sariq).

Rang va oʻqilishi oson matn

Matn fon bilan kuchli kontrast hosil qilishi kerak. WCAG foydalanish qulayligi tavsiyalari oddiy matn uchun kamida 4.5 : 1, katta matn uchun esa 3 : 1 kontrast nisbatini talab qiladi. Bepul onlayn kontrast tekshirgichlar ikki rang kodi asosida bu nisbatni hisoblab beradi.

[!WARNING] Maʼnoni hech qachon faqat rang orqali yetkazmang. Taxminan har 12 erkakdan biri va har 200 ayoldan biri rangni farqlashda qandaydir qiyinchilikka ega. Yorliq, ikonka yoki naqsh qoʻshing: faqat qizil chegara emas, ogohlantirish ikonkasi bilan qizil rangda yozilgan “Xato: parol juda qisqa” matni.

Rang va madaniyat

Ranglarning maʼnosi madaniyat va vaziyatga qarab oʻzgaradi. Koʻp joylarda oq rang toʻy rangi, boshqa joylarda esa motam rangi; qizil xavfni ham, bayramni ham bildirishi mumkin. Dizayningizni kim koʻrishini oʻylab koʻring.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Sevgan joyingizni, masalan, Samarqandning moviy koshinlarini suratga oling. Undan beshta rang tanlang: bitta asosiy, bitta ikkinchi darajali, bitta urgʻu rang va matn hamda fon uchun ikkita neytral rang. Matn rangingiz fon bilan kamida 4.5 : 1 kontrast hosil qilishini tekshiring.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Bir vaqtda koʻplab yorqin ranglardan foydalanish — natijada hech narsa ajralib turmaydi.
  • Oq fondagi och kulrang matn — nafis koʻrinadi, ammo oʻqish qiyin.
  • Holatni faqat rang bilan koʻrsatish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Tus, toʻyinganlik, yorqinlik — rang oilasi, uning jadalligi va ochligi.
  • HEX / RGB / CMYK — ekranlar uchun rang kodlari va chop etish uchun boʻyoqlar.
  • Palitra — dizaynda ishlatiladigan ranglar toʻplami.
  • Kontrast nisbati — ikki rangning yorqinlik boʻyicha farqi; oʻqilishini baholash uchun ishlatiladi.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Shrift asoslari — Type basics · article · 402 words

Summary
Choose, size and pair typefaces for readable text in Latin and Cyrillic.
Lotin va kirill yozuvidagi oʻqilishi oson matn uchun shriftlarni tanlang, oʻlchamini belgilang va juftlang.
Lesson text

Why type matters

Most designs are mostly text. Good typography is invisible: people read easily and understand the message. In this lesson you will choose, size and combine typefaces with confidence.

Typeface or font?

A typeface is the design of the letters, such as Inter or Georgia. A font is one specific style of it, such as Inter Bold 16 px. In everyday speech the words are used interchangeably.

Main families:

  • Serif — small strokes at the ends of letters; traditional and bookish.
  • Sans-serif — no serifs; clean and common on screens.
  • Display — decorative, for large headlines only.
  • Monospace — every character the same width; used for code.

Readable text

Setting Good starting point
Body size on screens 16 px or larger
Line height 1.4–1.6 times the font size
Line length About 45–75 characters per line
Number of typefaces One or two per design

Long lines make the eye lose its place; tight line height makes text feel cramped.

Hierarchy with type

Make levels clearly different. A simple scale such as 32 / 24 / 16 px, combined with weight (bold for headings, regular for body), is enough for most layouts. If two sizes are almost the same, the difference looks like a mistake.

Type for Uzbek and Russian

Before choosing a typeface, check that it contains every character you need:

  • Uzbek Latin: oʻ and gʻ use the ʻ mark, and the tutuq belgisi ʼ appears in words such as maʼlumot.
  • Uzbek and Russian Cyrillic: letters such as Ў Қ Ғ Ҳ and Ё Ж Щ Ы.

Type a test line such as Oʻzbekiston, maʼlumot — Ўзбекистон, Ғалаба and look for missing or mismatched letters.

[!TIP] Many free typefaces on Google Fonts are released under the SIL Open Font License, which allows commercial use. For any other typeface, read its licence before using it in a paid project.

Try it

Set the same short paragraph in a serif and a sans-serif typeface at 16 px with a line height of 1.5. Compare which is easier to read on your phone. Then check both with the Uzbek and Cyrillic test line.

Common mistakes

  • Using four or five typefaces in one design.
  • Body text that is too small or too long per line.
  • Choosing a typeface that lacks oʻ, gʻ or Cyrillic letters, so the computer substitutes a different font.

Key terms

  • Typeface / font — a letter design / one style and size of it.
  • Serif / sans-serif — with or without small strokes at letter ends.
  • Line height (leading) — the vertical space between lines.
  • Type scale — a set of sizes used consistently for hierarchy.

Shrift nega muhim

Aksariyat dizaynlarning katta qismi matndan iborat. Yaxshi tipografiya koʻzga tashlanmaydi: odamlar matnni oson oʻqiydi va fikrni tushunadi. Bu darsda shriftlarni ishonch bilan tanlash, oʻlchamini belgilash va birlashtirishni oʻrganasiz.

Garnitura yoki shrift?

Garnitura (typeface) — harflar dizayni, masalan, Inter yoki Georgia. Shrift (font) esa uning muayyan uslubi, masalan, Inter Bold 16 px. Kundalik nutqda bu soʻzlar bir-birining oʻrnida ishlatiladi.

Asosiy oilalar:

  • Serifli — harflar uchida kichik chiziqchalar bor; anʼanaviy va kitobiy.
  • Serifsiz (sans-serif) — serifsiz; toza va ekranlarda keng tarqalgan.
  • Dekorativ (display) — bezakli, faqat katta sarlavhalar uchun.
  • Monospace — har bir belgi bir xil kenglikda; kod uchun ishlatiladi.

Oʻqilishi oson matn

Sozlama Yaxshi boshlangʻich qiymat
Ekranlardagi asosiy matn oʻlchami 16 px yoki undan katta
Qatorlar oraligʻi Shrift oʻlchamidan 1.4–1.6 marta
Qator uzunligi Har qatorda taxminan 45–75 belgi
Shriftlar soni Har bir dizaynda bitta yoki ikkita

Uzun qatorlarda koʻz joyini yoʻqotadi; qatorlar oraligʻi tor boʻlsa, matn siqilgandek tuyuladi.

Shrift bilan ierarxiya

Darajalarni bir-biridan aniq farqlang. 32 / 24 / 16 px kabi oddiy shkala va qalinlik (sarlavhalar uchun qalin, asosiy matn uchun oddiy) aksariyat maketlar uchun yetarli. Agar ikki oʻlcham deyarli bir xil boʻlsa, farq xatodek koʻrinadi.

Oʻzbek va rus tillari uchun shrift

Shriftni tanlashdan oldin unda sizga kerakli barcha belgilar borligini tekshiring:

  • Oʻzbek lotin yozuvi: oʻ va gʻ ʻ belgisidan foydalanadi, tutuq belgisi ʼ esa maʼlumot kabi soʻzlarda uchraydi.
  • Oʻzbek va rus kirill yozuvi: Ў Қ Ғ Ҳ hamda Ё Ж Щ Ы kabi harflar.

Oʻzbekiston, maʼlumot — Ўзбекистон, Ғалаба kabi sinov qatorini yozing va yetishmayotgan yoki mos kelmayotgan harflarni qidiring.

[!TIP] Google Fonts’dagi koʻplab bepul shriftlar tijoriy foydalanishga ruxsat beruvchi SIL Open Font License asosida tarqatiladi. Boshqa har qanday shriftdan pullik loyihada foydalanishdan oldin uning litsenziyasini oʻqing.

Mashq

Bitta qisqa xatboshini serifli va serifsiz shriftda 16 px oʻlchamda, 1.5 qatorlar oraligʻi bilan yozing. Telefoningizda qaysi birini oʻqish osonroq ekanini solishtiring. Keyin ikkalasini ham oʻzbekcha va kirill sinov qatori bilan tekshiring.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Bitta dizaynda toʻrt-besh xil shriftdan foydalanish.
  • Asosiy matnning juda kichik yoki qatorlarning juda uzun boʻlishi.
  • oʻ, gʻ yoki kirill harflari boʻlmagan shriftni tanlash — natijada kompyuter ularni boshqa shrift bilan almashtiradi.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Garnitura / shrift — harflar dizayni / uning bitta uslubi va oʻlchami.
  • Serifli / serifsiz — harf uchlarida kichik chiziqchalari bor yoki yoʻq.
  • Qatorlar oraligʻi (leading) — qatorlar orasidagi vertikal masofa.
  • Shrift shkalasi — ierarxiya uchun izchil ishlatiladigan oʻlchamlar toʻplami.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Rang va shrift: bilimni tekshirish — Colour and type: check · quiz

Summary
Five questions on colour and typography.
Rang va tipografiya boʻyicha beshta savol.
Test: “Rang va shrift” moduli boʻyicha test
Q1

In the 60-30-10 rule, what is the 10% used for?

60-30-10 qoidasida 10% nima uchun ishlatiladi?

Options
  • ✓ An accent for the most important elements
  • The background
  • Body text
  • ✓ Eng muhim elementlar uchun urgʻu rang
  • Fon
  • Asosiy matn
Explanation

The small accent share highlights the most important elements.

Kichik urgʻu ulushi eng muhim elementlarni ajratib koʻrsatadi.

Q2

What minimum contrast ratio does WCAG ask for normal body text?

WCAG oddiy asosiy matn uchun qanday eng kam kontrast nisbatini talab qiladi?

Options
  • ✓ 4.5 : 1
  • 2 : 1
  • 1 : 1
  • ✓ 4.5 : 1
  • 2 : 1
  • 1 : 1
Explanation

Normal text needs at least 4.5 : 1; large text at least 3 : 1.

Oddiy matn uchun kamida 4.5 : 1, katta matn uchun kamida 3 : 1.

Q3

A form shows errors only with a red border. What is the problem?

Forma xatolarni faqat qizil chegara bilan koʻrsatadi. Muammo nimada?

Options
  • ✓ Meaning is carried by colour alone
  • Red is not allowed in forms
  • Borders slow the page down
  • ✓ Maʼno faqat rang orqali yetkaziladi
  • Formalarda qizil rang taqiqlangan
  • Chegaralar sahifani sekinlashtiradi
Explanation

People with colour blindness may not see it; add text or an icon.

Rangni farqlashda qiynaladigan odamlar uni koʻrmasligi mumkin; matn yoki ikonka qoʻshing.

Q4

Which line length is most comfortable for reading body text?

Asosiy matnni oʻqish uchun qanday qator uzunligi eng qulay?

Options
  • ✓ About 45–75 characters
  • About 150 characters
  • About 10 characters
  • ✓ Taxminan 45–75 belgi
  • Taxminan 150 belgi
  • Taxminan 10 belgi
Explanation

About 45–75 characters per line is easiest to follow.

Har qatorda taxminan 45–75 belgini kuzatish eng oson.

Q5

Before using a typeface for an Uzbek poster, what should you check first?

Oʻzbekcha afisha uchun shriftdan foydalanishdan oldin birinchi navbatda nimani tekshirish kerak?

Options
  • ✓ That it has every letter you need, such as oʻ and gʻ
  • That it is the newest typeface
  • That it has a decorative style
  • ✓ Unda oʻ va gʻ kabi sizga kerakli barcha harflar borligini
  • U eng yangi shrift ekanini
  • Uning bezakli uslubi borligini
Explanation

It must contain oʻ, gʻ, ʼ (and Cyrillic letters if needed).

Unda oʻ, gʻ, ʼ (kerak boʻlsa, kirill harflari ham) boʻlishi shart.

Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Modul: Maket va tanqidiy tahlil (Layout and critique)

Module description
Arrange elements on a grid, judge designs with precise words and make your first poster.
Elementlarni toʻrga joylashtiring, dizaynlarni aniq atamalar bilan baholang va birinchi afishangizni yarating.

Toʻrlar va maket — Grids and layout · article · 420 words

Summary
Columns, gutters and spacing systems that make layouts look intentional.
Maketlarni puxta oʻylangan koʻrinishga keltiradigan ustunlar, oraliqlar va masofa tizimlari.
Lesson text

The invisible structure

Behind most good layouts is a grid: invisible lines that decide where things go. In this lesson you will place content on a grid and use consistent spacing, so your designs look organised even before you add colour.

A grid has three parts:

  • Columns — vertical areas that hold content.
  • Gutters — the gaps between columns.
  • Margins — the space between the content and the edge of the page or screen.

Common grids

Grid Where it is used
Single column Articles and simple mobile screens
4 columns Phone layouts
12 columns Websites — 12 divides into 2, 3, 4 and 6 equal parts
Modular grid Columns and rows, for galleries and dashboards

A spacing system

Pick spacing values from a small set and reuse them. Many designers use multiples of 8: 8, 16, 24, 32, 48 px. When every gap comes from the same set, the layout feels calm and consistent — this is the principle of repetition at work.

How people scan a page

  • On text-heavy pages, people often read in an F-pattern: across the top, then down the left side.
  • On simple pages such as posters, the eye tends to follow a Z-pattern: top left, top right, diagonally down, bottom right.

Place the most important elements where the eye naturally lands.

Balance

  • Symmetrical layouts feel formal and stable.
  • Asymmetrical layouts feel dynamic; a large element on one side is balanced by several small ones or by white space on the other.

[!TIP] Turn on the grid or layout guides in your design tool (Figma, Canva and others have them) and snap elements to the columns. Alignment becomes automatic.

A worked example

A poster on a 6-column grid with 24 px margins:

  1. The title spans all 6 columns at the top.
  2. A large photo spans columns 1–4.
  3. Date and place sit in columns 5–6, aligned with the top of the photo.
  4. Small print sits along the bottom, aligned to column 1.

Every element starts and ends on a column line, so the poster looks deliberate.

Try it

Draw a 6-column grid (on paper or in a design tool) and lay out a poster for a school or community event using only rectangles for text and images. Use spacing values from the 8-point set only.

Common mistakes

  • Placing elements by eye, so edges almost — but not quite — line up.
  • Using a different gap size every time.
  • Filling every column, leaving no white space.

Key terms

  • Grid — a set of columns (and sometimes rows) used to place content.
  • Gutter — the space between columns.
  • Margin — the space around the content.
  • Spacing system — a fixed set of spacing values, such as multiples of 8.

Koʻrinmas tuzilma

Aksariyat yaxshi maketlar ortida toʻr (grid) turadi: narsalar qayerga joylashishini belgilaydigan koʻrinmas chiziqlar. Bu darsda kontentni toʻrga joylashtirasiz va izchil masofalardan foydalanasiz — shunda dizaynlaringiz rang qoʻshilishidan oldin ham tartibli koʻrinadi.

Toʻr uch qismdan iborat:

  • Ustunlar — kontent joylashadigan vertikal maydonlar.
  • Oraliqlar (gutters) — ustunlar orasidagi boʻshliqlar.
  • Chetlar (margins) — kontent bilan sahifa yoki ekran cheti orasidagi masofa.

Keng tarqalgan toʻrlar

Toʻr Qayerda ishlatiladi
Bir ustunli Maqolalar va oddiy mobil ekranlar
4 ustunli Telefon maketlari
12 ustunli Veb-saytlar — 12 soni 2, 3, 4 va 6 teng qismga boʻlinadi
Modulli toʻr Ustun va qatorlar; galereyalar va boshqaruv panellari uchun

Masofa tizimi

Masofa qiymatlarini kichik toʻplamdan tanlang va ulardan qayta foydalaning. Koʻp dizaynerlar 8 ga karrali sonlardan foydalanadi: 8, 16, 24, 32, 48 px. Har bir boʻshliq bir xil toʻplamdan olinganda maket xotirjam va izchil tuyuladi — bu takrorlash tamoyilining amaldagi koʻrinishi.

Odamlar sahifani qanday koʻzdan kechiradi

  • Matni koʻp sahifalarda odamlar koʻpincha F-shaklida oʻqiydi: tepada chapdan oʻngga, soʻngra chap tomon boʻylab pastga.
  • Afisha kabi oddiy sahifalarda nigoh Z-shaklida harakatlanishga moyil: yuqori chap, yuqori oʻng, diagonal boʻylab pastga, pastki oʻng.

Eng muhim elementlarni nigoh tabiiy ravishda tushadigan joylarga qoʻying.

Muvozanat

  • Simmetrik maketlar rasmiy va barqaror tuyuladi.
  • Asimmetrik maketlar dinamik tuyuladi; bir tomondagi katta element boshqa tomondagi bir nechta kichik element yoki boʻsh joy bilan muvozanatlanadi.

[!TIP] Dizayn dasturingizda (Figma, Canva va boshqalarda bor) toʻr yoki maket yoʻnaltiruvchilarini yoqing va elementlarni ustunlarga yopishtiring. Tekislash avtomatik boʻladi.

Ishlangan misol

24 px chetli 6 ustunli toʻrdagi afisha:

  1. Sarlavha yuqorida barcha 6 ta ustunni egallaydi.
  2. Katta fotosurat 1–4-ustunlarni egallaydi.
  3. Sana va joy 5–6-ustunlarda, fotosuratning yuqori qirrasi bilan tekislangan holda joylashadi.
  4. Mayda yozuv pastda, 1-ustunga tekislangan holda joylashadi.

Har bir element ustun chizigʻida boshlanib, ustun chizigʻida tugaydi, shuning uchun afisha puxta oʻylangan koʻrinadi.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

6 ustunli toʻr chizing (qogʻozda yoki dizayn dasturida) va matn hamda rasmlar uchun faqat toʻrtburchaklardan foydalanib, maktab yoki mahalla tadbiri uchun afisha maketini tuzing. Faqat 8 lik toʻplamdagi masofa qiymatlaridan foydalaning.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Elementlarni koʻz bilan joylashtirish — chetlar deyarli, lekin toʻliq emas, bir chiziqqa tushadi.
  • Har safar har xil boʻshliq oʻlchamidan foydalanish.
  • Har bir ustunni toʻldirib, boʻsh joy qoldirmaslik.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Toʻr (grid) — kontentni joylashtirish uchun ishlatiladigan ustunlar (baʼzan qatorlar ham) toʻplami.
  • Oraliq (gutter) — ustunlar orasidagi masofa.
  • Chet (margin) — kontent atrofidagi masofa.
  • Masofa tizimi — 8 ga karrali sonlar kabi qatʼiy masofa qiymatlari toʻplami.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Tanqidiy fikr bildirish va qabul qilish — Giving and receiving critique · article · 379 words

Summary
Judge designs with precise vocabulary and turn feedback into better work.
Dizaynlarni aniq atamalar bilan baholang va fikr-mulohazani yaxshiroq ishga aylantiring.
Lesson text

Why critique matters

Designers improve fastest by showing work early and discussing it. A critique is a structured conversation about whether a design achieves its goal. In this lesson you will learn to give useful critique and to receive it calmly.

A simple structure for giving critique

  1. Start with the goal. “This poster should make students sign up for the robotics club.”
  2. Say what works, and why. “The large photo of the robot grabs attention immediately.”
  3. Name problems with a principle. “The date and the sign-up link are the same size as the small print, so the hierarchy hides them.”
  4. Suggest a direction, not an order. “What if the date and the link were larger and in the accent colour?”

[!NOTE] Critique is about the work, not the person. “The text is hard to read on this background” helps; “You have bad taste” does not.

Words that make feedback precise

Use the vocabulary from this course instead of “I like it” or “it feels off”: hierarchy, contrast, alignment, proximity, repetition, white space, palette, type scale, grid.

A critique checklist

Question Principle
What do I see first, second and third? Hierarchy
Is the text easy to read? Contrast, type
Do the edges line up? Alignment, grid
Are related things grouped? Proximity
Are colours, fonts and spacing consistent? Repetition
Does the design have room to breathe? White space
Can everyone use it? Accessibility

Receiving critique

  • Listen to the end before answering, and take notes.
  • Ask questions: “Which part did you notice first?”
  • Do not defend every choice — explain the goal, then decide later what to change.
  • You do not have to accept every suggestion, but every comment tells you how someone experienced your design.

Try it

Find three real designs: a poster in your street, an app screen and a website. Critique each one in four sentences using the structure above and at least two words from the checklist.

Common mistakes

  • Feedback without reasons: “Make it pop.”
  • Rewriting someone's design for them instead of naming the problem.
  • Taking critique personally and stopping listening.

Key terms

  • Critique — a structured discussion of how well a design meets its goal.
  • Actionable feedback — feedback that points to a specific change.
  • Design rationale — the reasons behind your design choices.
  • Iteration — improving a design in repeated rounds.

Tanqidiy tahlil nega muhim

Dizaynerlar ishini erta koʻrsatib, uni muhokama qilganda eng tez oʻsadi. Tanqidiy tahlil (critique) — dizayn oʻz maqsadiga erishgan-erishmaganligi haqidagi tuzilmali suhbat. Bu darsda foydali tanqidiy fikr bildirishni va uni xotirjam qabul qilishni oʻrganasiz.

Tanqidiy fikr bildirishning oddiy tuzilmasi

  1. Maqsaddan boshlang. “Bu afisha talabalarni robototexnika toʻgaragiga yozilishga undashi kerak.”
  2. Nima ishlayotganini va nega ishlayotganini ayting. “Robotning katta surati darhol eʼtiborni tortadi.”
  3. Muammolarni tamoyil bilan nomlang. “Sana va roʻyxatdan oʻtish havolasi mayda yozuv bilan bir xil oʻlchamda, shuning uchun ierarxiya ularni yashirib qoʻyadi.”
  4. Buyruq emas, yoʻnalish taklif qiling. “Sana va havola kattaroq va urgʻu rangda boʻlsa-chi?”

[!NOTE] Tanqidiy tahlil insonga emas, ishga qaratiladi. “Bu fonda matnni oʻqish qiyin” degan gap yordam beradi; “Didingiz yoʻq” degani esa yordam bermaydi.

Fikr-mulohazani aniq qiladigan soʻzlar

“Menga yoqdi” yoki “nimadir notoʻgʻri” oʻrniga ushbu kursdagi atamalardan foydalaning: ierarxiya, kontrast, tekislash, yaqinlik, takrorlash, boʻsh joy, palitra, shrift shkalasi, toʻr.

Tanqidiy tahlil uchun nazorat roʻyxati

Savol Tamoyil
Birinchi, ikkinchi va uchinchi boʻlib nimani koʻraman? Ierarxiya
Matnni oʻqish osonmi? Kontrast, shrift
Chetlar bir chiziqqa tushyaptimi? Tekislash, toʻr
Bir-biriga aloqador narsalar guruhlanganmi? Yaqinlik
Ranglar, shriftlar va masofalar izchilmi? Takrorlash
Dizaynda “nafas olish” uchun joy bormi? Boʻsh joy
Undan hamma foydalana oladimi? Foydalanish qulayligi

Tanqidiy fikrni qabul qilish

  • Javob berishdan oldin oxirigacha tinglang va qayd qilib boring.
  • Savol bering: “Birinchi boʻlib qaysi qismni payqadingiz?”
  • Har bir tanlovingizni himoya qilmang — maqsadni tushuntiring, nimani oʻzgartirishni esa keyinroq hal qiling.
  • Har bir taklifni qabul qilishingiz shart emas, ammo har bir izoh kimdir dizayningizni qanday his qilganini aytadi.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Uchta haqiqiy dizayn toping: koʻchangizdagi afisha, ilova ekrani va veb-sayt. Har birini yuqoridagi tuzilma va nazorat roʻyxatidagi kamida ikkita soʻz yordamida toʻrt gap bilan tahlil qiling.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Sababsiz fikr-mulohaza: “Yorqinroq qiling.”
  • Muammoni nomlash oʻrniga birovning dizaynini uning oʻrniga qayta chizish.
  • Tanqidiy fikrni shaxsiy qabul qilib, tinglashni toʻxtatish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Tanqidiy tahlil (critique) — dizayn oʻz maqsadiga qanchalik erishganini tuzilmali muhokama qilish.
  • Amaliy fikr-mulohaza — muayyan oʻzgarishni koʻrsatadigan fikr-mulohaza.
  • Dizayn asoslanishi (rationale) — dizayn tanlovlaringiz ortidagi sabablar.
  • Iteratsiya — dizaynni takroriy bosqichlarda yaxshilash.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Loyiha: tadbir afishasi — Project: an event poster · project · 171 words

Summary
Design a poster for a real or imagined local event and explain your choices.
Haqiqiy yoki toʻqima mahalliy tadbir uchun afisha yarating va tanlovlaringizni tushuntiring.
Lesson text

Your project

Design a poster for an event — a concert, a school fair, a sports match, a book club or a startup meetup. Use a free tool such as Figma or Canva, and apply everything in this course.

Requirements

  1. A clear hierarchy: the event name first, then the date, time and place, then the details.
  2. A palette of three to five colours following the 60-30-10 idea, with text contrast of at least 4.5 : 1.
  3. At most two typefaces, with a clear type scale. If the poster includes Uzbek or Russian text, the typeface must support every letter.
  4. A grid: elements align to columns and use consistent spacing.
  5. Enough white space for the poster to feel calm.
  6. A short rationale (about 150 words) explaining your choices with the course vocabulary.

How to submit

Upload the poster as a PNG or PDF and paste your rationale in the text box, or share a link to your Figma or Canva file.

[!NOTE] A reviewer critiques your poster against the rubric. Use the feedback, improve the design and resubmit.

Loyihangiz

Tadbir uchun afisha yarating — konsert, maktab yarmarkasi, sport musobaqasi, kitobxonlar klubi yoki startap uchrashuvi. Figma yoki Canva kabi bepul dasturdan foydalaning va ushbu kursdagi hamma narsani qoʻllang.

Talablar

  1. Aniq ierarxiya: avval tadbir nomi, keyin sana, vaqt va joy, soʻngra tafsilotlar.
  2. 60-30-10 gʻoyasiga amal qilgan, matn kontrasti kamida 4.5 : 1 boʻlgan uchtadan beshtagacha rangli palitra.
  3. Aniq shrift shkalasiga ega koʻpi bilan ikkita shrift. Agar afishada oʻzbekcha yoki ruscha matn boʻlsa, shrift barcha harflarni qoʻllab-quvvatlashi shart.
  4. Toʻr: elementlar ustunlarga tekislangan va izchil masofalardan foydalanilgan.
  5. Afisha xotirjam tuyulishi uchun yetarli boʻsh joy.
  6. Tanlovlaringizni kurs atamalari bilan tushuntiradigan qisqa asoslash (taxminan 150 soʻz).

Qanday topshiriladi

Afishani PNG yoki PDF sifatida yuklang va asoslash matnini matn maydoniga joylang yoki Figma yoxud Canva faylingizga havola ulashing.

[!NOTE] Tekshiruvchi afishangizni baholash mezonlari asosida tahlil qiladi. Fikr-mulohazadan foydalanib, dizaynni yaxshilang va qayta topshiring.

Assignment brief

Design an event poster with clear hierarchy, an accessible palette, consistent type and a grid, plus a short rationale.

Aniq ierarxiya, hamma uchun qulay palitra, izchil shrift va toʻrga ega tadbir afishasini qisqa asoslash bilan birga yarating.

Rubric
Criterion Points
Hierarchy: the most important information is seen first 25
Palette and contrast (60-30-10 idea, 4.5 : 1 text contrast) 20
Typography: two or fewer typefaces, clear scale, full letter support 20
Grid, alignment and consistent spacing 20
Rationale uses precise design vocabulary 15
Mezon Ball
Ierarxiya: eng muhim maʼlumot birinchi koʻrinadi 25
Palitra va kontrast (60-30-10 gʻoyasi, matn kontrasti 4.5 : 1) 20
Tipografiya: ikkitadan koʻp boʻlmagan shrift, aniq shkala, barcha harflar qoʻllab-quvvatlanadi 20
Toʻr, tekislash va izchil masofalar 20
Asoslashda aniq dizayn atamalaridan foydalanilgan 15

Yakuniy imtihon — Final assessment · quiz

Summary
A short exam covering the whole course. Pass mark: 70%.
Butun kursni qamrab oluvchi qisqa imtihon. Oʻtish balli: 70%.
Test: Dizayn tamoyillari: noldan boshlab — yakuniy imtihon
Q1

What is visual hierarchy?

Vizual ierarxiya nima?

Options
  • ✓ Arranging elements so people see them in order of importance
  • Using as many colours as possible
  • Centring everything
  • ✓ Elementlarni odamlar ularni muhimlik tartibida koʻradigan qilib joylashtirish
  • Iloji boricha koʻp rangdan foydalanish
  • Hamma narsani markazga joylash
Explanation

Arranging elements so people notice them in order of importance.

Elementlarni odamlar ularni muhimlik tartibida payqaydigan qilib joylashtirish.

Q2

A caption is far from its photo. Which principle is broken?

Rasm izohi rasmdan uzoqda joylashgan. Qaysi tamoyil buzilgan?

Options
  • ✓ Proximity
  • Repetition
  • Contrast
  • ✓ Yaqinlik
  • Takrorlash
  • Kontrast
Explanation

Proximity: related items belong close together.

Yaqinlik: bir-biriga aloqador elementlar yaqin joylashishi kerak.

Q3

Which palette approach keeps one accent for the most important elements?

Qaysi palitra usuli eng muhim elementlar uchun bitta urgʻu rangni ajratib qoʻyadi?

Options
  • ✓ 60-30-10
  • Use every colour equally
  • No neutral colours
  • ✓ 60-30-10
  • Barcha ranglardan teng foydalanish
  • Neytral ranglarsiz
Explanation

The 60-30-10 rule reserves about 10% for an accent.

60-30-10 qoidasi taxminan 10% ni urgʻu rang uchun ajratadi.

Q4

True or false: light grey text on a white background is always fine because it looks elegant.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: oq fondagi och kulrang matn nafis koʻrinishi sababli har doim maqbul.

Options
  • True
  • ✓ False
  • Toʻgʻri
  • ✓ Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

False — low contrast makes text hard to read; check for at least 4.5 : 1.

Notoʻgʻri — past kontrast matnni oʻqishni qiyinlashtiradi; kamida 4.5 : 1 ekanini tekshiring.

Q5

How many typefaces should a typical design use?

Odatiy dizaynda nechta shriftdan foydalanish kerak?

Options
  • ✓ One or two
  • At least four
  • A different one for every line
  • ✓ Bitta yoki ikkita
  • Kamida toʻrtta
  • Har bir qator uchun alohida
Explanation

One or two keeps a design consistent and readable.

Bitta yoki ikkita shrift dizaynni izchil va oʻqilishi oson qiladi.

Q6

Why do many web layouts use 12 columns?

Nega koʻplab veb-maketlar 12 ustunli toʻrdan foydalanadi?

Options
  • ✓ 12 divides into 2, 3, 4 and 6 equal parts
  • Browsers require it
  • It looks like a clock
  • ✓ 12 soni 2, 3, 4 va 6 teng qismga boʻlinadi
  • Brauzerlar buni talab qiladi
  • U soatga oʻxshaydi
Explanation

12 divides evenly into 2, 3, 4 and 6 parts, which makes flexible layouts easy.

12 soni 2, 3, 4 va 6 qismga teng boʻlinadi, bu esa moslashuvchan maketlar yaratishni osonlashtiradi.

Q7

Which is the most useful piece of critique?

Qaysi tanqidiy fikr eng foydali?

Options
  • ✓ “The date is as small as the fine print, so the hierarchy hides it — could it be larger?”
  • “Make it pop.”
  • “I don't like it.”
  • ✓ “Sana mayda yozuv bilan bir xil kichik, shuning uchun ierarxiya uni yashiradi — kattaroq qilsa boʻladimi?”
  • “Yorqinroq qiling.”
  • “Menga yoqmadi.”
Explanation

It names the problem, the principle and a possible direction.

U muammoni, tamoyilni va mumkin boʻlgan yoʻnalishni nomlaydi.

Q8

Which of these help a design work for people with colour blindness? Select all that apply.

Quyidagilardan qaysilari dizaynni rangni farqlashda qiynaladigan odamlar uchun qulay qiladi? Mos keladigan barcha variantlarni belgilang.

Options
  • ✓ Adding a text label to colour-coded states
  • ✓ Using icons alongside colour
  • ✓ Strong lightness contrast
  • Telling errors apart only by a red colour — Colour alone is not enough.
  • ✓ Rang bilan belgilangan holatlarga matnli yorliq qoʻshish
  • ✓ Rang bilan birga ikonkalardan foydalanish
  • ✓ Yorqinlik boʻyicha kuchli kontrast
  • Xatolarni faqat qizil rang orqali ajratish — Rangning oʻzi yetarli emas.
Explanation

Text labels, icons and strong contrast carry meaning without relying on colour.

Matnli yorliqlar, ikonkalar va kuchli kontrast maʼnoni rangga tayanmasdan yetkazadi.

Tekshiruvchi uchun:
