Oʻyin dvigateli nima? — What is a game engine? · article · 167 words

Summary
The shared foundation that saves teams years of work.
Jamoalarni yillik mehnatdan qutqaradigan umumiy poydevor.
Lesson text

Not starting from nothing

Every game needs to draw images, play sounds, read the controller, simulate physics and load levels. Writing all of that from scratch would take years. A game engine is a ready-made foundation that provides these systems so creators can focus on their game.

What an engine typically provides

  • Rendering — drawing 3D worlds in real time, many times per second.
  • Physics — gravity, collisions and objects that fall and bounce.
  • Input — keyboard, mouse, gamepad and touch.
  • Audio — positional sound and music.
  • Scripting — ways to describe game rules and behaviour.
  • Tools — an editor to build levels and manage assets.

Unreal Engine in particular

Unreal Engine is developed by Epic Games. Version 5 introduced systems such as Nanite (very detailed geometry) and Lumen (dynamic global illumination). Behaviour can be built with Blueprints, a visual node-based system, or written in C++.

[!NOTE] Unreal Engine is free to download and learn. Commercial releases are subject to Epic’s licence terms — always read the current EULA on unrealengine.com before publishing a product.

Noldan boshlamaslik

Har bir oʻyin tasvirlarni chizishi, tovushlarni ijro etishi, boshqaruv qurilmasini oʻqishi, fizikani simulyatsiya qilishi va darajalarni yuklashi kerak. Bularning barchasini noldan yozish yillar talab qiladi. Oʻyin dvigateli (game engine) — shu tizimlarni taqdim etadigan tayyor poydevor; u yaratuvchilarga oʻz oʻyiniga eʼtibor qaratish imkonini beradi.

Dvigatel odatda nimalarni taqdim etadi

  • Rendering — 3D olamlarni real vaqtda, soniyasiga koʻp marta chizish.
  • Fizika — tortishish kuchi, toʻqnashuvlar, yiqiladigan va sakraydigan obyektlar.
  • Kiritish — klaviatura, sichqoncha, geympad va sensorli ekran.
  • Audio — fazoviy tovush va musiqa.
  • Skriptlash — oʻyin qoidalari va xatti-harakatlarini tavsiflash usullari.
  • Vositalar — darajalar qurish va resurslarni boshqarish uchun muharrir.

Aynan Unreal Engine haqida

Unreal Engine’ni Epic Games kompaniyasi ishlab chiqadi. 5-versiyada Nanite (juda batafsil geometriya) va Lumen (dinamik global yoritish) kabi tizimlar paydo boʻldi. Xatti-harakatlarni tugunlarga asoslangan vizual tizim — Blueprints yordamida qurish yoki C++ tilida yozish mumkin.

[!NOTE] Unreal Engine’ni yuklab olish va oʻrganish bepul. Tijoriy mahsulotlar esa Epic litsenziya shartlariga boʻysunadi — mahsulot chiqarishdan oldin har doim unrealengine.com saytidagi amaldagi EULA (litsenziya kelishuvi)ni oʻqing.

Unreal Engine’ni oʻrnatish — Installing Unreal Engine · article · 738 words

Summary
Check your computer, install the engine through the Epic Games Launcher and create your first project.
Kompyuteringizni tekshiring, dvigatelni Epic Games Launcher orqali oʻrnating va birinchi loyihangizni yarating.
Lesson text

Before you install: is your computer ready?

Unreal Engine is professional software that renders detailed 3D worlds in real time, so it asks a lot of your computer. Requirements change between engine versions, so always open the official Hardware and Software Specifications page in the Unreal Engine documentation for the exact version you are installing. The table below is practical guidance for learning, not an official specification.

Part Workable for learning Comfortable
Operating system 64-bit Windows 10/11 or a recent macOS The same, fully updated
Processor (CPU) Recent quad-core 6–8 cores or more
Memory (RAM) 16 GB 32 GB or more
Graphics (GPU) Dedicated graphics card with current drivers More video memory (8 GB or more)
Storage SSD with plenty of free space Fast NVMe SSD

[!NOTE] The engine installation alone typically takes several tens of gigabytes, and projects grow as you add content. Install on an SSD rather than a hard drive: the editor opens and compiles far faster.

Laptops with only integrated graphics can usually open the editor, but advanced lighting features may be slow. You will learn to lower the quality settings at the end of this lesson. Linux is supported through separate downloads; this course shows Windows and macOS.

Step 1: Create an Epic Games account

Unreal Engine is distributed by Epic Games. Create a free account at epicgames.com and turn on two-factor authentication for security.

Step 2: Install the Epic Games Launcher

Download the Epic Games Launcher from the official Epic Games or Unreal Engine website, install it and sign in.

[!WARNING] Download only from official Epic Games websites. Unofficial “free Unreal Engine” downloads are a common way to spread malware — the real engine is free from Epic.

Step 3: Install an engine version

  1. In the Launcher, open Unreal Engine in the left sidebar, then the Library tab.
  2. Next to Engine Versions, click the + button and choose the latest 5.x release (this course states the version it was recorded with).
  3. Click Install, then choose a folder on your SSD.
  4. Open Options before confirming. Starter Content and Templates and Feature Packs are useful for learning. Editor symbols for debugging are large and only needed for C++ debugging, and extra Target Platforms (such as Android or iOS) can be added later.

The download is large. You can keep using your computer while it installs.

Step 4: The first launch

Click Launch. The first start can take a long time because Unreal prepares its shaders — small programs that tell the graphics card how to draw materials. You may see a percentage counter. This is normal and happens mostly the first time you open a version or a project.

Step 5: Create your first project

The Unreal Project Browser opens.

  1. Choose the Games category, then the Third Person template. It gives you a playable character, so you can test things immediately.
  2. Under the project defaults, choose Blueprint (you will learn C++ later), target Desktop, and quality preset Maximum — or Scalable on a modest computer.
  3. Keep Starter Content enabled. It adds free props and materials to practise with.
  4. Pick a location and a short name without spaces, such as MyFirstProject, and click Create.

When the editor opens, press Alt + P (or the Play button) to walk around with the character. Press Esc to stop playing.

Licence awareness

Unreal Engine is free to download and learn. If you release a commercial product, Epic's licence terms apply, including possible royalties above certain revenue thresholds. These terms change, so read the current EULA on Epic's website before you publish anything.

Common problems

Problem What to do
The download is stuck or very slow Pause and resume it in the Launcher, and check that the drive has enough free space
The editor crashes on start Update your graphics drivers from NVIDIA, AMD or Intel, then restart the computer
The editor is very slow Open Settings → Engine Scalability Settings in the viewport toolbar and choose Medium or Low
“Not enough disk space” Install the engine or the project on a drive with more free space

Try it

Install the engine, create MyFirstProject from the Third Person template, press Play, run around the level and then stop with Esc.

Key terms

  • Epic Games Launcher — the app that installs and updates Unreal Engine.
  • Engine version — a numbered release such as 5.x; projects open in the version they were made with.
  • Template — a ready-made starting project (Blank, First Person, Third Person and others).
  • Shaders — programs the graphics card uses to draw surfaces; Unreal compiles them the first time.

Oʻrnatishdan oldin: kompyuteringiz tayyormi?

Unreal Engine — batafsil 3D olamlarni real vaqtda chizadigan professional dastur, shuning uchun u kompyuterdan koʻp narsa talab qiladi. Talablar dvigatel versiyasiga qarab oʻzgaradi, shu bois oʻrnatayotgan versiyangiz uchun Unreal Engine hujjatlaridagi rasmiy Hardware and Software Specifications (apparat va dasturiy taʼminot talablari) sahifasini albatta oching. Quyidagi jadval rasmiy talab emas, balki oʻqish uchun amaliy tavsiya.

Qism Oʻqish uchun yetarli Qulay ishlash uchun
Operatsion tizim 64 bitli Windows 10/11 yoki yangi macOS Xuddi shunday, toʻliq yangilangan
Protsessor (CPU) Yangi avlod toʻrt yadroli 6–8 va undan ortiq yadro
Operativ xotira (RAM) 16 GB 32 GB va undan koʻp
Grafika (GPU) Drayverlari yangilangan alohida videokarta Koʻproq video xotira (8 GB va undan ortiq)
Doimiy xotira Boʻsh joyi yetarli SSD Tezkor NVMe SSD

[!NOTE] Dvigatelning oʻzi odatda bir necha oʻn gigabayt joy egallaydi, loyihalar esa kontent qoʻshilgani sari kattalashadi. Dvigatelni qattiq diskka emas, SSD ga oʻrnating: muharrir ancha tez ochiladi va kompilyatsiya qiladi.

Faqat integratsiyalashgan grafikaga ega noutbuklar odatda muharrirni ocha oladi, biroq murakkab yoritish imkoniyatlari sekin ishlashi mumkin. Dars oxirida sifat sozlamalarini qanday pasaytirishni oʻrganasiz. Linux alohida yuklab olinadigan versiyalar orqali qoʻllab-quvvatlanadi; bu kursda Windows va macOS koʻrsatiladi.

1-qadam: Epic Games hisobini yarating

Unreal Engine’ni Epic Games kompaniyasi tarqatadi. epicgames.com saytida bepul hisob yarating va xavfsizlik uchun ikki bosqichli autentifikatsiyani yoqing.

2-qadam: Epic Games Launcher dasturini oʻrnating

Epic Games Launcher dasturini Epic Games yoki Unreal Engine’ning rasmiy saytidan yuklab oling, oʻrnating va hisobingizga kiring.

[!WARNING] Faqat Epic Games’ning rasmiy saytlaridan yuklab oling. Norasmiy “bepul Unreal Engine” yuklamalari zararli dasturlarni tarqatishning keng tarqalgan usuli — haqiqiy dvigatel Epic’da bepul.

3-qadam: Dvigatel versiyasini oʻrnating

  1. Launcher’da chap paneldagi Unreal Engine boʻlimini, soʻngra Library yorligʻini oching.
  2. Engine Versions yonidagi + tugmasini bosing va eng soʻnggi 5.x versiyani tanlang (kursda qaysi versiyada yozilgani koʻrsatiladi).
  3. Install tugmasini bosing va SSD dagi papkani tanlang.
  4. Tasdiqlashdan oldin Options ni oching. Oʻqish uchun Starter Content va Templates and Feature Packs foydali. Editor symbols for debugging katta hajmli va faqat C++ kodini tuzatish uchun kerak, qoʻshimcha Target Platforms (masalan, Android yoki iOS) esa keyinroq qoʻshilishi mumkin.

Yuklama katta hajmli. Oʻrnatilayotgan paytda kompyuterdan foydalanishda davom etishingiz mumkin.

4-qadam: Birinchi ishga tushirish

Launch tugmasini bosing. Birinchi ishga tushirish uzoq davom etishi mumkin, chunki Unreal sheyderlarni (shaders) tayyorlaydi — bu videokartaga materiallarni qanday chizishni aytadigan kichik dasturlar. Foiz hisoblagichini koʻrishingiz mumkin. Bu odatiy hol va asosan versiya yoki loyiha birinchi marta ochilganda sodir boʻladi.

5-qadam: Birinchi loyihangizni yarating

Unreal Project Browser (loyihalar oynasi) ochiladi.

  1. Games toifasini, soʻngra Third Person shablonini tanlang. U boshqariladigan personaj beradi, shuning uchun narsalarni darhol sinab koʻrishingiz mumkin.
  2. Loyiha sozlamalarida Blueprint ni (C++ ni keyinroq oʻrganasiz), maqsadli platforma sifatida Desktop ni va sifat darajasi sifatida Maximum ni — oddiyroq kompyuterda esa Scalable ni tanlang.
  3. Starter Content yoqilgan holda qolsin. U mashq qilish uchun bepul buyumlar va materiallar qoʻshadi.
  4. Joylashuvni va boʻsh joysiz qisqa nomni, masalan, MyFirstProject ni tanlab, Create tugmasini bosing.

Muharrir ochilgach, personaj bilan yurib koʻrish uchun Alt + P ni (yoki Play tugmasini) bosing. Oʻyinni toʻxtatish uchun Esc ni bosing.

Litsenziya haqida

Unreal Engine’ni yuklab olish va oʻrganish bepul. Agar tijoriy mahsulot chiqarsangiz, Epic litsenziya shartlari amal qiladi, jumladan, maʼlum daromad chegarasidan yuqorida royalti toʻlash talab qilinishi mumkin. Bu shartlar oʻzgarib turadi, shuning uchun biror narsani eʼlon qilishdan oldin Epic saytidagi amaldagi EULA (litsenziya kelishuvi) ni oʻqing.

Keng tarqalgan muammolar

Muammo Nima qilish kerak
Yuklab olish toʻxtab qolgan yoki juda sekin Launcher’da yuklamani pauza qilib, qayta davom ettiring va diskda boʻsh joy yetarliligini tekshiring
Muharrir ishga tushganda yopilib qoladi Videokarta drayverlarini NVIDIA, AMD yoki Intel saytidan yangilang va kompyuterni qayta ishga tushiring
Muharrir juda sekin ishlaydi Koʻrinish maydoni panelidagi Settings → Engine Scalability Settings ni oching va Medium yoki Low ni tanlang
“Diskda joy yetarli emas” Dvigatel yoki loyihani boʻsh joyi koʻproq diskka oʻrnating

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Dvigatelni oʻrnating, Third Person shablonidan MyFirstProject loyihasini yarating, Play ni bosing, daraja boʻylab yuguring va Esc bilan toʻxtating.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Epic Games Launcher — Unreal Engine’ni oʻrnatadigan va yangilaydigan dastur.
  • Dvigatel versiyasi — 5.x kabi raqamlangan reliz; loyihalar ular yaratilgan versiyada ochiladi.
  • Shablon (template) — tayyor boshlangʻich loyiha (Blank, First Person, Third Person va boshqalar).
  • Sheyderlar (shaders) — videokarta sirtlarni chizish uchun foydalanadigan dasturlar; Unreal ularni birinchi marta kompilyatsiya qiladi.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:

Muharrir boʻylab sayohat — A tour of the editor · article · 749 words

Summary
Find your way around the Unreal Editor: viewport, Outliner, Details, Content Drawer, play controls and saving.
Unreal Editor’da yoʻlingizni toping: koʻrinish maydoni, Outliner, Details, Content Drawer, oʻyinni ishga tushirish va saqlash.
Lesson text

The main areas of the editor

When your project opens, you see the Level Editor. Its default layout has six areas worth knowing by name:

  1. Main toolbar (top) — saving, the editing mode selector, the Quickly add to the project button for placing actors, and the Play controls.
  2. Level Viewport (centre) — your window into the level. Here you look around, select objects and move them.
  3. Outliner (top right) — a list of every actor (object) in the level, like a table of contents.
  4. Details panel (right, below the Outliner) — the properties of whatever is selected: its position, rotation and scale (the Transform), its components and its settings.
  5. Content Drawer (bottom, or press Ctrl + Space) — all the assets in your project: meshes, materials, textures, sounds and Blueprints, organised in folders.
  6. Bottom toolbar — the Output Log and a console for advanced commands.

[!TIP] Every panel can be closed, moved and docked. If you lose one, open it again from the Window menu. Window → Load Layout → Default Editor Layout restores the original arrangement.

Moving around the viewport

Action How
Fly through the level Hold the right mouse button and use W A S D (Q and E move down and up)
Change flying speed While holding the right mouse button, scroll the mouse wheel
Focus on the selected actor Press F
Orbit around the focus point Alt + left mouse button drag
Zoom Scroll the mouse wheel

If you know Blender, notice the difference: Blender orbits with the middle mouse button, while Unreal is built around flying like a game camera.

Selecting and transforming actors

  • Click an actor to select it; Ctrl + click adds or removes actors from the selection.
  • W switches to Move, E to Rotate and R to Scale. Drag the coloured handles of the gizmo: red is X, green is Y and blue is Z. In Unreal, Z points up.
  • End drops the selected actor onto the surface below it.
  • Ctrl + D duplicates the selection, and Delete removes it.
  • In the viewport toolbar you can switch between world and local axes and turn grid snapping on or off for movement, rotation and scale.

Placing actors

There are two everyday ways to add something to the level:

  • Click Quickly add to the project in the main toolbar and choose, for example, Shapes → Cube.
  • Drag an asset from the Content Drawer into the viewport. Starter Content has chairs, tables, lamps and materials in its folders.

Play and Simulate

  • Play (Alt + P) starts the game in the viewport with you controlling the player. Press Esc to stop.
  • Simulate (Alt + S) runs physics and logic while you keep the editor camera, which is useful for watching things happen.

[!WARNING] Changes you make to the level while playing are thrown away when you stop. Always stop playing before you edit.

Saving your work

  • Ctrl + S saves the current level. Ctrl + Shift + S (Save All) saves every changed asset.
  • Levels are saved as .umap files and other assets as .uasset files inside the project's Content folder.

[!WARNING] Never move or rename asset files in Windows Explorer or Finder. Unreal keeps references between assets; moving files outside the editor breaks them. Always rename and move assets inside the Content Drawer.

Keeping a project tidy

Professional teams name assets with short prefixes so they can find them quickly:

Prefix Asset type
SM_ Static mesh
M_ Material
MI_ Material instance
T_ Texture
BP_ Blueprint

Create your own folder (for example Content/MyFirstProject) so your work never mixes with Starter Content.

Try it: a ten-step tour

  1. Open the Content Drawer with Ctrl + Space.
  2. Find a chair in the Starter Content Props folder.
  3. Drag it into the level.
  4. Press F to focus on it.
  5. Move it with W along the X axis only.
  6. Rotate it 90° with E and grid snapping on.
  7. Duplicate it with Ctrl + D and place the copy next to the table.
  8. Find both chairs in the Outliner and rename one of them.
  9. Press Alt + P, walk to the chairs, then stop with Esc.
  10. Save everything with Ctrl + Shift + S.

Common mistakes

  • Editing while the game is playing — the changes disappear when you stop.
  • Losing the view — select something and press F, or use the Perspective menu to reset the view.
  • Moving files outside the editor — broken references and missing assets.

Key terms

  • Actor — anything placed in a level: a mesh, a light, a camera, a character.
  • Asset — a file in the Content Drawer that actors can use, such as a mesh or a material.
  • Transform — an actor's location, rotation and scale.
  • Level — a map that contains actors; saved as a .umap file.

Muharrirning asosiy qismlari

Loyihangiz ochilganda Level Editor (daraja muharriri) ni koʻrasiz. Uning standart joylashuvida nomini bilib olish kerak boʻlgan oltita qism bor:

  1. Asosiy asboblar paneli (yuqorida) — saqlash, tahrirlash rejimini tanlash, obyekt joylashtirish uchun Quickly add to the project tugmasi va Play boshqaruvlari.
  2. Level Viewport (koʻrinish maydoni, markazda) — darajaga ochilgan oynangiz. Bu yerda atrofni koʻzdan kechirasiz, obyektlarni tanlaysiz va siljitasiz.
  3. Outliner (oʻng yuqorida) — darajadagi barcha aktorlar (obyektlar) roʻyxati, xuddi mundarija kabi.
  4. Details paneli (oʻngda, Outliner ostida) — tanlangan narsaning xususiyatlari: joylashuvi, burilishi va masshtabi (Transform), komponentlari va sozlamalari.
  5. Content Drawer (pastda yoki Ctrl + Space) — loyihangizdagi barcha resurslar (assets): meshlar, materiallar, teksturalar, tovushlar va Blueprint’lar papkalarga ajratilgan holda.
  6. Pastki asboblar paneli — Output Log (jurnal) va murakkab buyruqlar uchun konsol.

[!TIP] Har bir panelni yopish, koʻchirish va biriktirish mumkin. Biror panelni yoʻqotib qoʻysangiz, uni Window menyusidan qayta oching. Window → Load Layout → Default Editor Layout dastlabki joylashuvni tiklaydi.

Koʻrinish maydonida harakatlanish

Harakat Qanday qilinadi
Daraja boʻylab uchish Sichqonchaning oʻng tugmasini bosib turib, W A S D dan foydalaning (Q va E pastga va yuqoriga siljitadi)
Uchish tezligini oʻzgartirish Oʻng tugmani bosib turgan holda sichqoncha gʻildiragini aylantiring
Tanlangan aktorga eʼtibor qaratish F ni bosing
Diqqat nuqtasi atrofida aylanish Alt + sichqonchaning chap tugmasi bilan torting
Yaqinlashtirish Sichqoncha gʻildiragini aylantiring

Agar Blenderni bilsangiz, farqqa eʼtibor bering: Blender oʻrta tugma bilan aylantiradi, Unreal esa oʻyin kamerasi kabi uchishga asoslangan.

Aktorlarni tanlash va transformatsiya qilish

  • Aktorni tanlash uchun uni bosing; Ctrl + bosish tanlovga aktor qoʻshadi yoki undan olib tashlaydi.
  • W siljitishga, E aylantirishga, R masshtablashga oʻtkazadi. Gizmoning rangli dastaklarini torting: qizil — X, yashil — Y, koʻk — Z. Unreal’da Z oʻqi yuqoriga qaragan.
  • End tanlangan aktorni ostidagi sirtga tushiradi.
  • Ctrl + D tanlovni nusxalaydi, Delete esa oʻchiradi.
  • Koʻrinish maydoni panelida global (world) va lokal (local) oʻqlar orasida almashish hamda siljitish, aylantirish va masshtablash uchun toʻrga yopishishni (grid snapping) yoqish yoki oʻchirish mumkin.

Aktorlarni joylashtirish

Darajaga biror narsa qoʻshishning ikkita kundalik usuli bor:

  • Asosiy asboblar panelidagi Quickly add to the project tugmasini bosing va, masalan, Shapes → Cube ni tanlang.
  • Content Drawer dan resursni koʻrinish maydoniga torting. Starter Content papkalarida stullar, stollar, chiroqlar va materiallar bor.

Play va Simulate

  • Play (Alt + P) oʻyinni koʻrinish maydonida ishga tushiradi va oʻyinchini siz boshqarasiz. Toʻxtatish uchun Esc ni bosing.
  • Simulate (Alt + S) muharrir kamerasi sizda qolgan holda fizika va mantiqni ishga tushiradi — jarayonlarni kuzatish uchun qulay.

[!WARNING] Oʻyin ishlab turgan paytda darajaga kiritgan oʻzgarishlaringiz toʻxtatganingizda bekor boʻladi. Tahrirlashdan oldin har doim oʻyinni toʻxtating.

Ishingizni saqlash

  • Ctrl + S joriy darajani saqlaydi. Ctrl + Shift + S (Save All) oʻzgargan barcha resurslarni saqlaydi.
  • Darajalar .umap fayllari, boshqa resurslar esa loyihaning Content papkasida .uasset fayllari sifatida saqlanadi.

[!WARNING] Resurs fayllarini hech qachon Windows Explorer yoki Finder orqali koʻchirmang va qayta nomlamang. Unreal resurslar orasidagi bogʻlanishlarni saqlaydi; fayllarni muharrirdan tashqarida koʻchirish bu bogʻlanishlarni buzadi. Resurslarni har doim Content Drawer ichida qayta nomlang va koʻchiring.

Loyihani tartibli saqlash

Professional jamoalar resurslarni tez topish uchun ularga qisqa prefiksli nomlar beradi:

Prefiks Resurs turi
SM_ Statik mesh
M_ Material
MI_ Material nusxasi (material instance)
T_ Tekstura
BP_ Blueprint

Ishingiz Starter Content bilan aralashib ketmasligi uchun oʻz papkangizni yarating (masalan, Content/MyFirstProject).

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring: oʻn qadamli sayohat

  1. Ctrl + Space bilan Content Drawer ni oching.
  2. Starter Content’ning Props papkasidan stul toping.
  3. Uni darajaga torting.
  4. Unga eʼtibor qaratish uchun F ni bosing.
  5. Uni W yordamida faqat X oʻqi boʻylab siljiting.
  6. Toʻrga yopishish yoqilgan holda E bilan 90° ga aylantiring.
  7. Ctrl + D bilan nusxalang va nusxani stol yoniga qoʻying.
  8. Ikkala stulni Outliner’dan toping va ulardan birini qayta nomlang.
  9. Alt + P ni bosing, stullar yoniga boring va Esc bilan toʻxtating.
  10. Hammasini Ctrl + Shift + S bilan saqlang.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Oʻyin ishlab turganda tahrirlash — toʻxtatganingizda oʻzgarishlar yoʻqoladi.
  • Koʻrinishni yoʻqotib qoʻyish — biror narsani tanlab F ni bosing yoki koʻrinishni tiklash uchun Perspective menyusidan foydalaning.
  • Fayllarni muharrirdan tashqarida koʻchirish — bogʻlanishlar buziladi va resurslar yoʻqoladi.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Aktor (actor) — darajaga joylashtirilgan har qanday narsa: mesh, yorugʻlik manbai, kamera, personaj.
  • Resurs (asset) — Content Drawer dagi, aktorlar foydalanishi mumkin boʻlgan fayl, masalan, mesh yoki material.
  • Transform — aktorning joylashuvi, burilishi va masshtabi.
  • Daraja (level) — aktorlarni oʻz ichiga olgan xarita; .umap fayli sifatida saqlanadi.

Bilimingizni tekshiring — Check your understanding · quiz

Summary
Five questions on engines, installation and the editor.
Dvigatellar, oʻrnatish va muharrir boʻyicha beshta savol.
Test: Ishni boshlash boʻyicha test
Q1

Why do game teams use an engine such as Unreal instead of writing everything themselves?

Nega oʻyin jamoalari hamma narsani oʻzlari yozish oʻrniga Unreal kabi dvigateldan foydalanadi?

Options
  • ✓ It provides ready-made systems like rendering, physics and input
  • It designs the game automatically — An engine is a foundation of systems and tools; people still design and build the game.
  • It is required by app stores — Stores do not require any particular engine.
  • ✓ U rendering, fizika va kiritish kabi tayyor tizimlarni taqdim etadi
  • U oʻyinni avtomatik loyihalaydi — Dvigatel — tizimlar va vositalardan iborat poydevor; oʻyinni baribir odamlar loyihalaydi va yaratadi.
  • Ilova doʻkonlari buni talab qiladi — Doʻkonlar biror muayyan dvigatelni talab qilmaydi.
Explanation

An engine provides rendering, physics, input, audio, scripting and tools, so teams can focus on their own game.

Dvigatel rendering, fizika, kiritish, audio, skriptlash va vositalarni taqdim etadi, shuning uchun jamoalar oʻz oʻyiniga eʼtibor qarata oladi.

Q2

Where should you download Unreal Engine from?

Unreal Engine’ni qayerdan yuklab olish kerak?

Options
  • ✓ The Epic Games Launcher from the official Epic website
  • Any site that offers a free download — Unofficial downloads are a common way to spread malware.
  • A file shared in a chat group — Files from chats can be modified or infected.
  • ✓ Epic’ning rasmiy saytidagi Epic Games Launcher orqali
  • Bepul yuklab olishni taklif qiladigan istalgan saytdan — Norasmiy yuklamalar zararli dasturlarni tarqatishning keng tarqalgan usuli.
  • Chat guruhida ulashilgan fayldan — Chatlardagi fayllar oʻzgartirilgan yoki zararlangan boʻlishi mumkin.
Explanation

Only from Epic Games — through the official Epic Games Launcher.

Faqat Epic Games’dan — rasmiy Epic Games Launcher orqali.

Q3

The first launch shows a long “preparing shaders” counter. What does that mean?

Birinchi ishga tushirishda uzoq davom etadigan “preparing shaders” hisoblagichi koʻrinadi. Bu nimani bildiradi?

Options
  • ✓ Unreal is compiling shaders, which is normal the first time
  • The installation is broken — Shader compilation is expected on first launch.
  • Your licence is being checked — Shader compilation has nothing to do with licences.
  • ✓ Unreal sheyderlarni kompilyatsiya qilmoqda — birinchi marta bu odatiy hol
  • Oʻrnatish buzilgan — Birinchi ishga tushirishda sheyder kompilyatsiyasi kutilgan jarayon.
  • Litsenziyangiz tekshirilmoqda — Sheyder kompilyatsiyasining litsenziyaga aloqasi yoʻq.
Explanation

Unreal compiles shaders the first time; it is normal and mostly happens once per version or project.

Unreal sheyderlarni birinchi marta kompilyatsiya qiladi; bu odatiy hol va asosan har bir versiya yoki loyiha uchun bir marta sodir boʻladi.

Q4

Which panel lists every actor in the current level?

Qaysi panel joriy darajadagi barcha aktorlarni roʻyxat qilib koʻrsatadi?

Options
  • ✓ Outliner
  • Details — The Details panel shows the properties of the selected actor.
  • Content Drawer — The Content Drawer lists assets in the project, not actors in the level.
  • ✓ Outliner
  • Details — Details paneli tanlangan aktorning xususiyatlarini koʻrsatadi.
  • Content Drawer — Content Drawer darajadagi aktorlarni emas, loyihadagi resurslarni koʻrsatadi.
Explanation

The Outliner lists the actors in the level; the Details panel shows the properties of the selected one.

Outliner darajadagi aktorlar roʻyxatini koʻrsatadi; Details paneli esa tanlangan aktorning xususiyatlarini koʻrsatadi.

Q5

True or false: changes you make to the level while the game is playing are kept when you stop.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: oʻyin ishlab turgan paytda darajaga kiritilgan oʻzgarishlar toʻxtatilgandan keyin ham saqlanib qoladi.

Options
  • True — They are discarded when you stop playing.
  • ✓ False
  • Toʻgʻri — Oʻyin toʻxtatilganda ular bekor boʻladi.
  • ✓ Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

False — changes made during Play are thrown away when you stop. Stop playing before editing.

Notoʻgʻri — Play paytida kiritilgan oʻzgarishlar toʻxtatilganda bekor boʻladi. Tahrirlashdan oldin oʻyinni toʻxtating.

Modul: Loyiha va yakuniy imtihon (Project and final assessment)

Module description
Build your first scene and show what you have learned.
Birinchi sahnangizni yarating va oʻrganganlaringizni koʻrsating.

Loyiha: birinchi sahnangiz — Project: your first scene · project · 171 words

Summary
Set up a project and build a small, tidy scene from Starter Content using the editor tools.
Loyiha yarating va muharrir vositalari yordamida Starter Content’dan kichik, tartibli sahna yasang.
Lesson text

Your first Unreal project

Use what you learned in this module to build a small room or outdoor corner from Starter Content props. The goal is not beautiful art yet — it is confident, tidy use of the editor.

Requirements

  1. A project created from a template that opens without errors.
  2. At least 10 props from Starter Content placed in the level.
  3. Use all three transform tools (W, E, R) and grid snapping; props rest on surfaces (use End).
  4. Every placed actor has a meaningful name in the Outliner (for example Chair_Kitchen_01).
  5. Your own folder in the Content Drawer for anything you create or duplicate.
  6. The level and all assets are saved.

How to submit

Upload three screenshots — two from different viewpoints in the editor and one from Play mode — and write two or three sentences about what you built and which tools you used. You can also paste a link to a short screen recording.

[!NOTE] A reviewer checks your work against the rubric and leaves feedback. You can improve your scene and resubmit.

Birinchi Unreal loyihangiz

Ushbu modulda oʻrganganlaringizdan foydalanib, Starter Content buyumlaridan kichik xona yoki ochiq maydon burchagini yarating. Hozircha maqsad chiroyli sanʼat asari emas — muharrirdan ishonch bilan va tartibli foydalanish.

Talablar

  1. Shablondan yaratilgan va xatosiz ochiladigan loyiha.
  2. Darajaga joylashtirilgan kamida 10 ta Starter Content buyumi.
  3. Uchala transformatsiya vositasidan (W, E, R) va toʻrga yopishishdan foydalanilgan; buyumlar sirtlarda turibdi (End dan foydalaning).
  4. Joylashtirilgan har bir aktorning Outliner’da mazmunli nomi bor (masalan, Chair_Kitchen_01).
  5. Siz yaratgan yoki nusxalagan narsalar uchun Content Drawer’da oʻz papkangiz.
  6. Daraja va barcha resurslar saqlangan.

Qanday topshiriladi

Uchta skrinshot yuklang — ikkitasi muharrirdagi turli nuqtalardan, bittasi Play rejimidan — va nima yaratganingiz hamda qaysi vositalardan foydalanganingiz haqida ikki-uch gap yozing. Qisqa ekran yozuviga havola ham joylashingiz mumkin.

[!NOTE] Tekshiruvchi ishingizni baholash mezonlari asosida koʻrib chiqadi va fikr-mulohaza qoldiradi. Sahnangizni yaxshilab, qayta topshirishingiz mumkin.

Assignment brief

Build a small, tidy scene from Starter Content and submit three screenshots with a short description.

Starter Content’dan kichik, tartibli sahna yarating va qisqa tavsif bilan uchta skrinshot topshiring.

Rubric
Criterion Points
Project created from a template, saved, opens without errors 15
At least 10 Starter Content props placed with move, rotate and scale 25
Deliberate arrangement: snapping and props resting on surfaces 20
Meaningful actor names in the Outliner and an own Content folder 20
Three screenshots (two viewpoints, one in Play mode) with a short description 20
Mezon Ball
Loyiha shablondan yaratilgan, saqlangan va xatosiz ochiladi 15

Puxta joylashuv: toʻrga yopishish va sirtda turgan buyumlar 20
Outliner’da mazmunli aktor nomlari va oʻz Content papkasi 20

Summary
A short exam covering the whole course. Pass mark: 70%.

Test: Unreal Engine asoslari — yakuniy imtihon
Q1

Which template gives you a playable character so you can test your level immediately?

Qaysi shablon darajangizni darhol sinab koʻrishingiz uchun boshqariladigan personaj beradi?

Options
  • Blank — Blank starts with an empty project and no character.
  • ✓ Third Person
  • Film/Video & Live Events — That category is for film and virtual production, not a playable character.
  • Blank — Blank boʻsh loyiha bilan boshlanadi, unda personaj yoʻq.
  • ✓ Third Person
  • Film/Video & Live Events — Bu toifa boshqariladigan personaj uchun emas, kino va virtual ishlab chiqarish uchun.
Explanation

The Third Person template includes a character you can control with Play.

Third Person shablonida Play orqali boshqarish mumkin boʻlgan personaj bor.

Q2

You have not learned C++ yet. Which project type should you choose?

Siz hali C++ ni oʻrganmagansiz. Qaysi loyiha turini tanlashingiz kerak?

Options
  • ✓ Blueprint
  • C++ — C++ projects need a compiler and C++ knowledge; start with Blueprint.
  • ✓ Blueprint
  • C++ — C++ loyihalari kompilyator va C++ bilimini talab qiladi; Blueprint’dan boshlang.
Explanation

Q3

How do you fly through the level in the viewport?

Koʻrinish maydonida daraja boʻylab qanday uchiladi?

Options
  • ✓ Hold the right mouse button and use W A S D
  • Hold the middle mouse button and drag — That is how Blender orbits; Unreal flies with the right mouse button.
  • Press the arrow keys only — Arrow keys alone do not give you full camera control.
  • ✓ Sichqonchaning oʻng tugmasini bosib turib, W A S D dan foydalanish
  • Sichqonchaning oʻrta tugmasini bosib turib tortish — Blender shu tarzda aylantiradi; Unreal’da esa oʻng tugma bilan uchiladi.
  • Faqat strelka tugmalarini bosish — Strelka tugmalarining oʻzi kamerani toʻliq boshqarish imkonini bermaydi.
Explanation

Hold the right mouse button and use W A S D, like a game camera.

Sichqonchaning oʻng tugmasini bosib turib, xuddi oʻyin kamerasidagi kabi W A S D dan foydalaning.

Q4

Which key focuses the viewport on the selected actor?

Qaysi tugma koʻrinish maydonini tanlangan aktorga qaratadi?

Options
  • ✓ F
  • G — G toggles game view in the viewport.
  • Home — Home does not frame the selection in Unreal.
  • ✓ F
  • G — G koʻrinish maydonida oʻyin koʻrinishini yoqadi yoki oʻchiradi.
  • Home — Unreal’da Home tanlovni ekranga sigʻdirmaydi.
Explanation

F frames the selected actor.

F tanlangan aktorni ekranga sigʻdiradi.

Q5

Which keys switch the gizmo between Move, Rotate and Scale?

Qaysi tugmalar gizmoni siljitish, aylantirish va masshtablash rejimlari orasida almashtiradi?

Options
  • ✓ W, E, R
  • G, R, S — G, R and S are Blender's shortcuts.
  • M, R, S — These are not Unreal shortcuts.
  • ✓ W, E, R
  • G, R, S — G, R va S — Blender’ning tezkor tugmalari.
  • M, R, S — Bular Unreal’ning tezkor tugmalari emas.
Explanation

W moves, E rotates and R scales.

W siljitadi, E aylantiradi, R masshtablaydi.

Q6

Which axis points up in Unreal Engine?

Unreal Engine’da qaysi oʻq yuqoriga qaragan?

Options
  • X
  • Y
  • ✓ Z
  • X
  • Y
  • ✓ Z
Explanation

Unreal is Z-up, like Blender.

Unreal’da, xuddi Blenderdagi kabi, Z oʻqi yuqoriga qaragan.

Q7

You want to rename a texture asset. Where do you do it?

Tekstura resursini qayta nomlamoqchisiz. Buni qayerda qilasiz?

Options
  • ✓ In the Content Drawer
  • In Windows Explorer or Finder — Renaming outside the editor breaks references between assets.
  • Either — it makes no difference — It matters: only the editor updates references.
  • ✓ Content Drawer’da
  • Windows Explorer yoki Finder’da — Muharrirdan tashqarida qayta nomlash resurslar orasidagi bogʻlanishlarni buzadi.
  • Farqi yoʻq — ikkalasida ham boʻladi — Farqi bor: bogʻlanishlarni faqat muharrir yangilaydi.
Explanation

Rename and move assets inside the Content Drawer so Unreal can keep references between assets intact.

Resurslar orasidagi bogʻlanishlar buzilmasligi uchun ularni Content Drawer ichida qayta nomlang va koʻchiring.

Q8

True or false: Unreal Engine is free to learn, but commercial releases are subject to Epic's licence terms.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: Unreal Engine’ni oʻrganish bepul, ammo tijoriy mahsulotlar Epic litsenziya shartlariga boʻysunadi.

Options
  • ✓ True
  • False — Commercial releases are covered by Epic's licence, which can include royalties.
  • ✓ Toʻgʻri
  • Notoʻgʻri — Tijoriy relizlar Epic litsenziyasi bilan tartibga solinadi, unga royalti ham kirishi mumkin.
Explanation

True. Read the current EULA before publishing a commercial product.

Toʻgʻri. Tijoriy mahsulot chiqarishdan oldin amaldagi EULA ni oʻqing.

Modul: Montaj hunari (The craft of editing)

Module description
Ideas that every editor uses.
Har bir montajchi foydalanadigan gʻoyalar.

Video montaj aslida nima — What video editing really is · article · 173 words

Summary
Choosing, ordering and timing shots to tell a story.
Hikoya aytish uchun lavhalarni tanlash, tartiblash va vaqtini belgilash.
Lesson text

Editing is storytelling

Video editing is selecting the best moments from recorded footage, putting them in an order and timing them so they tell a story or deliver a message. Editors decide what the audience sees, for how long, and in what sequence.

A finished two-minute video might come from an hour of footage. Most of editing is choosing what to leave out.

Key vocabulary

  • Footage — the raw recorded video.
  • Clip — one piece of footage placed in your project.
  • Shot — a continuous recording from one camera angle.
  • Cut — the point where one shot ends and the next begins.
  • Sequence — clips arranged together in order.

Why cuts work

Our brains accept cuts naturally because we already “cut” our attention many times a minute when we look around. A good cut is often invisible: the viewer follows the story and never notices the edit.

[!TIP] Watch a scene from a favourite film with the sound off and count the cuts. You will notice far more than you expected — and that most of them felt invisible.

Montaj — bu hikoyachilik

Video montaj — yozib olingan materialdan eng yaxshi lahzalarni tanlab, ularni hikoya aytadigan yoki xabar yetkazadigan tarzda tartiblash va davomiyligini belgilash. Montajchi tomoshabin nimani, qancha vaqt va qanday ketma-ketlikda koʻrishini hal qiladi.

Tayyor ikki daqiqalik video bir soatlik materialdan yigʻilgan boʻlishi mumkin. Montajning katta qismi — nimani tashlab yuborishni tanlash.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Footage (xom material) — yozib olingan, ishlov berilmagan video.
  • Klip — loyihangizga joylashtirilgan bitta material boʻlagi.
  • Lavha (shot, plan) — bitta kamera rakursidan uzluksiz yozib olingan qism.
  • Kesish (cut) — bir lavha tugab, keyingisi boshlanadigan nuqta.
  • Ketma-ketlik (sequence) — tartib bilan joylashtirilgan kliplar toʻplami.

Kesishlar nega ishlaydi

Miyamiz kesishlarni tabiiy qabul qiladi, chunki atrofga qaraganimizda diqqatimizni daqiqasiga koʻp marta “kesib” turamiz. Yaxshi kesish koʻpincha sezilmaydi: tomoshabin hikoyani kuzatadi va montajni payqamaydi.

[!TIP] Sevimli filmingizdagi bir sahnani ovozsiz tomosha qiling va kesishlarni sanang. Kutganingizdan ancha koʻp kesish borligini — va ularning aksariyati sezilmaganini payqaysiz.

Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Vaqt shkalasi, kliplar va kesishlar — The timeline, clips and cuts · article · 232 words

Summary
Tracks, frame rates, resolution and the most useful types of cut.
Treklar, kadrlar chastotasi, ruxsat va eng foydali kesish turlari.
Lesson text

The timeline

Every editor has a timeline: time runs left to right, and clips sit on horizontal tracks.

  • Video tracks are stacked: higher tracks appear on top of lower ones (useful for titles and overlays).
  • Audio tracks sit below and play together.

The playhead is the vertical line that marks the current frame.

Project settings

Setting Common values Meaning
Resolution 1920×1080 (Full HD), 3840×2160 (4K) Number of pixels in each frame
Frame rate 24, 25, 30, 60 fps Images shown per second
Aspect ratio 16:9 (landscape), 9:16 (vertical), 1:1 Shape of the frame

[!NOTE] Choose the frame rate before you start and keep it consistent. 24 fps feels cinematic, 25 is common for broadcast in many countries, and 60 is smooth for sports and games.

Types of cut

  • Hard cut — a straight switch from one shot to the next. The most common edit.
  • Jump cut — a cut within the same shot that skips time; popular in vlogs.
  • J-cut — the audio of the next shot starts before its video.
  • L-cut — the audio of the current shot continues over the next shot’s video.
  • Cutaway — briefly showing something else (a detail, a reaction) before returning.

A professional workflow

  1. Organise — import and name your footage in folders (bins).
  2. Assembly — place the usable shots roughly in order.
  3. Rough cut — shape the story; get the structure right.
  4. Fine cut — perfect the timing of every cut.
  5. Colour and sound — correct, grade and mix.
  6. Export — render the final file for its destination.

Vaqt shkalasi

Har bir montaj dasturida vaqt shkalasi (timeline) bor: vaqt chapdan oʻngga oqadi, kliplar esa gorizontal treklarda joylashadi.

  • Video treklar ustma-ust joylashadi: yuqoridagi treklar pastdagilarning ustida koʻrinadi (titrlar va ustki qatlamlar uchun qulay).
  • Audio treklar pastda joylashadi va birgalikda ijro etiladi.

Ijro kursori (playhead) — joriy kadrni belgilovchi vertikal chiziq.

Loyiha sozlamalari

Sozlama Keng tarqalgan qiymatlar Maʼnosi
Ruxsat (resolution) 1920×1080 (Full HD), 3840×2160 (4K) Har bir kadrdagi piksellar soni
Kadrlar chastotasi (frame rate) 24, 25, 30, 60 fps Bir soniyada koʻrsatiladigan tasvirlar soni
Tomonlar nisbati (aspect ratio) 16:9 (gorizontal), 9:16 (vertikal), 1:1 Kadrning shakli

[!NOTE] Kadrlar chastotasini ishni boshlashdan oldin tanlang va butun loyiha davomida oʻzgartirmang. 24 fps kinematografik taassurot beradi, 25 fps koʻplab mamlakatlarda televideniye uchun odatiy, 60 fps esa sport va oʻyinlar uchun silliq harakat beradi.

Kesish turlari

  • Toʻgʻridan-toʻgʻri kesish (hard cut) — bir lavhadan keyingisiga toʻgʻridan-toʻgʻri oʻtish. Eng koʻp ishlatiladigan montaj usuli.
  • Jump cut (sakrash) — bitta lavha ichida vaqtni tashlab oʻtuvchi kesish; vloglarda mashhur.
  • J-cut — keyingi lavhaning ovozi uning tasviridan oldin boshlanadi.
  • L-cut — joriy lavhaning ovozi keyingi lavha tasviri ustida davom etadi.
  • Cutaway (chetga burilish) — asosiy lavhaga qaytishdan oldin qisqa vaqt boshqa narsani (detal, reaksiya) koʻrsatish.

Professional ish jarayoni

  1. Tartiblash — materialni import qiling va papkalarga (bins) nomlab joylang.
  2. Yigʻish (assembly) — yaroqli lavhalarni taxminiy tartibda joylashtiring.
  3. Qoralama montaj (rough cut) — hikoyani shakllantiring, tuzilmani toʻgʻri yoʻlga qoʻying.
  4. Yakuniy montaj (fine cut) — har bir kesishning vaqtini mukammallashtiring.
  5. Rang va ovoz — rang korreksiyasi, rang tusini berish va ovozni aralashtirish.
  6. Eksport — yakuniy faylni u moʻljallangan joy uchun render qiling.

Bilimingizni tekshiring — Check your understanding · quiz

Summary
Editing vocabulary and settings.
Montaj atamalari va sozlamalari.
Test: Montaj asoslari testi
Q1

The audio of the next shot begins before its picture appears. What is this?

Keyingi lavhaning ovozi uning tasviri paydo boʻlishidan oldin boshlanadi. Bu nima?

Options
  • L-cut — In an L-cut the audio of the *current* shot continues over the next picture.
  • ✓ J-cut
  • Jump cut — A jump cut skips time within one shot.
  • L-cut — L-cut usulida *joriy* lavhaning ovozi keyingi tasvir ustida davom etadi.
  • ✓ J-cut
  • Jump cut — Jump cut bitta lavha ichida vaqtni tashlab oʻtadi.
Explanation

A J-cut: on the timeline the audio and video form the shape of the letter J.

J-cut: vaqt shkalasida audio va video J harfi shaklini hosil qiladi.

Q2

Which aspect ratio is standard for vertical phone videos?

Telefondagi vertikal videolar uchun qaysi tomonlar nisbati standart hisoblanadi?

Options
  • 16:9
  • ✓ 9:16
  • 4:3
  • 16:9
  • ✓ 9:16
  • 4:3
Explanation

9:16 is vertical; 16:9 is landscape.

9:16 — vertikal; 16:9 — gorizontal.

Q3

True or false: on most editors, a clip on a higher video track appears on top of clips below it.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: aksariyat montaj dasturlarida yuqoridagi video trekdagi klip pastdagi kliplar ustida koʻrinadi.

Options
  • ✓ True
  • False
  • ✓ Toʻgʻri
  • Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

Higher video tracks are layered over lower ones.

Yuqoridagi video treklar pastdagilarning ustiga qatlam sifatida tushadi.

Modul: Tasvir va ovoz (Pictures and sound)

Module description
The shots you cut with and the sound that holds them together.
Montaj qilinadigan lavhalar va ularni bir butunga bogʻlaydigan ovoz.

Lavhalar, kompozitsiya va uzluksizlik — Shots, composition and continuity · article · 477 words

Summary
Shot sizes, framing and the continuity rules that make cuts feel natural.
Lavha oʻlchamlari, kadrga olish va kesishlarni tabiiy his qildiradigan uzluksizlik qoidalari.
Lesson text

The vocabulary of pictures

Editors choose between shots all day, so they need words for them. In this lesson you will learn to name shots, judge their framing and keep the action continuous across cuts.

Shot size What it shows Typical use
Extreme wide A whole landscape or city Establishing where we are
Wide A person from head to toe in their surroundings Showing action and place
Medium A person from the waist up Conversations, presenting
Close-up A face or an important object Emotion and detail
Extreme close-up Eyes, hands, a tiny detail Tension and emphasis
Insert A detail of the action, such as a hand pressing a button Clarifying what happens

A classic sequence moves from wide to close: first where, then who, then how they feel.

Camera angle

  • Eye level feels neutral and honest.
  • High angle (looking down) makes a subject look smaller or vulnerable.
  • Low angle (looking up) makes a subject look powerful.
  • Over-the-shoulder shows a conversation from one person's point of view.

Composition

  • Rule of thirds: imagine the frame divided into three both ways; place important things on the lines or where they cross.
  • Headroom: leave a little space above a person's head — not too much, not none.
  • Lead room: leave space in the direction a person is looking or moving.
  • Leading lines: roads, rails or walls that guide the eye to the subject.

Continuity: cuts that feel invisible

Continuity rules help the audience understand space and time without noticing the edit.

  • The 180° rule: imagine a line between two people talking. Keep the camera on one side of it; crossing the line makes them seem to swap places.
  • The 30° rule: when cutting between two shots of the same subject, change the angle by at least 30° (or change the shot size). Otherwise the cut looks like a mistake — a jump cut.
  • Match on action: cut in the middle of a movement, such as a door opening, so the movement carries the viewer across the cut.
  • Eyeline match: if a person looks off-screen to the right, the next shot should show what they see.

[!TIP] Breaking continuity on purpose is a style — vloggers use jump cuts to remove pauses. Know the rules first, then break them deliberately.

Try it

Watch two minutes of a film or series you like. For each shot, write its size and angle. Mark one place where a cut follows the 180° rule and one match on action.

Common mistakes

  • Only filming medium shots, leaving nothing to cut to.
  • Cutting between two almost identical angles of the same person.
  • Crossing the 180° line in a conversation, confusing the viewer.

Key terms

  • Shot size — how much of the subject the frame shows.
  • Rule of thirds — a composition guide using a 3 × 3 grid.
  • 180° rule — keeping the camera on one side of the line of action.
  • Match on action — cutting during a movement to hide the edit.

Tasvir tili

Montajchilar kun boʻyi lavhalar orasidan tanlov qiladi, shuning uchun ularga nom berish kerak. Bu darsda lavhalarni nomlashni, ularning kadrga olinishini baholashni va harakatni kesishlar orqali uzluksiz saqlashni oʻrganasiz.

Lavha oʻlchami Nimani koʻrsatadi Odatdagi qoʻllanilishi
Juda umumiy plan Butun manzara yoki shahar Voqea qayerda boʻlayotganini koʻrsatish
Umumiy plan Insonni boshdan oyoq atrof-muhiti bilan Harakat va joyni koʻrsatish
Oʻrta plan Insonni beldan yuqorisi Suhbatlar, taqdimotlar
Yirik plan Yuz yoki muhim buyum Hissiyot va tafsilot
Juda yirik plan Koʻzlar, qoʻllar, mayda detal Taranglik va urgʻu
Insert (detal) Harakatning tafsiloti, masalan, tugmani bosayotgan qoʻl Nima boʻlayotganini aniqlashtirish

Klassik ketma-ketlik umumiydan yirikka qarab boradi: avval qayerda, keyin kim, soʻngra u nimani his qilmoqda.

Kamera burchagi

  • Koʻz darajasi neytral va samimiy tuyuladi.
  • Yuqori burchak (pastga qarab) obyektni kichikroq yoki himoyasiz koʻrsatadi.
  • Past burchak (yuqoriga qarab) obyektni kuchli koʻrsatadi.
  • Yelka ortidan suhbatni bir kishining nigohidan koʻrsatadi.

Kompozitsiya

  • Uchdan bir qoidasi: kadrni har ikki yoʻnalishda uchga boʻlingan deb tasavvur qiling; muhim narsalarni chiziqlar ustiga yoki ular kesishgan joyga joylang.
  • Bosh ustidagi joy: inson boshi ustida biroz joy qoldiring — juda koʻp ham emas, umuman yoʻq ham emas.
  • Harakat yoʻnalishidagi joy: inson qarayotgan yoki harakatlanayotgan tomonda joy qoldiring.
  • Yoʻnaltiruvchi chiziqlar: nigohni obyektga olib boradigan yoʻllar, relslar yoki devorlar.

Uzluksizlik: sezilmaydigan kesishlar

Uzluksizlik qoidalari tomoshabinga montajni payqamasdan makon va vaqtni tushunishga yordam beradi.

  • 180° qoidasi: suhbatlashayotgan ikki kishi orasida chiziq borligini tasavvur qiling. Kamerani uning bir tomonida saqlang; chiziqni kesib oʻtish ularni oʻrin almashtirgandek koʻrsatadi.
  • 30° qoidasi: bitta obyektning ikki lavhasi orasida kesishda burchakni kamida 30° ga oʻzgartiring (yoki lavha oʻlchamini oʻzgartiring). Aks holda kesish xatodek — jump cut kabi koʻrinadi.
  • Harakat ustida kesish: eshik ochilishi kabi harakatning oʻrtasida kesing — harakat tomoshabinni kesish orqali olib oʻtadi.
  • Nigoh mosligi: agar inson kadr tashqarisiga oʻngga qarasa, keyingi lavha u koʻrayotgan narsani koʻrsatishi kerak.

[!TIP] Uzluksizlikni ataylab buzish — bu uslub: vlogerlar pauzalarni olib tashlash uchun jump cut dan foydalanadi. Avval qoidalarni biling, soʻngra ularni ongli ravishda buzing.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Yoqtirgan film yoki serialingizdan ikki daqiqa tomosha qiling. Har bir lavhaning oʻlchami va burchagini yozib boring. 180° qoidasiga amal qilingan bitta kesishni va harakat ustidagi bitta kesishni belgilang.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Faqat oʻrta planlarni suratga olish — montaj uchun boshqa lavha qolmaydi.
  • Bitta odamning deyarli bir xil ikki burchagi orasida kesish.
  • Suhbatda 180° chizigʻini kesib oʻtib, tomoshabinni chalgʻitish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Lavha oʻlchami (plan) — kadr obyektning qancha qismini koʻrsatishi.
  • Uchdan bir qoidasi — 3 × 3 toʻrdan foydalanadigan kompozitsiya qoʻllanmasi.
  • 180° qoidasi — kamerani harakat chizigʻining bir tomonida saqlash.
  • Harakat ustida kesish — montajni yashirish uchun harakat paytida kesish.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Montajchilar uchun ovoz — Sound for editors · article · 395 words

Summary
Dialogue, music, effects and levels — the half of the film people notice only when it is wrong.
Dialog, musiqa, effektlar va daraja — film yarmi, uni odamlar faqat notoʻgʻri boʻlganda payqaydi.
Lesson text

Sound is half the film

Viewers forgive slightly imperfect pictures, but they leave quickly when the sound is bad. In this lesson you will learn the layers of a soundtrack and how to balance them.

The four layers

  1. Dialogue — the people speaking. Usually the most important layer.
  2. Music — sets mood and rhythm.
  3. Sound effects — door slams, footsteps, clicks.
  4. Ambience — the background sound of a place: traffic, birds, a room's hum (called room tone indoors).

Put each layer on its own audio track, so you can adjust them separately.

Reading levels

Editing apps show audio levels in decibels relative to full scale (dBFS). 0 dBFS is the absolute maximum.

Level What it means
Peaks reaching 0 dBFS Clipping — harsh distortion that cannot be repaired
Dialogue peaks around −12 to −6 dBFS A safe, clear level for speech
Music under speech around −20 dBFS or lower Background that does not fight the voice

[!WARNING] Clipped audio is permanently damaged. If a recording clips, turning it down later does not remove the distortion — record at a safer level next time.

Balancing the mix

  • Duck the music — lower the music whenever someone speaks, then bring it back up in the pauses.
  • Fade — add short fades at the start and end of clips to avoid clicks.
  • Fill gaps with room tone — a few seconds of the room's natural sound covers cuts, because complete silence sounds unnatural.
  • Use J-cuts and L-cuts — letting sound start early or continue late makes scenes flow.

Music and copyright

Most commercial songs are protected by copyright. Using them in your videos can get the videos muted or removed, or bring legal trouble. Use music you have a licence for: royalty-free libraries, music with a Creative Commons licence that allows your use, or music made for you.

Try it

Record yourself speaking for 30 seconds on your phone in a quiet room, then again near an open window. In any editor, compare the waveforms and levels. Add soft music under the quiet recording and lower it until the voice is clearly understandable.

Common mistakes

  • Music louder than the voice.
  • Peaks at 0 dBFS, causing distortion.
  • Abrupt cuts in sound, leaving clicks and sudden silences.

Key terms

  • dBFS — the scale for digital audio levels; 0 is the maximum.
  • Clipping — distortion caused by a level that is too high.
  • Room tone — the natural background sound of a location.
  • Ducking — lowering music automatically or by hand under speech.

Ovoz — filmning yarmi

Tomoshabinlar biroz nomukammal tasvirni kechiradi, ammo ovoz yomon boʻlsa, tezda ketib qoladi. Bu darsda saundtrek qatlamlarini va ularni qanday muvozanatlashni oʻrganasiz.

Toʻrt qatlam

  1. Dialog — gapirayotgan odamlar. Odatda eng muhim qatlam.
  2. Musiqa — kayfiyat va ritmni belgilaydi.
  3. Ovoz effektlari — eshik taqillashi, qadam tovushlari, chertishlar.
  4. Muhit ovozi — joyning fon ovozi: transport, qushlar, xonaning gʻuvillashi (yopiq joyda xona ovozi, room tone, deb ataladi).

Har bir qatlamni alohida audio trekka qoʻying, shunda ularni alohida sozlay olasiz.

Darajalarni oʻqish

Montaj dasturlari audio darajalarini toʻliq shkalaga nisbatan detsibellarda (dBFS) koʻrsatadi. 0 dBFS — mutlaq maksimum.

Daraja Nimani bildiradi
Choʻqqilar 0 dBFS ga yetadi Kliping — tuzatib boʻlmaydigan keskin buzilish
Dialog choʻqqilari taxminan −12 dan −6 dBFS gacha Nutq uchun xavfsiz, aniq daraja
Nutq ostidagi musiqa taxminan −20 dBFS yoki undan past Ovoz bilan bellashmaydigan fon

[!WARNING] Kliping boʻlgan audio butunlay buziladi. Agar yozuvda kliping boʻlsa, keyinchalik ovozni pasaytirish buzilishni yoʻqotmaydi — keyingi safar xavfsizroq darajada yozing.

Miksni muvozanatlash

  • Musiqani pasaytiring — kimdir gapirganda musiqani pasaytiring va pauzalarda qayta oshiring.
  • Silliq kirish va chiqish qiling — chertish tovushlari boʻlmasligi uchun kliplar boshi va oxiriga qisqa fade qoʻshing.
  • Boʻshliqlarni xona ovozi bilan toʻldiring — xonaning tabiiy ovozidan bir necha soniya kesishlarni yashiradi, chunki toʻliq jimjitlik gʻayritabiiy eshitiladi.
  • J-cut va L-cut dan foydalaning — ovozning ertaroq boshlanishi yoki kechroq davom etishi sahnalarni ravon qiladi.

Musiqa va mualliflik huquqi

Aksariyat tijoriy qoʻshiqlar mualliflik huquqi bilan himoyalangan. Ulardan videolaringizda foydalansangiz, videolar ovozsiz qilinishi yoki oʻchirilishi, hatto huquqiy muammo kelib chiqishi mumkin. Litsenziyasi bor musiqadan foydalaning: royaltisiz kutubxonalar, sizning foydalanishingizga ruxsat beruvchi Creative Commons litsenziyali musiqa yoki siz uchun yaratilgan musiqa.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Telefoningizda oʻzingizning 30 soniyalik nutqingizni jimjit xonada, soʻngra ochiq deraza yonida yozib oling. Istalgan dasturda toʻlqin shakllari va darajalarni solishtiring. Jimjit xonadagi yozuv ostiga yengil musiqa qoʻshing va ovoz aniq tushunarli boʻlguncha uni pasaytiring.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Musiqaning ovozdan baland boʻlishi.
  • Choʻqqilarning 0 dBFS ga yetishi va buzilish paydo boʻlishi.
  • Ovozdagi keskin kesishlar — chertishlar va toʻsatdan jimjitlik qoladi.

Asosiy atamalar

  • dBFS — raqamli audio darajalari shkalasi; 0 — maksimum.
  • Kliping — haddan tashqari yuqori daraja tufayli yuzaga keladigan buzilish.
  • Xona ovozi (room tone) — joyning tabiiy fon ovozi.
  • Ducking — nutq ostida musiqani avtomatik yoki qoʻlda pasaytirish.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Tasvir va ovoz: bilimni tekshirish — Pictures and sound: check · quiz

Summary
Five questions on shots, continuity and sound.
Lavhalar, uzluksizlik va ovoz boʻyicha beshta savol.
Test: “Tasvir va ovoz” moduli boʻyicha test
Q1

Which shot is best for showing a person's emotion?

Insonning hissiyotini koʻrsatish uchun qaysi lavha eng yaxshi?

Options
  • ✓ Close-up
  • Extreme wide
  • Wide
  • ✓ Yirik plan
  • Juda umumiy plan
  • Umumiy plan
Explanation

A close-up shows the face and its expressions.

Yirik plan yuzni va uning ifodalarini koʻrsatadi.

Q2

Two people talk. In the next shot they seem to have swapped sides. Which rule was broken?

Ikki kishi suhbatlashmoqda. Keyingi lavhada ular oʻrin almashgandek koʻrinadi. Qaysi qoida buzilgan?

Options
  • ✓ The 180° rule
  • The rule of thirds
  • The 30° rule
  • ✓ 180° qoidasi
  • Uchdan bir qoidasi
  • 30° qoidasi
Explanation

The 180° rule keeps the camera on one side of the line between them.

180° qoidasi kamerani ular orasidagi chiziqning bir tomonida saqlaydi.

Q3

Your dialogue peaks hit 0 dBFS. What happens?

Dialog choʻqqilari 0 dBFS ga yetdi. Nima sodir boʻladi?

Options
  • ✓ The audio clips and distorts
  • The audio becomes clearer
  • Nothing — 0 dBFS is ideal
  • ✓ Audio kliping boʻlib, buziladi
  • Audio aniqroq boʻladi
  • Hech narsa — 0 dBFS ideal daraja
Explanation

At 0 dBFS the audio clips and distorts permanently.

0 dBFS da audio kliping boʻladi va butunlay buziladi.

Q4

Why fill silent gaps with room tone?

Nega jimjit boʻshliqlarni xona ovozi bilan toʻldirish kerak?

Options
  • ✓ Complete silence sounds unnatural
  • It makes the file smaller
  • It hides the music
  • ✓ Toʻliq jimjitlik gʻayritabiiy eshitiladi
  • U faylni kichraytiradi
  • U musiqani yashiradi
Explanation

Complete digital silence sounds unnatural; room tone keeps the space believable.

Toʻliq raqamli jimjitlik gʻayritabiiy eshitiladi; xona ovozi muhitni ishonarli saqlaydi.

Q5

True or false: you may use any popular song in your video as long as you credit the artist.

Toʻgʻrimi yoki notoʻgʻri: ijrochini koʻrsatib qoʻysangiz, videongizda istalgan mashhur qoʻshiqdan foydalanishingiz mumkin.

Options
  • True
  • ✓ False
  • Toʻgʻri
  • ✓ Notoʻgʻri
Explanation

False — credit is not permission. You need a licence that covers your use.

Notoʻgʻri — muallifni koʻrsatish ruxsat degani emas. Sizga foydalanishingizni qamrab oladigan litsenziya kerak.

Modul: Birinchi montajingiz (Your first edit)

Module description
Edit, finish and export a short film from your own footage.
Oʻz materialingizdan qisqa filmni montaj qiling, yakunlang va eksport qiling.

Birinchi montajingiz — Your first edit · exercise · 407 words

Summary
Shoot ten short clips on your phone and edit them into a 30-second sequence.
Telefoningizda oʻnta qisqa klip suratga oling va ularni 30 soniyalik ketma-ketlikka montaj qiling.
Lesson text

From clips to a story

Time to edit. In this exercise you will turn your own phone clips into a short sequence, using the workflow from this course. Any editor works: DaVinci Resolve (free, for computers) and CapCut (free, for phones and computers) are good choices. Menu names differ between apps, so the steps describe what to do rather than exact buttons.

1. Shoot your material

Choose a simple activity with a beginning, middle and end — making tea, packing a bag, cooking a dish. Film 10–15 short clips of 5–10 seconds each:

  • a wide shot to show where you are,
  • medium shots of the main steps,
  • close-ups and inserts of hands and details,
  • a final shot that shows the result.

Hold the phone steady with both hands, film in landscape (or vertical if your final video is for a vertical platform), and keep each shot still for a few seconds before and after the action.

2. Set up the project

Create a new project with the same resolution and frame rate as your clips, for example 1920 × 1080 at 30 fps. Import the clips into a folder (bin) called Footage.

3. Assemble

Place the clips on the timeline in story order. Do not worry about timing yet — the goal is a complete rough sequence.

4. Trim and tighten

  • Cut away the start and end of each clip where nothing happens.
  • Use close-ups to cover moments that are slow in the wide shot.
  • Aim for about 30 seconds in total.

5. Add sound

Add one piece of licensed music on its own track. Lower it so it supports the pictures, and add short fades at the start and end.

[!TIP] Play the sequence without looking at the screen. If the music alone does not feel finished, check that it starts and ends cleanly.

6. Review

Watch the whole sequence twice: once for the story (is every step clear?) and once for details (jumps, sudden sound changes, black frames between clips).

Try it

Complete steps 1–6 and save your project. You will export it in the next lesson and use the same skills in the course project.

Common mistakes

  • Filming one long clip instead of separate shots.
  • Mixing vertical and horizontal clips in one project.
  • Leaving dead time at the start of every clip.

Key terms

  • Bin — a folder for media inside an editing project.
  • Assembly — the first, rough arrangement of clips.
  • Trim — shortening a clip from its start or end.
  • Coverage — the set of different shots that lets you edit a scene.

Kliplardan hikoyaga

Montaj vaqti keldi. Bu mashqda oʻz telefon kliplaringizni ushbu kursdagi ish jarayoni asosida qisqa ketma-ketlikka aylantirasiz. Istalgan dastur mos keladi: DaVinci Resolve (bepul, kompyuterlar uchun) va CapCut (bepul, telefon va kompyuterlar uchun) — yaxshi tanlov. Menyu nomlari dasturlarda farq qiladi, shuning uchun qadamlarda aniq tugmalar emas, nima qilish kerakligi tasvirlangan.

1. Materialingizni suratga oling

Boshlanishi, oʻrtasi va oxiri bor oddiy ishni tanlang — choy damlash, sumka yigʻish, taom tayyorlash. Har biri 5–10 soniyalik 10–15 ta qisqa klip suratga oling:

  • qayerda ekaningizni koʻrsatadigan umumiy plan,
  • asosiy qadamlarning oʻrta planlari,
  • qoʻllar va tafsilotlarning yirik planlari va insertlari,
  • natijani koʻrsatadigan yakuniy lavha.

Telefonni ikki qoʻlda barqaror ushlang, gorizontal holatda suratga oling (yakuniy video vertikal platforma uchun boʻlsa, vertikal holatda) va har bir lavhani harakatdan oldin va keyin bir necha soniya qimirlatmay turing.

2. Loyihani sozlang

Kliplaringiz bilan bir xil ruxsat va kadrlar chastotasiga ega yangi loyiha yarating, masalan, 1920 × 1080, 30 fps. Kliplarni Footage deb nomlangan papkaga (bin) import qiling.

3. Yigʻing

Kliplarni vaqt shkalasiga hikoya tartibida joylashtiring. Hozircha vaqtga eʼtibor bermang — maqsad toʻliq qoralama ketma-ketlik.

4. Qirqing va ixchamlang

  • Har bir klipning hech narsa sodir boʻlmaydigan boshi va oxirini qirqib tashlang.
  • Umumiy plandagi sekin lahzalarni yirik planlar bilan yoping.
  • Jami taxminan 30 soniyaga intiling.

5. Ovoz qoʻshing

Alohida trekka litsenziyasi bor bitta musiqa qoʻshing. Tasvirni qoʻllab-quvvatlaydigan darajada pasaytiring va boshi hamda oxiriga qisqa fade qoʻshing.

[!TIP] Ketma-ketlikni ekranga qaramasdan ijro eting. Agar musiqaning oʻzi tugallangandek tuyulmasa, u toza boshlanib, toza tugashini tekshiring.

6. Koʻrib chiqing

Butun ketma-ketlikni ikki marta tomosha qiling: bir marta hikoya uchun (har bir qadam tushunarlimi?) va bir marta tafsilotlar uchun (sakrashlar, ovozning keskin oʻzgarishi, kliplar orasidagi qora kadrlar).

Mashq

1–6-qadamlarni bajaring va loyihangizni saqlang. Keyingi darsda uni eksport qilasiz, xuddi shu koʻnikmalardan esa kurs loyihasida foydalanasiz.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Alohida lavhalar oʻrniga bitta uzun klip suratga olish.
  • Bitta loyihada vertikal va gorizontal kliplarni aralashtirish.
  • Har bir klip boshida harakatsiz vaqt qoldirish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Bin — montaj loyihasi ichidagi media papkasi.
  • Yigʻish (assembly) — kliplarning birinchi, qoralama joylashuvi.
  • Qirqish (trim) — klipni boshidan yoki oxiridan qisqartirish.
  • Qamrov (coverage) — sahnani montaj qilish imkonini beradigan turli lavhalar toʻplami.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Videongizni eksport qilish — Exporting your video · article · 383 words

Summary
Containers, codecs and settings that give you a good-looking file of a sensible size.
Yaxshi koʻrinadigan va hajmi oqilona boʻlgan fayl beradigan konteynerlar, kodeklar va sozlamalar.
Lesson text

Turning a project into a file

Your edit lives inside the editing app as a project. Exporting (also called rendering or delivering) creates a single video file that anyone can play. In this lesson you will choose settings that look good and upload quickly.

Container and codec

  • The container is the file type, such as .mp4 or .mov. It holds video, audio and information about them.
  • The codec is the method used to compress the video, such as H.264 or H.265 (HEVC).

For almost every online use, MP4 with H.264 is the safe choice: every phone, computer and platform can play it.

The main settings

Setting Recommendation
Resolution The same as your project, such as 1920 × 1080 or 1080 × 1920 for vertical
Frame rate The same as your project, such as 25 or 30 fps
Codec H.264 (or H.265 for smaller files on modern devices)
Bitrate / quality The app's “high” or the platform preset
Audio AAC, 48 kHz, stereo

Bitrate is how much data is used per second of video. Higher bitrate means better quality and a bigger file. Platform presets (for YouTube, Instagram and others) choose sensible values for you.

[!WARNING] Never export at a different frame rate from your project unless you know why. Converting 30 fps to 25 fps makes motion stutter.

Name and check the file

Use clear names such as tea-routine_v02_2026-11-15.mp4. After exporting, watch the whole file on the device where it will be seen. Exports sometimes reveal problems you did not notice in the editor: a missing clip, a black frame or music that is too loud on phone speakers.

Keep the project

Keep the project and the original clips until the video is finished and published. You may need to change something after feedback, and re-exporting from the project is quick.

Try it

Export the sequence from the previous lesson as an MP4 with H.264 at the project's resolution and frame rate. Note the file size, then watch it on your phone from start to finish.

Common mistakes

  • Exporting at a lower resolution than the project by accident.
  • Changing the frame rate during export.
  • Deleting the project as soon as the file is exported.

Key terms

  • Export — creating a finished video file from a project.
  • Container — the file format, such as MP4.
  • Codec — the compression method, such as H.264.
  • Bitrate — the amount of data per second of video.

Loyihani faylga aylantirish

Montajingiz montaj dasturi ichida loyiha sifatida saqlanadi. Eksport (render yoki yetkazib berish deb ham ataladi) istalgan kishi ijro eta oladigan yagona video faylni yaratadi. Bu darsda yaxshi koʻrinadigan va tez yuklanadigan sozlamalarni tanlaysiz.

Konteyner va kodek

  • Konteyner — .mp4 yoki .mov kabi fayl turi. U video, audio va ular haqidagi maʼlumotlarni oʻz ichiga oladi.
  • Kodek — videoni siqish usuli, masalan, H.264 yoki H.265 (HEVC).

Deyarli har qanday onlayn foydalanish uchun H.264 kodekli MP4 xavfsiz tanlov: uni har qanday telefon, kompyuter va platforma ijro eta oladi.

Asosiy sozlamalar

Sozlama Tavsiya
Ruxsat Loyihangiz bilan bir xil, masalan, 1920 × 1080 yoki vertikal uchun 1080 × 1920
Kadrlar chastotasi Loyihangiz bilan bir xil, masalan, 25 yoki 30 fps
Kodek H.264 (yoki zamonaviy qurilmalarda kichikroq fayl uchun H.265)
Bitreyt / sifat Dasturdagi “high” (yuqori) yoki platforma preseti
Audio AAC, 48 kHz, stereo

Bitreyt — videoning har bir soniyasi uchun ishlatiladigan maʼlumot miqdori. Bitreyt qanchalik yuqori boʻlsa, sifat shunchalik yaxshi va fayl shunchalik katta boʻladi. Platforma presetlari (YouTube, Instagram va boshqalar uchun) oqilona qiymatlarni siz uchun tanlaydi.

[!WARNING] Nima uchunligini bilmasangiz, hech qachon loyihadan farqli kadrlar chastotasida eksport qilmang. 30 fps ni 25 fps ga aylantirish harakatni “sakraydigan” qiladi.

Faylni nomlang va tekshiring

tea-routine_v02_2026-11-15.mp4 kabi aniq nomlardan foydalaning. Eksportdan keyin faylni u tomosha qilinadigan qurilmada boshidan oxirigacha koʻring. Eksport baʼzan montaj dasturida payqamagan muammolarni koʻrsatadi: yoʻqolgan klip, qora kadr yoki telefon karnayida juda baland eshitiladigan musiqa.

Loyihani saqlang

Video tugallanib, eʼlon qilinmaguncha loyihani va asl kliplarni saqlang. Fikr-mulohazadan keyin biror narsani oʻzgartirishingiz kerak boʻlishi mumkin, loyihadan qayta eksport qilish esa tez.

Oʻzingiz sinab koʻring

Oldingi darsdagi ketma-ketlikni loyiha ruxsati va kadrlar chastotasida H.264 kodekli MP4 sifatida eksport qiling. Fayl hajmini yozib oling va uni telefoningizda boshidan oxirigacha tomosha qiling.

Keng tarqalgan xatolar

  • Bilmasdan loyihadan pastroq ruxsatda eksport qilish.
  • Eksport paytida kadrlar chastotasini oʻzgartirish.
  • Fayl eksport qilinishi bilanoq loyihani oʻchirib yuborish.

Asosiy atamalar

  • Eksport — loyihadan tayyor video fayl yaratish.
  • Konteyner — MP4 kabi fayl formati.
  • Kodek — H.264 kabi siqish usuli.
  • Bitreyt — videoning har bir soniyasidagi maʼlumot miqdori.
Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:

Loyiha: “Bir kun” filmi — Project: a day-in-the-life edit · project · 167 words

Summary
Shoot and edit a 60-second film about one day or one routine.
Bir kun yoki bitta kundalik odat haqida 60 soniyalik film suratga oling va montaj qiling.
Lesson text

Your project

Make a 60-second “day in the life” film about yourself, a family member, a shop owner or a place — with permission from anyone who appears in it. Shoot it on a phone and edit it in any app.

Requirements

  1. Length: 50–70 seconds.
  2. Story: a clear beginning, middle and end — for example morning, work, evening.
  3. Coverage: at least three shot sizes (wide, medium, close-up) and at least two inserts.
  4. Continuity: cuts that respect the 180° rule and the 30° rule, unless a jump cut is clearly intentional.
  5. Sound: licensed music balanced under any speech or natural sound; no clipping; fades at the start and end.
  6. Export: MP4 (H.264) at the project's resolution and frame rate.
  7. Notes: three or four sentences on your choices and where the music comes from.

How to submit

Upload the MP4 or share a link (for example, an unlisted video), and paste your notes in the text box.

[!NOTE] A reviewer watches your film against the rubric and leaves feedback. You can re-edit and resubmit.

Loyihangiz

Oʻzingiz, oila aʼzongiz, doʻkon egasi yoki biror joy haqida 60 soniyalik “bir kun” filmini yarating — unda ishtirok etadigan har bir kishidan ruxsat oling. Uni telefonda suratga oling va istalgan dasturda montaj qiling.

Talablar

  1. Davomiyligi: 50–70 soniya.
  2. Hikoya: aniq boshlanish, oʻrta va yakun — masalan, ertalab, ish, kechqurun.
  3. Qamrov: kamida uchta lavha oʻlchami (umumiy, oʻrta, yirik) va kamida ikkita insert.
  4. Uzluksizlik: jump cut ataylab qilingani aniq boʻlmasa, kesishlar 180° va 30° qoidalariga amal qiladi.
  5. Ovoz: litsenziyasi bor musiqa nutq yoki tabiiy ovoz ostida muvozanatlangan; kliping yoʻq; boshi va oxirida fade bor.
  6. Eksport: loyiha ruxsati va kadrlar chastotasida MP4 (H.264).
  7. Izohlar: tanlovlaringiz va musiqa qayerdan olingani haqida uch-toʻrt gap.

Qanday topshiriladi

MP4 faylni yuklang yoki havola ulashing (masalan, roʻyxatda koʻrinmaydigan video) va izohlaringizni matn maydoniga joylang.

[!NOTE] Tekshiruvchi filmingizni baholash mezonlari asosida tomosha qiladi va fikr-mulohaza qoldiradi. Qayta montaj qilib, qayta topshirishingiz mumkin.

Assignment brief

Shoot and edit a 60-second film with a clear story, varied coverage, clean sound and a correct export.

Aniq hikoyasi, xilma-xil lavhalari, toza ovozi va toʻgʻri eksporti bor 60 soniyalik film suratga oling va montaj qiling.

Rubric
Criterion Points
Clear beginning, middle and end within 50–70 seconds 20
Coverage: three shot sizes and inserts used purposefully 20
Continuity and pacing of the cuts 20
Sound: balanced levels, no clipping, licensed music 20
Correct export settings and notes on choices 20
Mezon Ball
50–70 soniya ichida aniq boshlanish, oʻrta va yakun 20
Qamrov: uchta lavha oʻlchami va insertlardan maqsadli foydalanilgan 20
Kesishlarning uzluksizligi va ritmi 20
Ovoz: muvozanatli daraja, kliping yoʻq, litsenziyali musiqa 20
Toʻgʻri eksport sozlamalari va tanlovlar haqida izohlar 20

Yakuniy imtihon — Final assessment · quiz

Summary
A short exam covering the whole course. Pass mark: 70%.
Butun kursni qamrab oluvchi qisqa imtihon. Oʻtish balli: 70%.
Test: Video montaj asoslari — yakuniy imtihon
Q1

What is most of video editing about?

Video montajning katta qismi nimadan iborat?

Options
  • ✓ Choosing what to leave out
  • Adding as many effects as possible
  • Making every video longer
  • ✓ Nimani tashlab yuborishni tanlash
  • Iloji boricha koʻp effekt qoʻshish
  • Har bir videoni uzunroq qilish
Explanation

Editing is mostly choosing what to keep and what to leave out.

Montajning katta qismi — nimani qoldirish va nimani tashlab yuborishni tanlash.

Q2

Which aspect ratio is standard for vertical phone video?

Telefondagi vertikal video uchun qaysi tomonlar nisbati standart?

Options
  • ✓ 9:16
  • 16:9
  • 4:3
  • ✓ 9:16
  • 16:9
  • 4:3
Explanation

9:16 is vertical; 16:9 is landscape.

9:16 — vertikal; 16:9 — gorizontal.

Q3

The audio of the next shot starts before its picture. What is this called?

Keyingi lavhaning ovozi uning tasviridan oldin boshlanadi. Bu nima deb ataladi?

Options
  • ✓ J-cut
  • L-cut
  • Jump cut
  • ✓ J-cut
  • L-cut
  • Jump cut
Explanation

A J-cut — the audio leads the picture.

J-cut — ovoz tasvirdan oldin keladi.

Q4

Why cut in the middle of a movement, such as a door opening?

Nega eshik ochilishi kabi harakatning oʻrtasida kesish kerak?

Options
  • ✓ The movement hides the cut
  • It makes the file smaller
  • Platforms require it
  • ✓ Harakat kesishni yashiradi
  • U faylni kichraytiradi
  • Platformalar buni talab qiladi
Explanation

Match on action lets the movement carry the viewer across the cut, hiding it.

Harakat ustida kesish harakatga tomoshabinni kesish orqali olib oʻtish va uni yashirish imkonini beradi.

Q5

Which export format is the safest choice for most online platforms?

Aksariyat onlayn platformalar uchun qaysi eksport formati eng xavfsiz tanlov?

Options
  • ✓ MP4 with H.264
  • A sequence of PNG images
  • An uncompressed AVI file
  • ✓ H.264 kodekli MP4
  • PNG tasvirlar ketma-ketligi
  • Siqilmagan AVI fayl
Explanation

MP4 with H.264 plays almost everywhere.

H.264 kodekli MP4 deyarli hamma joyda ijro etiladi.

Q6

Your project is 30 fps. Which frame rate should the export use?

Loyihangiz 30 fps. Eksport qaysi kadrlar chastotasida boʻlishi kerak?

Options
  • ✓ 30 fps
  • 25 fps
  • 60 fps
  • ✓ 30 fps
  • 25 fps
  • 60 fps
Explanation

Keep the project's frame rate to avoid stuttering motion.

Harakat sakramasligi uchun loyihaning kadrlar chastotasini saqlang.

Q7

Where should background music sit when someone is speaking?

Kimdir gapirayotganda fon musiqasi qanday darajada boʻlishi kerak?

Options
  • ✓ Clearly below the voice
  • Louder than the voice
  • At 0 dBFS
  • ✓ Ovozdan aniq pastroq
  • Ovozdan balandroq
  • 0 dBFS da
Explanation

Lower the music under speech so the voice stays clear.

Ovoz aniq qolishi uchun nutq ostida musiqani pasaytiring.

Q8

Which belong in a professional editing workflow? Select all that apply.

Quyidagilardan qaysilari professional montaj jarayoniga tegishli? Mos keladigan barcha variantlarni belgilang.

Options
  • ✓ Organising footage in bins
  • ✓ A rough cut before the fine cut
  • ✓ Watching the exported file before publishing
  • Deleting the project right after export — Keep the project until the video is finished; you may need changes.
  • ✓ Materialni binlarga tartiblash
  • ✓ Yakuniy montajdan oldin qoralama montaj
  • ✓ Eʼlon qilishdan oldin eksport qilingan faylni tomosha qilish
  • Eksportdan keyin darhol loyihani oʻchirish — Video tugaguncha loyihani saqlang; oʻzgartirish kerak boʻlishi mumkin.
Explanation

Organise, assemble, cut finely, finish colour and sound, then export and check.

Tartiblash, yigʻish, nozik montaj, rang va ovozni yakunlash, soʻngra eksport qilish va tekshirish.

Tekshiruvchi uchun:
Izohlar:
